2521 ;*****************************
2522 ;*        FREQ_SWEEP         *
2523 ;*****************************
2524
2525 ;See Users' Manual for description
2526 ;RET Z SET:  if note over
2527 ;RET Z RESET: if sweep in progress or note not over
2528
2529 FREQ_SWEEP
2530
2531 ;        * if freq not swept, dec NLEN and RET [setting Z flag]
2532
2533         LD      A,(IX+FSTEP)               ;check  for no sweep code
2534         OR      A                           ;SET Z flag if FSTEP = 0
2535         JR      NZ,L20                      ;if PSW is zero note not to be swept
2536         LD      A,(IX+NLEN)                 ;dec NLEN and
2537         DEC     A                           ;SET Z flag if NLEN = 0
2538         RET     Z                           ;leave if note over with Z flag SET
2539         LD      (IX+NLEN),A                 ;store decremented NLEN
2540         RET                                 ;RET with Z flag RESET [note not over]
2541
2542 ;        ENDIF
2543
2544 ;        * sweep going, so dec FPSV
2545
2546 L20
2547         PUSH    IX                          ;point HL to FPSV
2548         POP     HL
2549         LD      DE,FPSV
2550         ADD     HL,DE
2551         CALL    LOCL_DECLSN                 ;dec FPSV
2552 ;****   JR      NZ,L21                     ;if PSW is zero FPSV has timed out
2553         RET     NZ                          ;Added uOS
2554
2555 ;        * dec NLEN and leave if sweep is over
2556
2557         CALL    LOCL_MSNTOLSN               ;reload FPSV from FPS
2558         DEC     HL                          ;point to NLEN (# steps in the sweep)
2559         LD      A,(HL)                      ;dec NLEN and
2560         DEC     A                           ;SET Z flag if NLEN = 0
2561         RET     Z                           ;leave if sweep over with Z flag SET
2562
2563 ;        * sweep not over, so add FSTEP to FREQ
2564
2565         LD      (HL),A                      ;store decremented NLEN
2566         DEC     HL                          ;point HL
2567         DEC     HL                          ;to FREQ
2568         LD      A,(IX+FSTEP)                ;A = FSTEP (two's complement step size)
2569         CALL    LOC_ADD816                  ;FREQ = FREQ + FSTEP
2570         INC     HL                          ;point HL to hi FREQ
2571         RES     2,(HL)                      ;RESET B2 in hi FREQ in case add caused > 10 bit FREQ
2572         OR      0FFH                        ;RESET Z flag. sweep not over yet
2573
2574 ;        ENDIF
2575 ;*** L21
2576         RET
2577 ;        END FREQSWE

2579
2580 ;*****************************
2581 ;*        ATN_SWEEP          *
2582 ;*****************************
2583
2584 ;See User's Manual for description
2585 ;RET Z SET:  if byte 8 is 0 [means sweep is over, or note was never swept]
2586 ;RET Z RESET: if sweep in progress
2587
2588 ATN_SWEEP
2589
2590 ;        * RET with Z SET if byte 8 = 00
2591
2592         LD      A,(IX+8)                   ;check byte 8 for no sweep code
2593         OR      A                           ;Z is set if byte 8 = 0
2594         RET     Z                           ;leave if Z set, sweep not going
2595
2596 ;        * sweep going, so dec APSV
2597
2598         PUSH    IX                          ;point HL to APSV
2599         POP     HL
2600         LD      DE,APSV
2601         ADD     HL,DE
2602         CALL    LOCL_DECLSN                 ;dec APSV (LSN of byte 9)
2603 ;****   JR      NZ,L22                     ;if PSW is zero APSV has timed out
2604         RET     NZ                          ;Added uOS
2605
2606 ;        * dec ALEN to see if sweep over
2607
2608         CALL    LOCL_MSNTOLSN               ;reload APSV from APS
2609         DEC     HL                          ;point to ALEN (# of steps in the sweep)
2610         CALL    LOCL_DECLSN                 ;dec ALEN (LSN byte 8)
2611         JR      Z,L23                       ;if PSW is non-zero sweep not over yet
2612
2613 ;        * add ASTEP to ATN
2614
2615         LD      A,(HL)                      ;MSN A = ASTEP
2616         AND     0F0H                        ;mask LSN
2617         LD      E,A                         ;E = ASTEP   0
2618         DEC     HL                          ;point HL to ATN
2619         DEC     HL
2620         DEC     HL
2621         DEC     HL
2622         LD      A,(HL)                      ;MSN A = ATN
2623         AND     0F0H                        ;A = ATN     0
2624         ADD     A,E                         ;MSN A = (ASTEP + ATN)   0
2625         LD      E,A                         ;E = (ASTEP + ATN)       0
2626         LD      A,(HL)                      ;A = ATN freq or CTRL
2627         AND     0FH                         ;mask old ATN A = 0 freq or CTRL
2628         OR      E                           ;OR in new ATN
2629         LD      (HL),A                      ;store updated value back into song data area
2630         OR      0FFH                        ;RESET Z flag. sweep not over yet
2631 ;****   JR      L22
2632         RET                                 ;Added uOS
2633
2634 ;        ELSE   Z flag is SET: sweep over
2635
2636 L23
2637         LD      (HL),0                      ;set byte 8 to 0 to indicate end sweep
2638
2639 ;        ENDIF
2640 ;        ENDIF
2641
2642 ;*** L22
2643         RET
2644
2645 ;        END ATNSWEE
2646

2648
2649 ;*****************************
2650 ;*        UPAINCTRL          *
2651 ;*****************************
2652
2653 ;Perform single byte update of the snd chip noise control register or any
2654 ;attenuation register. IX is passed pointing to byte 0 of a song data area, MSN
2655 ;register C = formatted channel attenuation code.
2656
2657 UPAINCTRL
2658         LD      A,(IX+4)                   ;MSN A = ATN. LSN may be CTRL data
2659         BIT     4,C                         ;test for ATN
2660                                             ;If PSW is non-zero ATN is to be sent,
2661                                             ; move it to the LSN
2662         JR      Z,L24
2663         RRCA                                ;swap nibbles
2664         RRCA
2665         RRCA
2666         RRCA                                ;LSN A = ATN
2667
2668 ;        ENDIF
2669
2670 L24
2671         AND     0FH                         ;mask MSN
2672         OR      C                           ;A = formatted register#  ATN or CTRL
2673 ;****   CALL    OUT_TO_SOUND_PORT           ;output ATN or CTRL data
2674 ;****   RET
2675         JP      OUT_TO_SOUND_PORT           ;output ATN or CTRL data
2676

2678
2679 ;*****************************
2680 ;*        UPFREQ             *
2681 ;*****************************
2682
2683 ;Perform double byte update of a sound chip frequency register.  IX is passed
2684 ;pointing to byte0 of a song data area. MSN register D = formatted channel
2685 ;frequency code.
2686
2687 UPFREQ
2688         LD      A,(IX+FREQ)                 ;A = F2 F3 F4 F5 F6 F7 F8 F9
2689         AND     0FH                         ;A = 0  0  0  0  F6 F7 F8 F9
2690         OR      D                           ;A = formatted reg#  F6 F7 F8 F9
2691         CALL    OUT_TO_SOUND_PORT           ;output first freq byte
2692         LD      A,(IX+FREQ)                 ;A = F2 F3 F4 F5 F6 F7 F8 F9 again
2693         AND     0F0H                        ;A = F2 F3 F4 F5 0  0  0  0
2694         LD      D,A                         ;save in D
2695         LD      A,(IX+FREQ+1)               ;LSN A = 0  0  F0 F1
2696         AND     0FH                         ;A = 0  0  0  0  0  0  F0 F1
2697         OR      D                           ;A = F2 F3 F4 F5 0  0  F0 F1
2698         RRCA                                ;swap nibbles
2699         RRCA
2700         RRCA
2701         RRCA                                ;A = 0  0  F0 F1 F2 F3 F4 F5
2702 ;****   CALL    OUT_TO_SOUND_PORT           ;output 2nd (most significant) freq byte
2703 ;****   RET
2704         JP      OUT_TO_SOUND_PORT           ;output 2nd (most significant) freq byte
2705

2707
2708 ;*****************************
2709 ;*        LOCL_DECLSN        *
2710 ;*****************************
2711
2712 ;Without affecting the MSN, decrement the LSN of the byte pointed to by HL.
2713 ;HL remains the same.
2714 ;RET with Z flag set if dec LSN results in 0, reset otherwise.
2715 ;RET with C flag set if dec LSN results in -1, reset otherwise.
2716
2717 __DECLSN:
2718 LOCL_DECLSN
2719         XOR     A
2720         RRD                                 ;A = 0    LSN (HL)
2721         SUB     1                           ;Z flag set if dec to 0. C flag if dec to -1
2722         PUSH    AF                          ;save Z and C flags
2723         RLD                                 ;(HL) = old MSN    new LSN
2724         POP     AF                          ;restore Z and C flags. A = 0    new LSN
2725         RET
2726

2728
2729 ;*****************************
2730 ;*        LOCL_DECMSN        *
2731 ;*****************************
2732
2733 ;Without affecting the LSN, decrement the MSN of the byte pointed to by HL.
2734 ;HL remains the same.
2735 ;RET with Z flag set if dec MSN results in 0, reset otherwise.
2736 ;RET with C flag set if dec MSN results in -1, reset otherwise.
2737
2738 __DECMSN
2739 LOCL_DECMSN
2740         XOR     A
2741         RLD                                 ;A = 0    MSN (HL)
2742         SUB     1                           ;Z flag set if dec to 0, C flag set if dec -1
2743         PUSH    AF                          ;save Z and C flags
2744         RRD                                 ;(HL) = new MSN    old LSN
2745         POP     AF                          ;restore Z and C flags, A = 0    new MSN
2746         RET
2747

2749
2750 ;*****************************
2751 ;*        LOCL_MSNTOLSN      *
2752 ;*****************************
2753
2754 ;Copy MSN of the byte pointed to by HL to the LSN of that byte.  HL remains
2755 ;the same.
2756
2757 __MSNTOLSN
2758 LOCL_MSNTOLSN
2759         LD      A,(HL)                      ;A = MSN   LSN to be changed
2760         AND     0F0H                        ;A = MSN   0
2761         LD      B,A                         ;save in B
2762         RRCA                                ;swap nibbles
2763         RRCA
2764         RRCA
2765         RRCA                                ;A = 0     MSN
2766         OR      B                           ;A = MSN   MSN
2767         LD      (HL),A                      ;(HL) = MSN   MSN
2768         RET
2769

2771
2772 ;*****************************
2773 ;*        LOC_ADD816         *
2774 ;*****************************
2775
2776 ;Adds 8 bit two's complement signed value passed in A to the 16 bit location
2777 ;pointed to by HL.
2778
2779 __ADD816
2780 LOC_ADD816
2781         LD      B,0                         ;set B for positive value in A
2782         BIT     7,A                         ;if A is positive
2783         JR      Z,POS                       ;skip
2784 ;****   LD      B,0FFH                      ;A is neg: extend sign bit thru B
2785         DEC     B                           ;Added uOS
2786 POS
2787         ADD     A,(HL)                      ;do 8 bit add [and set Carry]
2788         LD      (HL),A                      ;store result into LSB 16 bit number
2789         INC     HL                          ;put MSB
2790         LD      A,(HL)                      ;into A
2791         ADC     A,B                         ;A = MSB + Carry + B [B is 0 or FF]
2792         LD      (HL),A                      ;store result into MSB
2793         DEC     HL                          ;re-point HL to LSB 16 bit number
2794         RET
2795

2797
2798 ;*****************************
2799 ;*        PT_IX_TO_SxDATA    *
2800 ;*****************************
2801
2802 ;SONGNO passed in B.
2803 ;Point IX to byte 0 in SONGNO's song data area.
2804 ;RET with both DE and IX pointing to SxDATA,
2805 ;HL pointing to MSB SxDATA entry in LST_OF_SND_ADDRS.
2806
2807 PT_IX_TO_SxDATA
2808
2809 ;        * IX & DE := addr of byte 0 in SONGNO's song  data area.
2810 ;          HL pointing to MSB SxDATA entry in LST_OF_SND_ADDRS.
2811
2812         LD      HL,(PTR_TO_LST_OF_SND_ADDRS) ;point HL to start LST_OF_SND_ADDRS
2813         DEC     HL                          ;init HL for addition
2814         DEC     HL
2815         LD      C,B                         ;form 4 * SONGNO in C
2816         LD      B,0
2817         RLC     C
2818         RLC     C
2819         ADD     HL,BC                       ;HL pts to SxDATA's entry in LST_OF_SND_ADDRS
2820         LD      E,(HL)                      ;move addr SxDATA to IX thru DE
2821         INC     HL
2822         LD      D,(HL)
2823         PUSH    DE
2824         POP     IX
2825         RET
2826

2828
2829 ;*****************************
2830 ;*        AREA_SONG_IS       *
2831 ;*****************************
2832
2833 ;The address of byte 0 of a song data area is passed in IX.  The song number of
2834 ;the song using that area is returned in A (0FFH if inactive).  If a special
2835 ;effect was using that area, 62 is returned in A and HL is returned with the
2836 ;address of the special sound effect routine.
2837
2838 AREA_SONG_IS
2839         LD      A,(IX+0)                   ;A := CH#   SONGNO or 62, or A := FF
2840         CP      0FFH
2841         RET     Z                           ;leave with A = FF if area inactive
2842         AND     00111111B                   ;mask CH#
2843         CP      62
2844         RET     NZ                          ;leave with A = SONGNO if not a special effect (62)
2845
2846 ;        special effect. so set HL to addr effect, stored in bytes 1&2
2847
2848         PUSH    IX                          ;point HL to byte 1
2849         POP     HL
2850         INC     HL
2851         LD      E,(HL)                      ;save LSB effect addr in E
2852         INC     HL                          ;HL to byte 2
2853         LD      D,(HL)                      ;save MSB effect addr in D
2854         EX      DE,HL                       ;HL := addr special effect
2855         RET
2856
2857 ;        END UTIL
2858

2860
2861 ;*****************************
2862 ;*        INIT_SOUND         *
2863 ;*****************************
2864
2865 ;See Users' Manual for description; includes ENTRY POINT ALL_OFF
2866 ;addr LST_OF_SND_ADDRS passed in HL
2867 ;n = # of song data areas to init, passed in B
2868
2869 ;        *** Sound chip register code EQUATES ***
2870
2871 ;        Tone generator frequency and attenuation formatted register codes
2872
2873 SR1FRQ          EQU     10000000B           ;BIT7 = 1, BIT6-4 = TONE GEN 1 FREQ CODE
2874 SR1ATN          EQU     10010000B           ;BIT7 = 1, BIT6-4 = TONE GEN 1 ATN CODE
2875 SR2FRQ          EQU     10100000B           ;BIT7 = 1, BIT6-4 = TONE GEN 2 FREQ CODE
2876 SR2ATN          EQU     10110000B           ;BIT7 = 1, BIT6-4 = TONE GEN 2 ATN CODE
2877 SR3FRQ          EQU     11000000B           ;BIT7 = 1, BIT6-4 = TONE GEN 3 FREQ CODE
2878 SR3ATN          EQU     11010000B           ;BIT7 = 1, BIT6-4 = TONE GEN 1 ATN CODE
2879
2880 ;        Noise generator control and attenuation formatted register codes
2881
2882 SRNCTL          EQU     11100000B           ;BIT7 = 1, BIT6-4 = NOISE GEN CONTROL CODE
2883 SRNATN          EQU     11110000B           ;BIT7 = 1, BIT6-4 = NOISE GEN ATN CODE
2884
2885 __SOUND_INIT:
2886 ;*** INIT_SOUND
2887
2888 ;        * initialize PTR_TO_LST_OF_SND_ADDRS with value passed in HL
2889
2890         LD      (PTR_TO_LST_OF_SND_ADDRS),HL
2891
2892 ;        * store inactive code at byte 0 of each of the n data areas (B = n)
2893
2894         INC     HL                          ;pt HL to song 1 data area entry in LST_OF_SND_ADDRS
2895         INC     HL
2896         LD      E,(HL)                      ;pt DE to byte 0 in first song data area
2897         INC     HL
2898         LD      D,(HL)
2899         EX      DE,HL                       ;pt HL to byte 0 in first song data area
2900         LD      DE,10                       ;set DE for 10 byte increment
2901         LD      A,0FFH                      ;Added uOS
2902 B1:
2903         LD      (HL),A                      ;deactivate area  *** Added uOS
2904         ADD     HL,DE                       ;pt HL to byte 0 next area (10 bytes away)
2905         DJNZ    B1                          ;do this for the n (passed in B) data areas
2906
2907 ;        * store end of data area code (0) at first byte after last song data area
2908
2909         LD      (HL),0                      ;store end of data area code in byte 0 data area n + 1
2910
2911 ;        * set the 4 channel data area pointers to a dummy, inactive data area
2912
2913         LD      HL,DUMAREA                  ;point HL to inactive byte below [after the RET]
2914         LD      (PTR_TO_S_ON_0),HL          ;store addr DUMAREA at PTR_TO_S_ON_0
2915         LD      (PTR_TO_S_ON_1),HL          ;store addr DUMAREA at PTR_TO_S_ON_1
2916         LD      (PTR_TO_S_ON_2),HL          ;store addr DUMAREA at PTR_TO_S_ON_2
2917         LD      (PTR_TO_S_ON_3),HL          ;store addr DUMAREA at PTR_TO_S_ON_3
2918
2919 ;        * initialize SAVE_CTRL
2920
2921 ;****   LD      A,0FFH                      ;note: this is only time MSN SAVE_CTRL will be non zero,
2922 ;****                                      ;Out uOS (loaded earlier)
2923
2924         LD      (SAVE_CTRL),A               ;thus ensuring PLAY_SONGS will output 1st real CTRL data
2925 __TURN_OFF_SOUND:
2926 ;*** ALL_OFF
2927
2928 ;        * turn off all 4 sound generators
2929
2930         LD      A,(SOUNDPORT)               ;Added uOS
2931         LD      C,A                         ;Added uOS
2932         LD      A,OFF+SR1ATN                ;form off code for tone generator 1
2933         OUT     (C),A                       ;send it out
2934         LD      A,OFF+SR2ATN                ;form off code for tone generator 2
2935         OUT     (C),A                       ;send it out
2936         LD      A,OFF+SR3ATN                ;form off code for tone generator 3
2937         OUT     (C),A                       ;send it out
2938         LD      A,OFF+SRNATN                ;form off code for noise generator, N
2939         OUT     (C),A                       ;send it out
2940         RET
2941
2942 DUMAREA         DEFB    INACTIVE
2943
2944 ;        END INITSOU
2945

2947
2948 ;*****************************
2949 ;*        JUKE_BOX           *
2950 ;*****************************
2951
2952 ;See Users' Manual for description
2953 ;SONGNO passed in B
2954
2955 __PLAY_IT
2956 JUKE_BOX
2957
2958 ;        * RET if song already in progress
2959
2960         PUSH    BC                          ;save SONGNO on stack
2961         CALL    PT_IX_TO_SxDATA             ;point IX to SONGNO's song data area
2962         LD      A,(IX+0)                    ;A := CH# [if any]   SONGNO [if any]
2963         AND     3FH                         ;A := 0 0 SONGNO
2964         POP     BC                          ;B := SONGNO
2965         CP      B                           ;test if already in progress
2966         RET     Z                           ;if so, leave
2967
2968 ;        * load first note and set NEXT_NOTE_PTR [thru LOAD_NEXT_NOTE]
2969
2970         LD      (IX+0),B                    ;store SONGNO in byte 0
2971         DEC     HL                          ;HL left by PT_IX_TO_SxDATA pointing to MSB SxDATA
2972         DEC     HL                          ;entry in LST_OF_SND_ADDRS point HL to note list
2973         LD      D,(HL)                      ;starting addr entry in LST_OF_SND_ADDRS and save this
2974         DEC     HL                          ;addr in DE
2975         LD      E,(HL)                      ;DE now has the initial value for NEXT_NOTE_PTR
2976         LD      (IX+1),E                    ;set NEXT_NOTE_PTR for first note in song
2977         LD      (IX+2),D
2978         CALL    LOAD_NEXT_NOTE              ;load note. byte 0 := CH# SONGNO, set new NEXT_NOTE_PTR
2979         JR      UP_CH_DATA_PTRS             ;new song. so update channel data ptrs
2980
2981 ;        END JUKEBOX
2982

2984
2985 ;*****************************
2986 ;*        PLAY_SONGS_        *
2987 ;*****************************
2988
2989 ;        *** Sound chip register code EQUATES
2990
2991 ;*** WHITE      EQU     00000100B           ;BIT2 = 1, white noise code
2992 ;*** PERIOD     EQU     00000000B           ;BIT2 = 0, periodic noise code
2993 ;*** NSRH1      EQU     00000000B           ;BIT0-1 Set for highest noise shift rate (N/512)
2994 ;*** NSRMED     EQU     00000001B           ;BIT0-1 Set for medium noise shift rate (N/1024)
2995 ;*** NSRLOW     EQU     00000010B           ;BIT0-1 Set for lowest noise shift rate (N/2048)
2996 ;*** NSRTG3     EQU     00000011B           ;BIT0-1 Set for shift from tone gen 3 output
2997
2998 SOUNDS
2999 ;*** PLAY_SONGS_
3000
3001 ;        * output CH1 attenuation and frequency
3002
3003         LD      A,OFF+SR1ATN               ;format CH1 OFF byte into A
3004         LD      C,SR1ATN                   ;format MSN C for CH1 attenuation
3005         LD      D,SR1FRQ                   ;format MSN D for CH1 frequency
3006         LD      IX,(PTR_TO_S_ON_1)          ;point IX to byte 0 data area of song for CH1
3007         CALL    TONE_OUT
3008
3009 ;        * output CH2 attenuation and frequency
3010
3011         LD      A,OFF+SR2ATN               ;format CH2 OFF byte into A
3012         LD      C,SR2ATN                   ;format MSN C for CH2 attenuation
3013         LD      D,SR2FRQ                   ;format MSN D for CH2 frequency
3014         LD      IX,(PTR_TO_S_ON_2)          ;point IX to byte 0 data area of song for CH2
3015         CALL    TONE_OUT
3016
3017 ;        * output CH3 attenuation and frequency
3018
3019         LD      A,OFF+SR3ATN               ;format CH3 OFF byte into A
3020         LD      C,SR3ATN                   ;format MSN C for CH3 attenuation
3021         LD      D,SR3FRQ                   ;format MSN D for CH3 frequency
3022         LD      IX,(PTR_TO_S_ON_3)          ;point IX to byte 0 data area of song for CH3
3023         CALL    TONE_OUT
3024
3025 ;        * output CH0 [noise] ATN [and CTRL, if different from last time]
3026
3027         LD      A,OFF+SRNATN               ;format CH0 OFF byte into A
3028         LD      C,SRNATN                   ;format MSN C for CH0 attenuation
3029         LD      IX,(PTR_TO_S_ON_0)          ;point IX to byte 0 data area of song for CH0
3030         LD      E,(IX+0)                   ;look for inactive code, OFFH
3031         INC     E                           ;this sets Z flag if E = OFFH
3032         JR      NZ,L5                       ;if PSW is zero song data area is inactive
3033         CALL    OUT_TO_SOUND_PORT           ; turn off CH0
3034         JR      L6                          ;SND_MANAGER
3035
3036 ;        ELSE
3037
3038 L5
3039         CALL    UPAINCTRL                  ;send out current ATN
3040         LD      A,(IX+CTRL)                ;LSN A = current CTRL data
3041         AND     0FH                         ;mask MSN
3042         LD      HL,SAVE_CTRL                ;point to last CTRL data sent
3043         CP      (HL)                        ;compare
3044         JR      Z,L6                        ;if PSW is zero CTRL has not changed (done)
3045         LD      (HL),A                      ;SAVE_CTRL = new CTRL data
3046         LD      C,SRNCTL                    ;send new CTRL data
3047         CALL    UPAINCTRL
3048         JR      L6                          ;SND_MANAGER
3049
3050 ;        ENDIF
3051 ;        ENDIF
3052
3053 TONE_OUT
3054         LD      E,(IX+0)                    ;look for inactive code, OFFH
3055         INC     E                           ;this sets Z flag if E = OFFH
3056         JR      NZ,L7                       ;if PSW is zero song data area is inactive
3057 ;****   CALL    OUT_TO_SOUND_PORT           ;turn off CHx
3058 ;****   JR      L8
3059         JP      OUT_TO_SOUND_PORT           ;turn off CHx
3060
3061 ;        ELSE send out current ATN and FREQ
3062
3063 L7
3064         CALL    UPAINCTRL                  ;send out attenuation
3065         JP      UPFREQ                      ;send out frequency
3066 ;****   CALL    UPFREQ                      ;send out frequency
3067 L8
3068 ;****   RET
3069 ;        ENDIF

3071 ;*****************************
3072 ;*        SND_MANAGER        *
3073 ;*****************************
3074
3075 ;See Users' Manual for description
3076
3077 L6
3078 SND_MANAGER
3079
3080 ;        * IX := addr of song #1 data area [S1DATA]
3081
3082         LD      B,1                         ;pt IX to byte 0 song data area for song # 1
3083         CALL    PT_IX_TO_SxDATA
3084
3085 L1                                           ;LOOP until end of song data areas
3086         LD      A,ENOSDATA                  ;check for end of song data areas
3087         CP      (IX+0)                      ;set Z flag if inactive
3088         RET     Z                           ;leave [Z set], if all data areas have been processed
3089
3090 ;        * process active song data areas
3091
3092         CALL    PROCESS_DATA_AREA           ;update counters or call effect get next note
3093
3094 ;        * point IX to byte 0 next song data area
3095
3096         LD      DE,10
3097         ADD     IX,DE
3098         JR      L1                          ;REPEAT LOOP
3099

3101
3102 ;*****************************
3103 ;*        UP_CH_DATA_PTRS     *
3104 ;*****************************
3105
3106 ;For each active data area, starting with S1DATA and proceeding in order, load
3107 ;the associated channel data area pointer [PTR_TO_S_ON_x] with the address of
3108 ;byte 0. This routine is called by JUKE_BOX, when a song starts and
3109 ;PROCESS_DATA_AREA when the channel using a data area has changed as a result of
3110 ;calling LOAD_NEXT_NOTE [this happens when a song finishes and when it switches
3111 ;back and forth between noise and tone notes].
3112
3113 UP_CH_DATA_PTRS
3114         PUSH    IX                          ;save curent IX
3115         LD      HL,DUMAREA                  ;set all 4 ch data ptrs to dummy inactive area
3116         LD      (PTR_TO_S_ON_0),HL
3117         LD      (PTR_TO_S_ON_1),HL
3118         LD      (PTR_TO_S_ON_2),HL
3119         LD      (PTR_TO_S_ON_3),HL
3120         LD      B,1                         ;set IX to byte 0 S1DATA
3121         CALL    PT_IX_TO_SxDATA             ;RETs with IX addr byte 0 song 1
3122
3123 L2                                           ;LOOP until end of song data areas
3124         LD      A,(IX+0)                    ;test for end of song data areas
3125         OR      A                           ;**** CP      ENOSDATA
3126         JR      Z,DONE_SNDMAN               ;leave loop if all data areas checked
3127
3128
3129
3130         INC     A                           ;**** CP      INACTIVE     ;check for inactive data area: don't update ptr if so
3131         JR      Z,L9                        ;if PSW is non-zero area is active: update channel data ptrs
3132         LD      A,(IX+0)                    ;get CH# in A
3133         AND     0C0H                        ;B7 - B6 in A = CH#
3134         RLCA                                ;form CH# * 2 in A, i.e., the offset from
3135         RLCA                                ;PTR_TO_S_ON_0 of the channel data area pointer
3136         RLCA                                ;that points to channel CH#
3137         LD      E,A                         ;add offset to addr of PTR_TO_S_ON_0
3138         LD      D,0
3139         LD      HL,PTR_TO_S_ON_0
3140         ADD     HL,DE                       ;HL points to proper channel data area pointer
3141         PUSH    IX                          ;store this song data area's byte 0 addr there
3142         POP     DE
3143         LD      (HL),E
3144         INC     HL
3145         LD      (HL),D
3146
3147 ;        ENDIF
3148
3149 ;        * point IX to byte 0 next song data area
3150
3151 L9
3152         LD      DE,10
3153         ADD     IX,DE
3154         JR      L2                          ;REPEAT LOOP
3155
3156 DONE_SNDMAN
3157         POP     IX                          ;restore IX
3158 ;*** L12
3159         RET
3160

3162
3163 ;*****************************
3164 ;*        LEAVE_EFFECT       *
3165 ;*****************************
3166
3167 ;LEAVE_EFFECT, called by a special sound effect routine when it's finished,
3168 ;restores the SONGNO of the song to which the effect note belongs to B5 - B0 of
3169 ;byte 0 in the effect's data area, and loads bytes 1 and 2 with the address of
3170 ;the next note in the song.  The address of the 1 byte SONGNO (saved by the
3171 ;effect when first called) is passed in DE.  The 2 byte address of the next note
3172 ;in the song, also saved by the effect, is passed in HL.  IX is assumed to be
3173 ;pointing to byte 0 of the data area to which the song number is to be restored.
3174 ;Bits 7 and 6 of the saved SONGNO are ignored, and therefore may be used by the
3175 ;effect to store flag information during the course of the note.
3176
3177 __EFFECT_OVER
3178 ;*** LEAVE_EFFECT
3179         LD      (IX+1),L                    ;LSB NEXT_NOTE_PTR := LSB addr next note in song
3180         LD      (IX+2),H                    ;MSB NEXT_NOTE_PTR := MSB addr next note in song
3181         LD      A,(DE)                      ;A := x x SONGNO (i.e., the saved, original SONGNO)
3182         AND     03FH                        ;A := 0 0 SONGNO
3183         LD      B,A                         ;B := 0 0 SONGNO (B now = original SONGNO)
3184         LD      A,(IX+0)                    ;A := CH#   62 (all effect notes have SONGNO = 62)
3185         AND     0C0H                        ;A := CH# 0 0 0 0 0 0
3186         OR      B                           ;A := CH# SONGNO
3187         LD      (IX+0),A                    ;restore the song number
3188         JR      EFXOVER
3189

3191
3192 ;*****************************
3193 ;*        PROCESS_DATA_AREA  *
3194 ;*****************************
3195
3196 ;See Users' Manual for description
3197 ;Terminology: SFX = address of sound effect routine
3198
3199 PROCESS_DATA_AREA
3200         CALL    AREA_SONG_IS                ;return area's SONGNO in A [and addr SFX in HL]
3201         CP      INACTIVE                    ;test for inactive code
3202         RET     Z                           ;RET, no processing if area inactive
3203
3204 ;        * if special effect, call it to process the data area
3205
3206         CP      62                          ;test for special sound effect
3207         JR      NZ,L10                      ;if PSW is zero data area used by sound effect
3208         LD      DE,7                        ;pt HL to SFX+7, starting adr of the effect's code
3209         ADD     HL,DE
3210         JP      (HL)                        ;do 1 pass thru effect, RET from effect
3211
3212 ;        ENDIF
3213
3214 ;        * else process a non-effect note
3215
3216 L10
3217         CALL    ATN_SWEEP                   ;process atn sweep data, if any
3218         CALL    FREQ_SWEEP                  ;proc frq sweep data, if any, & note dura timer s
3219 ;****   JR      NZ,L12                     ;if PSW is zero note is over
3220         RET     NZ
3221 EFXOVER
3222         LD      A,(IX+0)                    ;A := CH#   SONGNO this note
3223         PUSH    AF                          ;save on stack
3224         CALL    LOAD_NEXT_NOTE              ;load data for next note
3225         POP     BC                          ;B := CH#   SONGNO previous note
3226         LD      A,(IX+0)                    ;A := CH#   SONGNO new note [may be inactive]
3227         CP      B                           ;check against new note's CH#   SONGNO
3228 ;****   JR      Z,L12                      ;if PSW is non-zero change to/from tone/efx/noise
3229         RET     Z
3230         JR      UP_CH_DATA_PTRS             ;to maintain data area priority system
3231
3232 ;        ENDIF
3233 ;        END SNDMAN

3236
3237 ;*****************************
3238 ;*        LOAD_NEXT_NOTE     *
3239 ;*****************************
3240
3241 ;see Users' Manual for description
3242 ;SFX refers to the beginning address of a special sound effect routine
3243
3244 LOAD_NEXT_NOTE
3245
3246 ;        * deactivate area, save SONGNO on stack
3247
3248         LD      A,(IX+0)                    ;A := byte 0
3249         AND     00111111B                   ;mask CH#, if any
3250         PUSH    AF                          ;save SONGNO on stack
3251         LD      (IX+0),INACTIVE             ;deactivate area
3252
3253 ;        * A := header new note
3254
3255         LD      L,(IX+1)                    ;HL := addr new note in ROM
3256         LD      H,(IX+2)
3257         LD      A,(HL)                      ;A := header new note
3258
3259 ;        * save header of new note in song on stack and load its data CASE note type
3260
3261         LD      B,A                         ;save header new note in B
3262         BIT     5,A                         ;test for rest
3263         JR      Z,L13                       ;if PSW is non-zero note is a rest
3264
3265 ;*** REST
3266         PUSH    BC                          ;save header on stack
3267         AND     00011111B                   ;mask all but duration bits
3268
3269 ;        * set up NEXT_NOTE_PTR
3270
3271         INC     HL                          ;HL = address of the header of the note after this note
3272         LD      (IX+1),L                    ;store in NEXT_NOTE_PTR
3273         LD      (IX+2),H
3274
3275 ;        * move this note's data and fill in bytes where necessary
3276
3277         LD      (IX+ATN),0FH                ;set atn off
3278         LD      (IX+NLEN),A                 ;NLEN := 5 bit duration
3279         LD      (IX+FSTEP),0                ;indicate freq not to be swept
3280         LD      (IX+ASTEP),0                ;indicate atn not to be swept
3281         JP      MODBO
3282
3283 ;        ENDIF
3284
3285 L13
3286         BIT     4,A                         ;test for end
3287         JR      Z,L14                       ;if PSW is non-zero end of song
3288         BIT     3,A                         ;test for repeat
3289         JR      Z,ENDNOREP                  ;if PSW is non-zero repeat song
3290 ;*** ENDREP
3291         POP     BC                          ;B := SONGNO
3292         JP      JUKE_BOX                    ;to reload 1st note of this song
3293
3294         RET                                 ;to PROCESS_DATA_AREA, don't save header
3295 ;        ENDIF
3296
3297 ENDNOREP
3298         LD      A,INACTIVE
3299         PUSH    AF                          ;save inactive code to end song
3300         JP      MODBO                       ;to load byte 0
3301
3302 ;        ENDIF
3303 ;        - test for special sound effect
3304
3305 L14
3306         AND     00111100B                   ;mask irrelevant bits
3307         CP      00000100B                   ;test for B5 - B2 = 0001
3308         JR      NZ,L15                      ;if PSW is zero note is a special effect
3309 ;*** EFFECT
3310         POP     IY                          ;IY := SONGNO
3311         PUSH    IY                          ;put SONGNO back on stack
3312         PUSH    BC                          ;save header on stack NEXT_NOTE_PTR := SFX, DE := SFX
3313         INC     HL                          ;pt HL to next byte [LSB addr SFX]
3314         LD      E,(HL)                      ;E := LSB SFX
3315         LD      (IX+1),E                    ;put LSB of SFX in byte 1 of SxDATA [NEXT_NOTE_PTR]
3316         INC     HL                          ;pt HL to MSB SFX
3317         LD      D,(HL)                      ;D := MSB SFX
3318         LD      (IX+2),D                    ;put MSB SFX in byte 2 of SxDATA
3319         INC     HL                          ;point HL to next note [after this new note]
3320         PUSH    IY                          ;A := SONGNO
3321         POP     AF
3322         PUSH    DE                          ;IY := SFX
3323         POP     IY
3324         LD      DE,PASS1                    ;create "CALL (IY)" with RET to PASS1 by storing
3325         PUSH    DE                          ;PASS1 on the stack
3326         JP      (IY)                        ;1st 7 bytes SFX will save addr next note & SONGNO
3327 PASS1
3328         LD      DE,7                        ;in same fashion, create a "CALL (IY+7)"
3329         ADD     IY,DE                       ;to allow SFX to load initial values
3330         LD      DE,MODBO                    ;RET to MODBO
3331         PUSH    DE
3332         JP      (IY)
3333
3334 ;        ENDIF
3335
3336 ;        - if here, note is type 0 - 3
3337
3338 L15
3339         PUSH    BC                          ;save header on stack
3340         LD      A,B                         ;A := fresh copy header
3341         AND     00000011B                   ;mask all but type number
3342         OR      A                           ;test for type 0
3343         JR      NZ,L16                      ;if PSW is zero note is type 0: fixed freq and atn
3344
3345 ;*** TYPE0                         ;set up NEXT_NOTE_PTR
3346         INC     HL                          ;next note [after this new note] is 4 bytes away,
3347         INC     HL                          ;point HL to it
3348         INC     HL
3349         INC     HL
3350         LD      (IX+1),L                    ;put addr in NEXT_NOTE_PTR
3351         LD      (IX+2),H
3352
3353 ;        * move new note data and fill in bytes where necessary
3354
3355         DEC     HL                          ;point HL back to 1st ROM data to move, NLEN
3356         LD      DE,05                       ;point DE to destination: bytes 5, 4, and 3
3357         CALL    DE_TO_DEST
3358         LD      BC,3                        ;move 3 bytes
3359         LDDR
3360         LD      (IX+FSTEP),0                ;set for no freq sweep
3361         LD      (IX+ASTEP),0                ;set for no atn sweep
3362         JR      MODBO
3363
3364 ;        ENDIF
3365
3366 L16
3367         CP      1                           ;test for type 1
3368         JR      NZ,L17                      ;if PSW is zero note is type 1: swept
3369                                             ; freq, fixed attenuation
3370 ;*** TYPE1                         ;set up NEXT_NOTE_PTR
3371         LD      DE,6                        ;note after this note is 6 bytes away,
3372         ADD     HL,DE                       ;pt HL to it
3373         LD      (IX+1),L                    ;store in NEXT_NOTE_PTR
3374         LD      (IX+2),H
3375
3376 ;        * move new note data and fill in bytes where necessary
3377
3378         DEC     HL                          ;point HL back to 1st ROM data to move, FSTEP
3379         INC     E                           ;E: point DE to destination: bytes 7 - 3
3380         CALL    DE_TO_DEST
3381         LD      BC,5                        ;move 5 bytes
3382         LDDR
3383         LD      (IX+ASTEP),0                ;set for no atn sweep
3384         JR      MODBO
3385
3386 ;        ENDIF
3387
3388 L17
3389         CP      2                           ;test for type 2
3390         JR      NZ,TYPE3                    ;if PSW is zero note is type 2: fixed freq, swept attenuation
3391 ;*** TYPE2                         ;set up NEXT_NOTE_PTR
3392         LD      DE,6                        ;pt HL to note after this note, since it's 6 bytes away.
3393         ADD     HL,DE                       ;pt HL to it by adding 6
3394         POP     AF                          ;A := header this note [CH#   SONGNO]
3395         PUSH    AF                          ;put back on stack
3396         AND     11000000B                   ;mask SONGNO, leaving CH#
3397         JR      NZ,L18                      ;if PSW is zero this is a noise note, which is only 5 ROM bytes long
3398         DEC     HL                          ;so move HL back 1 byte
3399
3400 ;        ENDIF
3401
3402 L18
3403         LD      (IX+1),L                    ;put addr in NEXT_NOTE_PTR
3404         LD      (IX+2),H
3405
3406 ;        * move new note data and fill in bytes where necessary
3407
3408         DEC     HL                          ;point HL back to 1st ROM data to move, APS
3409         LD      E,9                         ;point DE to destination: bytes 9,8,5 - 3
3410         CALL    DE_TO_DEST
3411         LD      BC,2                        ;move 2 bytes
3412         LDDR                                ;when done, DE points to FSTEP, HL to ROM NLEN
3413         XOR     A
3414         LD      (DE),A                      ;FSTEP := 0 for no freq sweep
3415         DEC     DE                          ;pt DE to RAM NLEN
3416         DEC     DE
3417         LD      C,3                         ;move last 3 ROM bytes if this is a noise note, garbage
3418         LDDR                                ;will be loaded into byte 3, buts that's OK
3419         JR      MODBO
3420
3421 ;        ENDIF
3422
3423 ;        if here, note is type 3: swept freq, swept attenuation
3424
3425 TYPE3                                           ;set up NEXT_NOTE_PTR
3426         LD      DE,8                         ;note after this note is 8 bytes away.
3427         ADD     HL,DE                        ;pt HL to it
3428         LD      (IX+1),L                     ;put addr in NEXT_NOTE_PTR
3429         LD      (IX+2),H
3430
3431 ;        * move new note data and fill in bytes where necessary
3432
3433         DEC     HL                          ;point HL back to 1st ROM data to move, APS
3434         PUSH    IX                          ;point DE to destination: bytes 9 - 3
3435         POP     IY                          ;IY := addr byte 0 [and DE = 6]
3436         INC     E                           ;DE := 9
3437         ADD     IY,DE                       ;IY := addr byte 9 [APS]
3438         PUSH    IY
3439         POP     DE                          ;DE := addr APS
3440         LD      BC,7                        ;move 7 bytes
3441         LDDR
3442
3443 ;        * modify byte 0 basis header new note
3444
3445 MODBO
3446         PUSH    IX                          ;pt HL to byte 0
3447         POP     HL
3448         POP     AF                          ;A := header new note
3449         POP     BC                          ;B := SONGNO
3450         CP      INACTIVE                    ;test for inactive [song over, as detected above]
3451         RET     Z
3452         LD      D,A                         ;save header in D
3453         AND     3FH                         ;Rid channel bits
3454         CP      04                          ;Special effect
3455         JR      NZ,L20_LOAD_NEX
3456         LD      B,62
3457
3458 L20_LOAD_NEX:
3459         LD      A,D                         ;restore A to header
3460         AND     0C0H                        ;A := CH# 0 0 0 0 0 0
3461         OR      B                           ;A := new CH#   SONGNO
3462         LD      (HL),A                      ;store back in byte 0
3463
3464 ;        ENDIF
3465
3466 ;*** L19
3467         RET
3468
3469 DE_TO_DEST
3470         PUSH    IX                          ;DE passed = offset from byte 0, RETed w addr byte offset
3471         POP     IY                          ;IY := addr byte 0 [and DE = offset]
3472         ADD     IY,DE                       ;IY := addr byte 0 + offset
3473         PUSH    IY
3474         POP     DE                          ;DE := addr of destination byte in SxDATA
3475         RET
3476
3477 ;        END LOADNEX
3478

3480 ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
3481 ;
3482 ; Out_to_sound_port
3483 ;             input:   a=sound port data to output
3484 ;             register usage:  n.a.
3485 ;
3486 OUT_TO_SOUND_PORT:
3487         PUSH    BC                          ;SAVE BC
3488         LD      B,A                         ;THE IDEA IS TO GET THE
3489         LD      A,(SOUNDPORT)               ;INDIRECT PORT ADDRESSING
3490         LD      C,A                         ;THRU THE PORT TABLE IN EOS
3491         OUT     (C),B
3492         LD      A,B
3493         POP     BC
3494         RET
