3497 ;====================================================================
3498 ;====================================================================
3499 ;
3500

3502         ;GLB    __QUERY_FILE,__SET_FILE,__MAKE_FILE
3503         ;GLB    __FILE_QUERY
3504         ;EXT    FCB_HEAD_ADDR,FCB_DATA_ADDR
3505         ;EXT    SCAN_FOR_FILE
3506         ;EXT    BLOCKS_REQ,USER_BUF,USER_NAME
3507         ;EXT    BUF_START,BUF_END
3508         ;EXT    STRCMP,BASECMP
3509         ;EXT    NEW_HOLE_SIZE,NEW_HOLE_START,HOLE_FILE_NAME
3510         ;EXT    EOS_YEAR,EOS_MONTH,EOS_DAY
3511         ;EXT    BLK_STRT_PTR,VOL_BLK_SZ      ;DLS(8/29/83)
3512         ;EXT    READ_BLOCK,WRITE_BLOCK_CHECK_IF_DIRECTORY
3513         ;EXT    DIR_BLOCK_NO,FOUND_AVAIL_ENTRY
3514         ;EXT    FILENAME_CMPS
3515
3516 ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
3517 ;
3518 ; __QUERY_FILE -- Read the file's directory entry. (USES STRCMP FOR FILE NAME COMPARISIONS)
3519 ; __FILE_QUERY -- SAME AS ABOVE BUT SETS UP SCAN_FOR_FILE FOR BASE COMPARES ( USES BASECMP)
3520 ;
3521 ; CALLING PARAMETERS: Device number in A
3522 ;                     address of name string in DE
3523 ;                     address of buffer in HL
3524 ;
3525 ; EXIT PARAMETERS: if no errors -- Z = 1; A = 0; BCDE = file's start block;
3526 ;                  directory entry in caller's buffer
3527 ;                  if errors -- Z = 0; A = error code; DE = junk;
3528 ;                  caller's buffer undefined
3529 ;
3530 ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
3531
3532 __FILE_QUERY:
3533         SCF                                 ; THIS ENTRY DOES NOTHING MORE THAN SET CARRY FLAG
3534         JR      QUEER
3535
3536 __QUERY_FILE
3537         SCF                                 ; THIS ENTRY CLEARS CARRY FLAG
3538         CCF
3539 QUEER:
3540         PUSH    HL                          ;SAVE REG'S
3541         PUSH    IX
3542
3543         PUSH    AF
3544
3545         JR      C,SET_UP_A                  ; DID WE COME IN AT FILE_QUERY
3546
3547         XOR     A                           ; USE STRCMP FOR FILE NAME COMPARE
3548 SET_UP_A:
3549         LD      (FILENAME_CMPS),A           ; 0 = COMPARE COMPLETE FILE NAME
3550                                             ; NOT 0 - COMPARE ONLY THE BASE
3551         POP     AF
3552
3553         LD      (USER_BUF),HL               ;SAVE USER'S DATA ADDRESS
3554
3555         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)          ;POINT TO SYSTEM'S FCB
3556         LD      H,D                         ;GET NAME POINTER IN HL
3557         LD      L,E
3558         CALL    SCAN_FOR_FILE               ;LOOK IN TH' 'RECTORY
3559         JR      NZ,Q_ERROR                  ;BRANCH IF ERROR RETURNED
3560
3561         PUSH    DE                          ;SAVE START BLOCK OF FILE
3562         PUSH    BC
3563
3564         LD      DE,(USER_BUF)               ;GET BUFFER ADDRESS IN DE
3565         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)          ;GET ADDR OF ENTRY IN HL
3566         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
3567         LD      BC,DIR_ENT_LENGTH-3         ;SET BYTE COUNT TO LOAD ONLY VALID INFO THAT
3568                                             ;THAT WAS STORED ON THE DEVICE
3569
3570         LDIR                                ;COPY DATA TO CALLER'S BUFFER
3571
3572         POP     BC                          ;GET FILE'S START BLOCK
3573         POP     DE
3574
3575         XOR     A                           ;SHOW NO ERROR
3576         LD      (FILENAME_CMPS),A           ; DEFAULT FOR SCAN FOR FILE
3577 Q_ERROR:
3578         POP     IX                          ;RESTORE REG'S
3579         POP     HL
3580         RET
3581

3583 ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
3584 ;
3585 ; __SET_FILE -- Re-write the file's directory entry.
3586 ;
3587 ; CALLING PARAMETERS: Device number in A
3588 ;                     address of name string in DE
3589 ;                     address of buffer in HL
3590 ;
3591 ; EXIT PARAMETERS: if no errors -- Z = 1; A = 0;
3592 ;                  directory entry updated
3593 ;                  if errors -- Z = 0; A = error code;
3594 ;                  directory entry unchanged
3595 ;
3596 ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
3597
3598 __SET_FILE
3599         PUSH    BC                          ;SAVE REG'S
3600         PUSH    DE
3601         PUSH    HL
3602         PUSH    IX
3603         LD      (USER_BUF),HL               ;SAVE ADDR OF USER'S DATA
3604
3605         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)          ;POINT TO SYSTEM'S FCB
3606
3607         LD      H,D                         ;GET STRING ADDRESS IN HL
3608         LD      L,E
3609         CALL    SCAN_FOR_FILE               ;GET THE DIR ENTRY
3610         JR      NZ,S_ERROR                  ;BRANCH IF ERROR
3611
3612         LD      HL,(USER_BUF)               ;GET BUFFER ADDRESS
3613         LD      E,(IX+FCB_POINTER)          ;GET ENTRY ADDR IN DE
3614         LD      D,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
3615         LD      BC,DIR_ENT_LENGTH-3         ;SET BYTE COUNT
3616         LDIR                                ;COPY THEIR BUFFER TO BLOCK BUFFER
3617
3618 TAPE_TIMED_OUT:
3619         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)           ;SET DEVICE NUMBER
3620         LD      HL,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)           ;GET ADDR OF MY BUFFER
3621         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)            ;GET BLOCK ADDRESS IN BCDE
3622         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
3623         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
3624         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
3625         CALL    WRITE_BLOCK                 ;RE-WRITE THE DIRECTORY BLOCK
3626
3627         JR      NZ,WRITE_ERRORS
3628         XOR     A                           ;SHOW NO ERRORS
3629
3630 WRITE_ERRORS:
3631 S_ERROR
3632
3633         POP     IX                          ;RESTORE REG'S
3634         POP     HL
3635         POP     DE
3636         POP     BC
3637         RET
3638

3640 ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
3641 ;
3642 ; __MAKE_FILE: creates a file in the directory.
3643 ;
3644 ; ENTRY PARAMETERS: called with device ID in A; address of name
3645 ;                   string in HL; file size (in bytes) in BC DE.
3646 ;
3647 ; NOTE:  IF FILE SIZE = 0 (BC DE), THEN THE REMAINDER OF THE TAPE IS
3648 ;        ALLOCATED TO THE FILE
3649 ;
3650 ; EXIT PARAMETERS: CONDITION FLAGS
3651 ;                  Z  -   NO ERRORS
3652 ;                  NZ -   ERRORS
3653 ;                         A = ERROR CODE
3654 ;
3655 ;        ALL REGISTERS ARE PRESERVED EXCEPT AF
3656 ;
3657 ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
3658
3659 FOUND_ENTRY     EQU     0                   ; BIT INDICATING DELETED FILE FOUND FOR OVERLAYING
3660
3661 __MAKE_FILE
3662         PUSH    IY                          ;SAVE REGISTERS
3663         PUSH    IX
3664         PUSH    HL
3665         PUSH    DE
3666         PUSH    BC
3667
3668         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)          ;POINT TO SYSTEM FCB
3669         LD      (IX+FCB_MODE),0             ; INIT IT IN CASE IT WAS SET
3670
3671         LD      (IX+FCB_DEVICE),A           ;SAVE DEVICE NUMBER
3672         LD      (USER_NAME),HL              ;SAVE POINTER TO NAME
3673
3674         LD      A,B                         ; CHECK IF TO ALLOCATE REST OF TAPE
3675         OR      C
3676         JR      NZ,GOT_FILE_SIZE            ; NOPE, GOT SIZE
3677
3678         LD      A,E
3679         OR      D
3680         JR      NZ,GOT_FILE_SIZE
3681         SET     MODE_REMAINDER_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ; FLAG IT
3682
3683         JR      SET_UP_DIR
3684
3685
3686 ; SINCE WE ARE CALLED WITH A BYTE COUNT, DIVIDE IT BY 1024
3687 ; (SHIFT RIGHT 10 BITS) TO GET A BLOCK COUNT.
3688
3689 GOT_FILE_SIZE:
3690         LD      E,D                         ;DO A QUICK SHIFT BY 8
3691         LD      D,C
3692         LD      C,B
3693         LD      B,0
3694         SRL     C                           ;SHIFT ONE MORE
3695         RR      D
3696         RR      E
3697         SRL     C                           ;THEN ONE LAST TIME
3698         RR      D
3699         RR      E
3700
3701 ; FOR THIS TO BE A LEGAL FILE SIZE, IT MUST FIT INTO DE.
3702
3703         LD      A,C                         ;CHECK FOR TOO BIG
3704         OR      A
3705         JP      NZ,TOO_BIG                  ;JUMP IF TOO BEEG, SENOR
3706         INC     DE                          ;ROUND UP IN CASE THERE WAS A FRACTION
3707         LD      A,E                         ;CHECK FOR TOO BIG AGAIN
3708         OR      D
3709         JP      Z,TOO_BIG
3710         LD      (BLOCKS_REQ),DE             ;ELSE SAVE THE BLOCK COUNT
3711
3712 SET_UP_DIR:
3713         LD      DE,0                        ; ZERO OUT HI ADDRESS
3714         LD      (BLOCKS_REQ+2),DE
3715         LD      (DIR_BLOCK_NO),DE
3716         XOR     A
3717         LD      (FOUND_AVAIL_ENTRY),A
3718
3719         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK),1            ;INIT NUMBER OF FIRST DIR BLOCK
3720         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+1),A          ; ZERO IT OUT
3721         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+2),A
3722         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+3),A
3723
3724         LD      (IX+FCB_START_BLOCK),1      ;INIT NUMBER OF FIRST DIR BLOCK
3725         LD      (IX+FCB_START_BLOCK+1),A
3726         LD      (IX+FCB_START_BLOCK+2),A
3727         LD      (IX+FCB_START_BLOCK+3),A
3728
3729         LD      DE,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)          ;INIT POINTER TO BUFFER
3730         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),E
3731         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),D
3732
3733         LD      (BUF_START),DE              ;ALSO INIT HERE WITH SAME
3734         LD      HL,1024                     ;CALC ADDR OF END OF BUFFER TOO
3735         ADD     HL,DE
3736         LD      (BUF_END),HL                ;AND SAVE IT
3737
3738 ;THIS LABEL NOT USED IN THIS ROUTINE, NOT DECLARED GLOBAL
3739 ;REMOVED BECAUSE IT CONFLICTS WITH IDENTICAL LABEL ELSEWHERE
3740 ;READ_TIMEOUT:
3741         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)           ;GET PARAMETERS FROM FCB
3742         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)
3743         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
3744         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)
3745         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
3746         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
3747         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
3748         CALL    READ_BLOCK                  ;THEN READ THE FIRST BLOCK
3749
3750         JP      NZ,MAKE_ERROR               ;BRANCH IF ERROR
3751
3752 SET_UP_FCB:
3753         LD      IY,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)          ;POINT     THE BUFFER

3754 *
3755         LD      A,(IY+VOL_DIRSIZE)      ;GET THE DIR SIZE BYTE
3756         AND     7FH                     ;ZERO THE PERM BIT
3757 ;       INC     A                       ;BECAUSE WE START IN BLOCK 1
3758 ;       DEC     A                       ;TO SOLVE PICKET FENCE PROBLEM
3759         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK),A   ;SET THE LAST BLOCK #
3760         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+1),0 ;OTHER BYTES WILL BE ZERO
3761         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+2),0
3762         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+3),0
3763
3764         CALL    CHECK_IF_DIRECTORY      ; CHECK IF DIRECTORY EXISTS
3765         JP      NZ,MAKE_ERROR
3766
3767         LD      B,ENT_PER_BLOCK-1       ;INIT ENTRY COUNT, ALLOW FOR VOL ID
3768
3769 NEXT_ENT
3770         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)      ;GET CURRENT POINTER
3771         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
3772         LD      DE,DIR_ENT_LENGTH       ;GET LENGTH OF AN ENTRY
3773         ADD     HL,DE                   ;ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT ENTRY
3774         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),L      ;AND SAVE IT AGAIN
3775         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),H
3776
3777 CHECK_ENT
3778         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)      ;GET POINTER INTO IY THE LONG WAY
3779         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
3780         PUSH    HL
3781         POP     IY                      ;!!!
3782
3783         LD      A,(IY+DIR_ATTR)         ;GET THE ATTRIBUTE BYTE FOR THIS ENTRY
3784         LD      C,A                     ;SAVE IT HERE FOR NOW
3785         BIT     ATTR_HOLE_BIT,A         ;CHECK IF HOLE
3786         JP      NZ,FOUND_HOLE           ;BRANCH IF IT'S A HOLE!!! <<------
3787
3788         BIT     MODE_REMAINDER_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ; DO WE ALLOCATE REST OF TAPE?
3789         JR      NZ,D_FILE               ;YUP. DON'T BOTHER CHECKING
3790
3791         BIT     ATTR_DEL_BIT,C          ;IS IT A DELETED FILE?
3792         JR      Z,ACTIVE_FILE           ;NOPE. AN ACTIVE ONE
3793
3794 *       LET'S LOOK FOR DELETED FILE TO USE
3795
3796         LD      L,(IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH)   ; GET ALLOCATED SIZE
3797         LD      H,(IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH+1)
3798
3799         LD      DE,(BLOCKS_REQ)         ; MIN NO OF BLOCKS NEEDED
3800         OR      A                       ;clear carry flag
3801         SBC     HL,DE                   ; WILL WE FIT?
3802         JR      C,D_FILE                ; NOPE. LOOK FOR ANOTHER ENTRY
3803
3804         LD      HL,FOUND_AVAIL_ENTRY    ; FLAG BYTE
3805         BIT     FOUND_ENTRY,(HL)        ; DO WE ALREADY HAVE FIRST FIT?
3806         JR      NZ,D_FILE               ; YUP. DON'T EVEN CONSIDER THIS ONE
3807
3808         SET     FOUND_ENTRY,(HL)
3809         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)        ;GET BLOCK NO OF THIS DIRECTORY
3810         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
3811
3812         LD      (DIR_BLOCK_NO),DE       ; SAVE IT
3813         JR      D_FILE
3814
3815
3816 ; IF THIS IS A REAL FILE, WE CAN SEE IF ITS NAME MATCHES THE
3817 ; ONE WE ARE ABOUT TO MAKE -- IT'S AN ERROR IF SO.
3818
3819 ACTIVE_FILE:
3820         LD      E,(IX+FCB_POINTER)      ;POINT TO THIS ENTRY
3821         LD      D,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
3822         LD      HL,(USER_NAME)          ;POINT TO DESIRED NAME
3823         CALL    BASECMP                 ;COMPARE THE BASE OF THE FILENAMES
3824         JP      Z,FILE_EXISTS           ;BRANCH IF THEY'RE THE SAME
3825 D_FILE
3826         DJNZ    NEXT_ENT                ;ELSE GO TO NEXT ENTRY (IF IT EXISTS)
3827
3828         LD      HL,(BUF_START)          ;IF NOT RESET FCB FOR NEXT BLOCK
3829         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),L
3830         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),H
3831         INC     (IX+FCB_BLOCK)          ;INC THE BLOCK -- NO CARRY OUT
3832
3833 ; DO 4-BYTE COMPARE OF FCB_BLOCK VS FCB_LAST_BLOCK TO SEE
3834 ; IF THERE'S MORE FILE LEFT.
3835
3836         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+3) ;COMPARE MS BYTES
3837         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
3838         JP      C,FULL_DIR              ;BRANCH IF BLOCK IS LARGER
3839         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+2) ;COMPARE NEXT BYTES
3840         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
3841         JP      C,FULL_DIR              ;BRANCH IF BLOCK IS LARGER
3842         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+1) ;COMPARE NEXT BYTES
3843         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
3844         JP      C,FULL_DIR              ;BRANCH IF BLOCK IS LARGER
3845         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+0) ;COMPARE LS BYTES
3846         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+0)
3847         JP      C,FULL_DIR              ;BRANCH IF BLOCK IS LARGER
3848 ; IF WE FALL OUT, FCB_BLOCK <= FCB_LAST_BLOCK.
3849 DO_READ_AGAIN:
3850
3851         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)       ;SET UP THE PARAMETERS
3852         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)
3853         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
3854         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)
3855         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
3856         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
3857         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
3858         CALL    READ_BLOCK              ;AND READ THE NEXT BLOCK OF ENTRIES
3859         JP      NZ,MAKE_ERROR           ; NOPE, A REAL ERROR
3860
3861 NO_TIMEOUT:
3862         LD      B,ENT_PER_BLOCK         ;SET THE ENTRY COUNTER
3863         JP      CHECK_ENT               ;AND GO CHECK THE NEW ENTRY
3864
3865 FULL_DIR:
3866         LD      HL,FOUND_AVAIL_ENTRY
3867         BIT     FOUND_ENTRY,(HL)        ; HAVE WE FOUND AN ENTRY?
3868         JP      Z,FULL_DIR_EXIT         ; NOPE, EXIT
3869         JR      USE_ENTRY               ; YUP, CREATE NEW ENTRY THERE
3870
3871 ; ARRIVE HERE WHEN WE'VE FOUND THE HOLE ENTRY IN THE DIRECTORY.
3872 ; IY IS STILL POINTING TO THE ENTRY IN THE BUFFER.
3873
3874 FOUND_HOLE
3875         LD      HL,FOUND_AVAIL_ENTRY
3876         BIT     FOUND_ENTRY,(HL)        ; HAVE WE FOUND A DELETED ENTRY WE COULD USE?
3877         JR      Z,USE_HOLE              ; NOPE, STICK IT IN THE END
3878
3879 USE_ENTRY:
3880         RES     FOUND_ENTRY,(HL)        ; RESET FOR NEXT TIME
3881
3882         LD      L,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)        ; GET THE CURRENT BLOCK
3883         LD      H,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
3884
3885         LD      DE,(DIR_BLOCK_NO)       ; BLOCK NO WHERE WE HAVE ENTRY TO USE
3886         OR      A                       ;clear carry flag
3887         SBC     HL,DE                   ; SAME BLOCK?
3888
3889         LD      HL,(BUF_START)          ; POINT TO START OF BLOCK
3890         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),L
3891         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),H
3892
3893         JR      Z,GOT_BLOCK             ; YUP, DON'T NEED TO READ IN ANOTHER BLOCK
3894
3895         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK),E        ; GET THIS BLOCK
3896         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+1),D
3897
3898         LD      BC,0
3899         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)       ; DEVICE ID
3900
3901         CALL    READ_BLOCK
3902         JP      NZ,MAKE_ERROR
3903         JR      GOT_BLOCK               ; READ IN NEW DIRECTORY
3904
3905 NEXT_DIR:
3906 TOO_SMALL:
3907         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)      ; GET POINTER TO DIR ENTRY
3908         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
3909
3910         LD      DE,DIR_ENT_LENGTH       ; LENGTH OF ENTRY
3911
3912         ADD     HL,DE                   ; POINT TO NEXT ENTRY
3913
3914         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),L
3915         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),H
3916
3917 GOT_BLOCK:
3918         PUSH    HL
3919         POP     IY
3920
3921         BIT     ATTR_DEL_BIT,(IY+DIR_ATTR) ; IS IT DELETED?
3922         JR      Z,NEXT_DIR              ; NOPE, LOOK FOR ANOTHER DELETED ENTRY
3923
3924         LD      L,(IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH)   ; GET ALLOCATED AMOUNT OF DELETED FILE
3925         LD      H,(IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH+1)
3926
3927         LD      DE,(BLOCKS_REQ)         ; MIN AMOUNT NEEDED
3928
3929         PUSH    HL
3930         OR      A                       ;clear carry flag
3931         SBC     HL,DE                   ; WILL IT FIT?
3932         POP     HL
3933
3934         JR      C,TOO_SMALL             ; NOPE, TRY ANOTHER ONE
3935
3936         LD      (BLOCKS_REQ),HL         ; FORCE ALLOCATED AMOUNT
3937
3938         CALL    LOAD_NEW_ENTRY_INFO
3939
3940         JP      NZ,MAKE_ERROR
3941
3942         JP      TIME_TO_WRITE
3943
3944 ; COMPARE FCB_BLOCK TO FCB_LAST_BLOCK.  GO TO MORE_BLOCKS
3945 ; IF FCB_LAST_BLOCK > FCB_BLOCK.
3946
3947 USE_HOLE:
3948         LD      A,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
3949         CP      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+3)
3950         JR      C,MORE_BLOCKS
3951         LD      A,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
3952         CP      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+2)
3953         JR      C,MORE_BLOCKS
3954         LD      A,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
3955         CP      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+1)
3956         JR      C,MORE_BLOCKS
3957         LD      A,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)
3958         CP      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK)
3959         JR      C,MORE_BLOCKS
3960
3961 ; FALL THROUGH IF THIS IS THE LAST BLOCK OF THE FILE.
3962
3963         LD      A,B                      ;LOOK AT ENTRY COUNTER
3964         CP      1
3965         JP      Z,TAPE_FULL             ;ERROR IF NO ENTRIES LEFT VACANT
3966
3967 MORE_BLOCKS
3968         LD      L,(IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH)   ;GET THE SIZE OF THE HOLE
3969         LD      H,(IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH+1)
3970
3971         BIT     MODE_REMAINDER_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ; NEED TO ALLOCATE REST OF TAPE?
3972         JR      Z,CHECK_HOLE_SIZE       ; NOPE
3973
3974         LD      A,H                     ; YUP. CHECK IF THERE ARE ANY BLOCKS LEFT
3975         OR      L
3976         JP      Z,TAPE_FULL             ; TAPE FULL
3977
3978         LD      (BLOCKS_REQ),HL         ; NOT FULL. REQUEST REST OF TAPE
3979         RES     MODE_REMAINDER_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE)
3980
3981 CHECK_HOLE_SIZE:
3982         LD      DE,(BLOCKS_REQ)         ;GET THE REQUESTED SIZE
3983         OR      A
3984         SBC     HL,DE                   ;COMPARE THEM
3985         JP      C,TAPE_FULL             ;BRANCH IF THE HOLE IS TOO SMALL
3986         LD      (NEW_HOLE_SIZE),HL      ;ELSE SAVE THE RESULT
3987
3988
3989 ; ADD BLOCKS_REQ TO DIR_START_BLOCK TO FIND THE HOLE'S NEW
3990 ; START BLOCK.  NOTICE HOW STOOOPID THE Z80 IS WITH ADDITION!
3991         LD      HL,BLOCKS_REQ           ;POINT TO THE NUMBER TO ADD
3992         LD      A,(IY+DIR_START_BLOCK)
3993         ADD     A,(HL)
3994         INC     HL
3995         LD      (NEW_HOLE_START),A
3996         LD      A,(IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+1)
3997         ADC     A,(HL)
3998         INC     HL
3999         LD      (NEW_HOLE_START+1),A
4000         LD      A,(IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+2)
4001         ADC     A,(HL)
4002         INC     HL
4003         LD      (NEW_HOLE_START+2),A
4004         LD      A,(IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+3)
4005         ADC     A,(HL)
4006         INC     HL
4007         LD      (NEW_HOLE_START+3),A
4008
4009         PUSH    BC                      ;SAVE ENTRY COUNT (IN B)
4010
4011         CALL    LOAD_NEW_ENTRY_INFO
4012
4013         POP     BC                      ;RESTORE ENTRY COUNT
4014
4015         JP      NZ,MAKE_ERROR
4016
4017         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)      ;GET CURRENT POINTER
4018
4019
4020         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
4021         LD      DE,DIR_ENT_LENGTH       ;GET LENGTH OF AN ENTRY
4022         ADD     HL,DE                   ;ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT ENTRY
4023         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),L      ;AND SAVE IT AGAIN
4024         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),H
4025
4026         DJNZ    NOT_END                 ;JUMP IF THERE ARE MORE ENTRIES
4027
4028 ;THIS LABEL NOT USED IN THIS ROUTINE, NOT DECLARED GLOBAL
4029 ;REMOVED BECAUSE IT CONFLICTS WITH IDENTICAL LABEL ELSEWHERE
4030 ;WRITE_AGAIN:
4031         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)       ;GET PARAMETERS FROM FCB
4032         LD      HL,(BUF_START)
4033         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)
4034         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
4035         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
4036         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
4037
4038         CALL    WRITE_BLOCK
4039
4040         JP      NZ,MAKE_ERROR           ; NOPE, A REAL LIVE ERROR
4041
4042
4043 RESET_THE_POINTER:
4044         LD      HL,(BUF_START)          ;RESET THE POINTER
4045         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),L
4046         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),H
4047
4048         INC     (IX+FCB_BLOCK)          ;INC BLOCK -- NO CARRY OUT
4049
4050 TIMEOUT_IN_READ:
4051         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)       ;GET PARAMETERS FROM FCB
4052         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)
4053         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
4054         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)
4055         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
4056         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
4057         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
4058         CALL    READ_BLOCK              ;AND READ THE NEXT BLOCK
4059
4060         JR      NZ,MAKE_ERROR           ; NOPE, ERROR!!!!!!
4061
4062
4063         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)      ;GET POINTER INTO IY THE LONG WAY
4064         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
4065
4066 NOT_END:
4067         PUSH    HL
4068         POP     IY                      ;!!!!
4069
4070 *
4071 * STILL TRYING TO CALC THE NEW START ADDR
4072 *
4073         LD      HL,(NEW_HOLE_SIZE)
4074         LD      (IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH),L   ; BLOCKS LEFT ON TAPE
4075         LD      (IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH+1),H
4076
4077         LD      A,(NEW_HOLE_START)
4078         LD      (IY+DIR_START_BLOCK),A
4079         LD      A,(NEW_HOLE_START+1)
4080         LD      (IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+1),A
4081         LD      A,(NEW_HOLE_START+2)
4082         LD      (IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+2),A
4083         LD      A,(NEW_HOLE_START+3)
4084         LD      (IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+3),A
4085
4086         LD      (IY+DIR_ATTR),ATTR_HOLE ; SET HOLE UP
4087
4088         PUSH    IY
4089         POP     DE
4090
4091         LD      HL,HOLE_FILE_NAME
4092         LD      BC,12
4093         LDIR
4094
4095 TIME_TO_WRITE:
4096         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)       ;GET PARAMETERS FROM FCB
4097         LD      HL,(BUF_START)
4098         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)
4099         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
4100         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
4101         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
4102         CALL    WRITE_BLOCK             ;WRITE THE BLOCK OUT
4103
4104         JR      NZ,MAKE_ERROR           ; IT'S A BOO BOO!!
4105         XOR     A                       ;SHOW NO ERROR
4106
4107 LETS_GET_OUT:
4108 MAKE_ERROR:
4109                                             ;RESTORE THE REGISTERS
4110         OR      A
4111         POP     BC
4112         POP     DE
4113         POP     HL
4114         POP     IX
4115         POP     IY
4116         RET
4117
4118 TOO_BIG
4119         LD      A,TOO_BIG_ERR           ;SHOW AN ERROR
4120         JR      MAKE_ERROR
4121
4122
4123 FILE_EXISTS
4124         LD      A,FILE_EXISTS_ERR
4125         JR      MAKE_ERROR
4126
4127 FULL_DIR_EXIT:
4128         LD      A,FULL_DIR_ERR
4129         JR      MAKE_ERROR
4130
4131 TAPE_FULL
4132         LD      A,FULL_TAPE_ERR
4133         JR      MAKE_ERROR
4134 *
4135 *
4136 * SUBROUTINE NAME: GET FILE NAME LENGTH
4137 *
4138 *   ENTRY: HL- POINTS TO TEXT STRING
4139 *   EXIT:  Z=1=FOUND AND PROPER SIZE (1-12)
4140 *          BC=BYTE COUNT
4141 *          A=TRASHED
4142 *
4143 *          Z=0=ERROR
4144 *          BC=TRASH
4145 *          A=FILE_NAME_TOO_LONG
4146 * DLS(8/28/83)
4147 *
4148 GET_FILE_NM_LEN:
4149         PUSH    HL                      ;SAVE THE POINTER TO FILE NAME
4150         LD      B,12                    ;SCAN UP TO 12 BYTE
4151         LD      C,1                     ;SET COUNT TO 1
4152 SRCH_LOOP:
4153         LD      A,(HL)                  ;GET FN()
4154         CP      03                      ;ETX
4155         JR      Z,GOT_IT
4156 *
4157         INC     C
4158         INC     HL                      ;ADVANCE POINTER TO FILE NAME STRING
4159         DJNZ    SRCH_LOOP
4160 *
4161 ERR_GFN
4162         LD      A,FILE_NM_ERR
4163         OR      A                       ;SHOW ERROR
4164         POP     HL
4165         RET
4166 *
4167 GOT_IT
4168         LD      A,C
4169         CP      1                       ;ETX ALONE IS NOT VALID
4170         JR      Z,ERR_GFN
4171         LD      B,0
4172         XOR     A                       ;SHOW OK
4173         POP     HL
4174         RET
4175
4176
4178
4179 LOAD_NEW_ENTRY_INFO:
4180         PUSH    IY                     ;GET NAME ADDRESS INTO DE
4181         POP     DE
4182
4183         LD      HL,(USER_NAME)         ;GET USER'S STRING ADDRESS
4184 *
4185         CALL    GET_FILE_NM_LEN        ; DLS(8/28/83)
4186         JP      NZ,MAKE_ERR_1
4187 *
4188         LDIR                           ;COPY USER'S NAME INTO DIR ENTRY
4189
4190         LD      A,ATTR_USER            ;SET THE DEFAULT ATTRIBUTE
4191         LD      (IY+DIR_ATTR),A
4192
4193         LD      BC,(BLOCKS_REQ)        ;GET NEW FILE'S MAX SIZE
4194         LD      (IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH),C  ;PUT INTO DIR
4195         LD      (IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH+1),B
4196
4197
4198         LD      (IY+DIR_USED_LENGTH),1 ;INIT COUNT OF BLOCKS USED
4199         LD      (IY+DIR_USED_LENGTH+1),0
4200
4201         LD      (IY+DIR_LAST_COUNT),0  ;INIT BYTECOUNT IN LAST BLOCK
4202         LD      (IY+DIR_LAST_COUNT+1),0
4203
4204         LD      A,(EOS_YEAR)           ;INSERT THE DATE
4205         LD      (IY+DIR_YEAR),A
4206         LD      A,(EOS_MONTH)
4207         LD      (IY+DIR_MONTH),A
4208         LD      A,(EOS_DAY)
4209         LD      (IY+DIR_DAY),A
4210
4211         XOR     A
4212
4213 MAKE_ERR_1:
4214         RET
4215
4216 ;
4217 ;===============================================================
4218 ;===============================================================
4219 ;===============================================================
4220 ;
4221
4223         ;GLB    __OPEN_FILE,__CLOSE_FILE,__RESET_FILE
4224         ;EXT    FCB_HEAD_ADDR,FCB_DATA_ADDR
4225         ;EXT    BUF_START,FMGR_DIR_ENT
4226         ;EXT    __QUERY_FILE,MODE_CHECK,READ_BLOCK,WRITE_BLOCK
4227         ;EXT    __SET_FILE
4228         ;EXT    FILE_NAME_ADDR
4229
4230 ;----------------------------------------------------------------
4231 ;
4232 ; __OPEN_FILE -- Sets up an FCB for the caller to access a file.
4233 ;
4234 ; CALLING PARAMETERS: device number in A; address of name string
4235 ;                     in HL; mode in B.
4236 ;
4237 ; EXIT PARAMETERS: if no error -- Z = 1; A = file number
4238 ;                  if error -- Z = 0; A = error code; B = junk
4239 ;
4240 ;----------------------------------------------------------------
4241
4242 ; NEXT_FCB CHANGED TO O_NEXT_FCB TO PREVENT CONFLICT WITH
4243 ; IDENTICAL LABEL ELSEWHERE
4244
4245 __OPEN_FILE
4246         PUSH    IY                     ;SAVE REGISTERS
4247         PUSH    HL
4248         PUSH    IX
4249         PUSH    DE
4250
4251         PUSH    AF                     ;SAVE DEVICE NUMBER
4252         PUSH    BC                     ;SAVE MODE
4253
4254 ; FIRST, WE HAVE TO LOCATE A FREE FCB.
4255         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)     ;GET POINTER TO FIRST FCB'S HEAD
4256         LD      DE,FCB_LENGTH
4257         ADD     IX,DE                  ; SKIP OVER IT -- BELONGS TO SYSTEM
4258
4259         LD      IY,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)     ;GET POINTER TO FIRST FCB'S BODY
4260         LD      DE,1024
4261         ADD     IY,DE                  ; SKIP OVER IT TOO
4262
4263         LD      B,1                    ;SET FCB NUMBER
4264 O_NEXT_FCB
4265         LD      A,(IX+FCB_MODE)        ;GET THE MODE BYTE FROM FCB
4266         OR      A                      ;CHECK IT
4267         JR      Z,GOT_ONE              ;BRANCH IF THIS ONE IS FREE
4268
4269         LD      DE,FCB_LENGTH          ;ELSE SKIP OVER IT
4270         ADD     IX,DE
4271         LD      DE,1024
4272         ADD     IY,DE
4273
4274         INC     B                      ;INC THE FCB NUMBER
4275         LD      A,B
4276         CP      NUM_FCBS               ;ARE THERE MORE FCBS TO CHECK?
4277         JR      C,O_NEXT_FCB           ;LOOP UNTIL WE'VE SEEN THEM ALL
4278 NO_FCBS
4279         POP     BC                     ;RESTORE MODE
4280         POP     AF
4281         POP     DE
4282         POP     IX
4283         POP     HL
4284         POP     IY
4285
4286         LD      A,NO_FCB_ERR          ;SET THE ERROR CODE
4287         OR      A                     ;SET THE CONDITIONS
4288         RET
4289
4290 GOT_ONE
4291         LD      (BUF_START),IY        ;SAVE THE FCB BUFFER'S ADDRESS
4292
4293         POP     AF                    ;GET THE MODE
4294         LD      (IX+FCB_MODE),A       ;PUT IT INTO FCB
4295         POP     AF                    ;GET THE DEVICE
4296         LD      (IX+FCB_DEVICE),A     ;PUT IT INTO FCB
4297
4298         PUSH    BC                    ;SAVE THE FCB NUMBER
4299
4300         PUSH    HL
4301         POP     DE                    ; ADDRESS OF NAME STRING
4302
4303         PUSH    IX                    ;POINT TO FCB NAME (ASSUME OFFSET = 0)
4304         POP     HL
4305         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)     ;GET DEVICE NUMBER
4306         CALL    __QUERY_FILE          ;GET THE FILE'S DIR ENTRY
4307         JP      NZ,OP_ERR             ;BRANCH IF THERE WAS AN ERROR
4308                                     ;*** SET PARAMS ***
4309         CALL    MODE_CHECK            ;SEE IF MODE MATCHES ATTRIBUTES
4310         JP      NZ,OP_ERR             ;BRANCH IF NOT
4311
4312         LD      A,(IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH) ;CALC. & SET LAST BLOCK
4313         ADD     A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK)
4314         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK),A
4315         LD      A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK+1)
4316         ADC     A,(IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH+1)
4317         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+1),A
4318         LD      A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK+2)
4319         ADC     A,0
4320         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+2),A
4321         LD      A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK+3)
4322         ADC     A,0
4323         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+3),A
4324
4325         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+0)
4326         SUB     1
4327         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+0),A
4328
4329         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+1)
4330         SBC     A,0
4331         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+1),A
4332
4333         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+2)
4334         SBC     A,0
4335         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+2),A
4336
4337         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+3)
4338         SBC     A,0
4339         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+3),A
4340
4341         LD      DE,(BUF_START)         ;INIT BUFFER POINTER
4342         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),E
4343         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),D
4344
4345         LD      A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK) ;SET BLOCK TO FIRST_BLOCK
4346         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK),A
4347         LD      A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK+1)
4348         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+1),A
4349         LD      A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK+2)
4350         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+2),A
4351         LD      A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK+3)
4352         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+3),A
4353
4354         LD      A,(IX+FCB_MODE)        ;LOOK AT THE MODE
4355         AND     MODE_MODE
4356         CP      MODE_WRITE             ;IS IT WRITE MODE?
4357         JR      Z,OPEN5                ;JUMP IF SO -- NO PRE-READ NECESSARY
4358
4359 READ_TIMEOUT:
4360 *       If FCB_USED_LENGTH = 1 (file size of one block)
4361         XOR     A                      ;Load Acc with zero
4362         CP      (IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH+1) ;High order must be zero
4363         JR      NZ,NOT_SIZE_1          ;If not then past bitset
4364         INC     A                      ;Inc Acc to a 1
4365         CP      (IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH)   ;Compare with low order
4366         JR      NZ,NOT_SIZE_1          ;If not equal then exit
4367
4368 *       Then set last block bit in mode
4369         SET     MODE_LAST_BLOCK_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ;Set it
4370 *       Endif
4371 NOT_SIZE_1:
4372
4373         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)      ;GET THE DEVICE NUMBER
4374         LD      HL,(BUF_START)         ;GET BUFFER ADDRESS
4375         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)       ;GET BLOCK NUMBER
4376         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
4377         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
4378         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
4379         CALL    READ_BLOCK             ;READ THE FIRST BLOCK
4380
4381         JR      NZ,OP_ERR              ; IT'S AN ERROR
4382
4383 OPEN5
4384         XOR     A                      ;SHOW NO ERROR
4385         POP     BC                     ;GET THE FILE NUMBER IN B
4386         LD      A,B
4387
4388         JR      REST_REGS
4389 ;       POP     DE                     ;RESTORE REGISTERS
4390 ;       POP     IX
4391 ;       POP     HL
4392 ;       POP     IY
4393 ;       RET
4394
4395 OP_ERR
4396         LD      (IX+FCB_MODE),MODE_UNUSED ;MAKE THE FCB UNUSED AGAIN
4397
4398         POP     BC                     ;RESTORE REGISTERS
4399 REST_REGS:
4400         POP     DE
4401         POP     IX
4402         POP     HL
4403         POP     IY
4404         RET
4405
4406 ;----------------------------------------------------------------
4407 ;
4408 ; __CLOSE_FILE -- does the necessary clean-up and marks the FCB
4409 ;                 as unused.
4410 ;
4411 ; ENTRY PARAMETERS -- file number in A.
4412 ;
4413 ; EXIT PARAMETERS -- if no error -- Z = 1; A = 0
4414 ;                   if error -- Z = 0; A = error code
4415 ;
4416 ;----------------------------------------------------------------
4417
4418 __CLOSE_FILE
4419         PUSH    IX                     ;SAVE REGISTERS
4420         PUSH    HL
4421         PUSH    DE
4422         PUSH    BC
4423
4424         OR      A                      ;CHECK FILE NUMBER
4425         JR      Z,C_ERROR              ;BRANCH IF ZERO
4426         CP      NUM_FCBS               ;CHECK FOR MAX RANGE
4427         JR      NC,C_ERROR             ;BRANCH IF TOO LARGE
4428
4429         LD      B,A                    ;PUT IT INTO B
4430         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)     ;GET ADDRESS OF 0TH FCB
4431         LD      HL,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)     ;GET ADDRESS OF 0TH BUFFER
4432 CLOSE2
4433         LD      DE,FCB_LENGTH          ;SET OFFSET
4434         ADD     IX,DE                  ;ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT FCB
4435         LD      DE,1024
4436         ADD     HL,DE                  ;ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT BUFFER
4437         DJNZ    CLOSE2                 ;UNTIL WE GET TO THE ONE WE WANT
4438
4439         LD      A,(IX+FCB_MODE)        ;LOOK AT THE MODE BYTE
4440         OR      A
4441         JR      Z,C_ERROR              ;BRANCH IF FCB NOT IN USE
4442
4443         AND     MODE_DIRTY             ;ZERO UNWANTED BITS
4444         JR      Z,CLOSE3               ;BRANCH IF BUFFER NOT DIRTY
4445
4446         LD      (FILE_NAME_ADDR),IX    ; POINTER TO FILE NAME IN FCB
4447 WRITE_TIMEOUT:
4448         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)      ; GET THE DEVICE NUMBER
4449         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)       ;GET THE BLOCK NUMBER
4450         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
4451         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
4452         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
4453                                     ;HL STILL HAS ADDRESS OF BUFFER
4454         CALL    WRITE_BLOCK            ;FLUSH THE BLOCK TO TAPE
4455         JR      NZ,C_ERROR2            ;BRANCH IF ERROR
4456
4457 CLOSE4:
4458         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)      ; GET DEVICE ID
4459         PUSH    IX
4460         POP     HL
4461         LD      DE,(FILE_NAME_ADDR)
4462
4463         CALL    __SET_FILE
4464
4465 CLOSE3
4466         LD      (IX+FCB_MODE),MODE_UNUSED ;MARK THE FCB EMPTY
4467
4468         POP     BC                     ;RESTORE REGISTERS
4469         POP     DE
4470         POP     HL
4471         POP     IX
4472         RET
4473 C_ERROR
4474         POP     BC                     ;RESTORE REGISTERS
4475         POP     DE
4476         POP     HL
4477         POP     IX
4478         LD      A,BAD_FNUM_ERR        ;SET THE ERROR
4479         OR      A                     ;SET THE CONDITIONS
4480         RET
4481 C_ERROR2
4482         POP     BC                     ;RESTORE REGISTERS
4483         POP     DE
4484         POP     HL
4485         POP     IX
4486         OR      A                     ;SET CONDITIONS
4487         RET
4488
4489 ;----------------------------------------------------------------
4490 ;
4491 ; __RESET_FILE -- rewinds the file back to the first byte.
4492 ;
4493 ; ENTRY PARAMETERS: file number in A
4494 ;
4495 ; EXIT PARAMETERS: no error -- Z = 1; A = 0
4496 ;                  if error -- Z = 0; A = error code
4497 ;
4498 ;----------------------------------------------------------------
4499
4500 __RESET_FILE
4501         PUSH    IX                     ;SAVE REGISTERS
4502         PUSH    DE
4503         PUSH    BC
4504         PUSH    HL
4505
4506         OR      A                      ;CHECK FILE NUMBER
4507         JP      Z,R_ERROR              ;BRANCH IF ZERO
4508         CP      NUM_FCBS               ;CHECK FOR MAX RANGE
4509         JP      NC,R_ERROR             ;BRANCH IF TOO LARGE
4510
4511         LD      B,A                    ;PUT IT INTO B
4512         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)     ;GET ADDRESS OF 0TH FCB
4513         LD      HL,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)     ;GET ADDRESS OF 0TH BUFFER
4514 RES1
4515         LD      DE,FCB_LENGTH          ;SET OFFSET
4516         ADD     IX,DE                  ;ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT FCB
4517         LD      DE,1024                ;SET BUFFER SIZE
4518         ADD     HL,DE                  ;ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT BUFFER
4519         DJNZ    RES1                   ;UNTIL WE GET TO THE ONE WE WANT
4520
4521         LD      A,(IX+FCB_MODE)        ;LOOK AT THE MODE BYTE
4522         OR      A
4523         JR      Z,R_ERROR              ;BRANCH IF FCB NOT IN USE
4524
4525         AND     MODE_DIRTY             ;ZERO UNWANTED BITS
4526         JR      Z,RES2                 ;BRANCH IF BUFFER NOT DIRTY
4527
4528 TIMED_OUT:
4529         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)      ;GET THE DEVICE NUMBER
4530         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)       ;GET THE BLOCK NUMBER
4531         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
4532         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
4533         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
4534                                     ;HL STILL HAS ADDRESS OF BUFFER
4535         CALL    WRITE_BLOCK            ;FLUSH THE BLOCK TO TAPE
4536         JR      NZ,R_ERROR2            ;BRANCH IF ERROR
4537
4538 RESET_BIT:
4539         RES     MODE_DIRTY_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ;CLEAR THE DIRTY BIT
4540 RES2
4541         RES     MODE_LAST_BLOCK_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ; BACK TO FIRST BLOCK
4542         LD      A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK) ;SET BLOCK TO FIRST_BLOCK
4543         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK),A
4544         LD      A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK+1)
4545         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+1),A
4546         LD      A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK+2)
4547         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+2),A
4548         LD      A,(IX+FCB_FIRST_BLOCK+3)
4549         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+3),A
4550
4551         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+0),L   ;reset pointer for new (first) block
4552         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),H
4553
4554         LD      A,(IX+FCB_MODE)        ;GET THE MODE AGAIN
4555         AND     MODE_MODE              ;LOOK AT MODE TYPE ONLY
4556         CP      MODE_WRITE             ;ARE WE WRITING?
4557         JR      NZ,READ_IT             ;NOPE, NEED TO PRE-READ
4558
4559         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_COUNT),0
4560         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_COUNT+1),0
4561
4562         LD      (IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH),1
4563         LD      (IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH+1),0
4564
4565         JR      NO_READ
4566
4567 READ_IT:
4568         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)      ;GET THE DEVICE NUMBER
4569         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)       ;GET THE BLOCK NUMBER
4570         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
4571         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
4572         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
4573                                     ;HL STILL HAS ADDRESS OF BUFFER
4574         CALL    READ_BLOCK             ;READ THE FIRST BLOCK
4575
4576         JR      NZ,R_ERROR2            ;BRANCH ON ERROR
4577
4578 NO_READ
4579         POP     HL                     ;RESTORE THE REGISTERS
4580         POP     BC
4581         POP     DE
4582         POP     IX
4583         XOR     A                      ;SHOW NO ERROR
4584         RET
4585
4586 R_ERROR
4587         POP     HL                     ;RESTORE THE REGISTERS
4588         POP     BC
4589         POP     DE
4590         POP     IX
4591         LD      A,BAD_FNUM_ERR         ;SHOW THE ERROR
4592         OR      A                      ;SET CONDITIONS
4593         RET
4594 R_ERROR2
4595         POP     HL                     ;RESTORE REGISTERS
4596         POP     BC
4597         POP     DE
4598         POP     IX
4599         OR      A                      ;SET CONDITION
4600         RET
4601 ;
4602 ;===============================================================
4603 ;===============================================================
4604 ;===============================================================
4605 ;
4606
4608
4609 ;Packed some code to allow room for
4610
4611         ;GLB    __READ_FILE,__WRITE_FILE
4612         ;EXT    BUF_START,BUF_END,USER_BUF,BYTES_REQ,BYTES_TO_GO
4613         ;EXT    FNUM
4614         ;EXT    FCB_HEAD_ADDR,FCB_DATA_ADDR
4615         ;EXT    READ_BLOCK,WRITE_BLOCK
4616         ;EXT    MODE_CHECK
4617
4618 ;----------------------------------------------------------------
4619 ;
4620 ; __READ_FILE -- read some data from a file into the user's buffer.
4621 ;
4622 ; ENTRY PARAMETERS: device number in A; buffer address in HL;
4623 ;                   desired number of bytes in BC.
4624 ;
4625 ; EXIT PARAMETERS: no errors -- Z = 1; A = 0
4626 ;                  if errors -- Z = 0; A = error code;
4627 ;                  BC = number of bytes transferred
4628 ;
4629 ;----------------------------------------------------------------
4630
4631 ; GET_OUT CHANGED TO R_GET_OUT TO PREVENT CONFLICT WITH
4632 ; IDENTICAL LABEL ELSEWHERE
4633
4634 __READ_FILE
4635         PUSH    BC                     ;SAVE REGISTERS
4636         PUSH    DE
4637         PUSH    HL
4638         PUSH    IX
4639
4640         LD      (USER_BUF),HL          ;SAVE USER'S BUFFER ADDRESS
4641         LD      (BYTES_REQ),BC         ;SAVE REQUESTED COUNT
4642         LD      (FNUM),A               ;SAVE FILE NUMBER
4643         LD      (BYTES_TO_GO),BC       ;INIT COUNTER (COUNTS DOWN)
4644
4645         OR      A                      ;CHECK FILE NUMBER
4646         JP      Z,READ_ERR2            ;BRANCH IF ZERO
4647         CP      NUM_FCBS               ;CHECK FOR MAX RANGE
4648         JP      NC,READ_ERR2           ;BRANCH IF TOO LARGE
4649
4650         LD      B,A                    ;PUT IT INTO B
4651         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)     ;GET ADDRESS OF 0TH FCB
4652         LD      HL,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)     ;GET ADDRESS OF 0TH BUFFER
4653 READ1
4654         LD      DE,FCB_LENGTH          ;ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT FCB
4655         ADD     IX,DE
4656         LD      DE,1024                ;ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT BUFFER
4657         ADD     HL,DE
4658         DJNZ    READ1                  ;UNTIL WE GET TO THE ONE WE WANT
4659
4660         LD      (BUF_START),HL         ;SAVE ADDRESS OF MY BUFFER
4661         ADD     HL,DE                  ;POINT HL PAST END OF MY BUFFER
4662         LD      (BUF_END),HL           ;SAVE IT TOO
4663
4664                                     ;*** CHECK FOR MODE ***
4665         PUSH    IX
4666         POP     HL
4667
4668         CALL    MODE_CHECK            ;SEE IF THE MODE IS OK
4669         JP      NZ,READ_ERR1          ;BRANCH IF NOT
4670
4671 READ3
4672         BIT     MODE_LAST_BLOCK_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ;LOOK AT LAST BLOCK BIT
4673         JP      NZ,READ_LAST          ;BRANCH IF THIS IS THE LAST FILE BLOCK
4674
4675         LD      HL,(BUF_END)          ;GET END ADDRESS OF BUFFER
4676         LD      E,(IX+FCB_POINTER)    ;GET POINTER INTO BUFFER
4677         LD      D,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
4678         OR      A
4679         SBC     HL,DE                 ;CALC. BYTES LEFT IN BUFFER
4680         JR      Z,_O_BYTES_LEFT2
4681
4682         LD      B,H                   ;SAVE BYTES LEFT IN CASE WE NEED IT
4683         LD      C,L
4684
4685         LD      DE,(BYTES_TO_GO)      ;GET BYTES LEFT TO SEND
4686         OR      A
4687         SBC     HL,DE                 ;COMPARE TO WHAT WE HAVE
4688         JP      NC,ENOUGH             ;BRANCH IF WE HAVE ENOUGH DATA HERE
4689
4690 ; IF WE FALL THROUGH HERE, WE KNOW WE WILL HAVE TO GET ANOTHER
4691 ; BLOCK AFTER THIS ONE.  FIRST, LET'S TRANSFER WHAT WE HAVE.
4692
4693         LD      HL,(BYTES_TO_GO)      ;UPDATE NUMBER OF BYTES TO GO
4694         OR      A
4695         SBC     HL,BC                 ;SUBTRACT NUM OF BYTES WE ARE SENDING HERE
4696         LD      (BYTES_TO_GO),HL
4697
4698         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)    ;POINT INTO MY BUFFER
4699         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
4700         LD      DE,(USER_BUF)         ;POINT INTO THE CALLER'S BUFFER
4701                                     ;BC HAS COUNT FROM ABOVE
4702         LDIR                          ;GIVE WHAT WE HAVE
4703
4704         LD      (USER_BUF),DE         ;POINT TO NEXT SPOT
4705
4706 _O_BYTES_LEFT2:
4707         LD      HL,(BUF_START)        ;RESET THE POINTER
4708         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),L
4709         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),H
4710
4711         INC     (IX+FCB_BLOCK)        ;INC THE BLOCK NUMBER
4712         JR      NZ,READ4              ;BRANCH IF NO CARRY OUT
4713         INC     (IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
4714         JR      NZ,READ4
4715         INC     (IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
4716         JR      NZ,READ4
4717         INC     (IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
4718
4719 READ4
4720
4721         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)     ;DEVICE ID
4722         LD      HL,(BUF_START)              ; GET ADDRESS OF USERS BUFFER
4723         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)            ; GET BLOCK NUMBER
4724         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
4725         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
4726         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
4727
4728         CALL    READ_BLOCK                  ; READ THE NEXT BLOCK
4729
4730         JP      NZ,READ_ERR1                ; BRANCH IF ERROR
4731
4732 NOT_TAPE1:
4733         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+3)     ; SEE IF THIS IS THE LAST BLOCK
4734         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
4735         JR      NZ,JP_TO_READ3              ; BRANCH IF NOT
4736         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+2)
4737         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
4738         JR      NZ,JP_TO_READ3
4739         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+1)
4740         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
4741         JR      NZ,JP_TO_READ3
4742         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK)
4743         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK)
4744         JR      NZ,JP_TO_READ3
4745         SET     MODE_LAST_BLOCK_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ; ELSE SET THE LAST BLOCK BIT
4746 JP_TO_READ3:
4747         JP      READ3                       ; AND DEAL WITH THE NEXT BLOCK
4748
4749 ; ARRIVE HERE IF THIS BLOCK CONTAINS ENOUGH BYTES TO SATISFY
4750 ; THIS CALL.
4751
4752 ENOUGH:
4753         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+3)     ; SEE IF THIS IS THE LAST BLOCK
4754         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
4755         JR      NZ,READ_LAST1               ; BRANCH IF NOT
4756         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+2)
4757         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
4758         JR      NZ,READ_LAST1
4759         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+1)
4760         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
4761         JR      NZ,READ_LAST1
4762         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK)
4763         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK)
4764         JR      Z,LAST_BLOCK
4765
4766 READ_LAST1:
4767         LD      BC,(BYTES_TO_GO)            ; GET BYTE COUNT TO MOVE
4768
4769
4770         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)          ; POINT INTO MY BUFFER
4771         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
4772         LD      DE,(USER_BUF)               ; POINT TO THE USER'S BUFFER
4773         LDIR                                ; MOVE THE DATA
4774
4775         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),L          ; UPDATE MY POINTER
4776         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),H
4777
4778
4779         POP     IX                          ; RESTORE REGISTERS
4780         POP     HL
4781         POP     DE
4782         POP     BC
4783         XOR     A                           ; SHOW NO ERROR
4784         RET
4785
4786 ; ARRIVE HERE IF THIS IS THE LAST BLOCK OF THE FILE.
4787 LAST_BLOCK:
4788         SET     MODE_LAST_BLOCK_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE)
4789
4790 READ_LAST
4791         LD      HL,(BUF_START)              ; POINT TO MY BUFFER
4792         LD      E,(IX+FCB_LAST_COUNT)       ; GET # BYTES IN IT
4793         LD      D,(IX+FCB_LAST_COUNT+1)
4794         ADD     HL,DE                       ; CALC ADDR OF LAST BYTE + 1
4795         LD      E,(IX+FCB_POINTER)          ; GET CURRENT POINTER
4796         LD      D,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
4797         OR      A                           ; CLEAR CARRY
4798         SBC     HL,DE                       ; CALC # BYTES LEFT IN BUFFER
4799         LD      B,H                         ; SAVE IT IN CASE IT'S NEEDED
4800         LD      C,L
4801         JR      Z,_0_BYTES_LEFT
4802
4803         LD      DE,(BYTES_TO_GO)            ; GET REQUESTED COUNT
4804         OR      A                           ; CLEAR CARRY (UGLY Z80)
4805         SBC     HL,DE                       ; SEE IF WE HAVE ENOUGH BYTES
4806         JR      NC,READ_LAST1               ; BRANCH IF SO -- NO SWEAT
4807
4808         LD      HL,(BYTES_TO_GO)            ; UPDATE NUMBER OF BYTES TO GO
4809         OR      A
4810         SBC     HL,BC                       ; SUBTRACT NUM OF BYTES WE ARE SENDING HERE
4811         LD      (BYTES_TO_GO),HL
4812
4813         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)          ; GET POINTER TO MY BUFFER
4814         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
4815         LD      DE,(USER_BUF)               ; GET POINTER TO USER'S BUFFER
4816                                             ; BC HAS COUNT FROM ABOVE
4817         LDIR                                ; MOVE WHAT WE HAVE TO USER
4818
4819         LD      HL,(BYTES_REQ)              ; GET # BYTES REQUESTED
4820         LD      BC,(BYTES_TO_GO)            ; GET # BYTES WE DIDN'T DO
4821         OR      A
4822         SBC     HL,BC                       ; CALC. # OF BYTES WE DID DO
4823         LD      B,H                         ; PUT IT INTO BC
4824         LD      C,L
4825
4826 _0_BYTES_LEFT:
4827         LD      A,EOF_ERR                   ; SHOW AN ERROR
4828 R_GET_OUT:
4829         POP     IX                          ; RESTORE REGISTERS
4830         POP     HL
4831         POP     DE
4832         INC     SP                          ; DISCARD OLD BC
4833         INC     SP
4834
4835         OR      A
4836         RET
4837
4838 RQ_STAT_ERR:
4839         POP     IX
4840         POP     HL
4841         POP     DE
4842         POP     BC
4843
4844         LD      A,RQ_TP_STAT_ERR
4845         OR      A
4846         RET
4847
4848 READ_ERR2:
4849         LD      A,BAD_FNUM_ERR
4850
4851 READ_ERR1
4852         LD      HL,(BYTES_REQ)              ; GET # BYTES REQUESTED
4853         LD      BC,(BYTES_TO_GO)            ; GET # BYTES WE DIDN'T DO
4854         OR      A
4855         SBC     HL,BC                       ; CALC. # OF BYTES WE DID DO
4856         LD      B,H                         ; PUT IT INTO BC
4857         LD      C,L
4858
4859         JR      R_GET_OUT
4860
4861 *       POP     IX                         ; RESTORE REGISTERS
4862 *       POP     HL
4863 *       POP     DE
4864 *       INC     SP                         ; DISCARD OLD BC
4865 *       INC     SP
4866 *       OR      A                          ; SET CONDITION CODES
4867 *       RET
4868
4869 *READ_ERR2
4870 *       LD      HL,(BYTES_REQ)             ; GET # BYTES REQUESTED
4871 *       LD      BC,(BYTES_TO_GO)           ; GET # BYTES WE DIDN'T DO
4872 *       OR      A
4873 *       SBC     HL,BC                      ; CALC. # OF BYTES WE DID DO
4874 *       LD      B,H                        ; PUT IT INTO BC
4875 *       LD      C,L
4876 *
4877 *       POP     IX                         ; RESTORE REGISTERS
4878 *       POP     HL
4879 *       POP     DE
4880 *       INC     SP                         ; DISCARD OLD BC
4881 *       INC     SP
4882 *       LD      A,BAD_FNUM_ERR             ; SET THE ERROR CODE
4883 *       OR      A                          ; SET CONDITIONS
4884 *       RET
4885
4886 ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
4887 ;
4888 ; __WRITE_FILE -- write some bytes to a file.
4889 ;
4890 ; ENTRY PARAMETERS: file number in A; byte count in BC; pointer
4891 ;                   to data in HL.
4892 ;
4893 ; EXIT PARAMETERS: no errors -- Z = 1; A = 0
4894 ;                  if errors -- Z = 0; A = error code
4895 ;
4896 ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
4897
4898 __WRITE_FILE
4899         PUSH    BC                          ; SAVE REGISTERS
4900         PUSH    DE
4901         PUSH    HL
4902         PUSH    IX
4903
4904         LD      (USER_BUF),HL              ; SAVE USER'S BUFFER ADDRESS
4905         LD      (BYTES_REQ),BC             ; SAVE REQUESTED COUNT
4906         LD      (FNUM),A                   ; SAVE FILE NUMBER
4907         LD      (BYTES_TO_GO),BC           ; INIT COUNTER (COUNTS DOWN)
4908
4909         OR      A                           ; CHECK FILE NUMBER
4910         JP      Z,WRITE_ERR2               ; BRANCH IF ZERO
4911         CP      NUM_FCBS                   ; CHECK FOR MAX RANGE
4912         JP      NC,WRITE_ERR2              ; BRANCH IF TOO LARGE
4913
4914         LD      B,A                         ; PUT IT INTO B
4915         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)          ; GET ADDRESS OF 0TH FCB
4916         LD      DE,FCB_LENGTH              ; SET OFFSET
4917 WRITE1
4918         ADD     IX,DE                       ; ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT FCB
4919         DJNZ    WRITE1                      ; UNTIL WE GET TO THE ONE WE WANT
4920
4921         LD      B,A                         ; POINT HL AT MY BUFFER
4922         LD      DE,1024
4923         LD      HL,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)
4924 WRITE2
4925         ADD     HL,DE
4926         DJNZ    WRITE2
4927
4928         LD      (BUF_START),HL             ; SAVE ADDRESS OF MY BUFFER
4929         ADD     HL,DE                       ; POINT HL PAST END OF MY BUFFER
4930         LD      (BUF_END),HL               ; SAVE IT TOO
4931
4932                                             ; *** CHECK FOR MODE ***
4933         PUSH    IX
4934         POP     HL
4935
4936         CALL    MODE_CHECK
4937         JP      NZ,WRITE_ERR1              ; JUMP IF WRONG MODE
4938
4939 WRITE3
4940         LD      A,(IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH+1)
4941         CP      (IX+FCB_MAX_LENGTH+1)
4942         JR      C,SET_UP
4943         LD      A,(IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH+0)
4944         CP      (IX+FCB_MAX_LENGTH)
4945         JR      C,SET_UP
4946
4947         SET     MODE_LAST_BLOCK_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE)
4948 SET_UP:
4949         LD      HL,(BUF_END)               ; GET LAST ADDR + 1 OF BUFFER
4950         LD      E,(IX+FCB_POINTER)         ; GET CURRENT POINTER
4951         LD      D,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
4952         OR      A                           ; CLEAR CARRY
4953         SBC     HL,DE                       ; CALC. ROOM LEFT IN BUFFER
4954         LD      B,H                         ; SAVE IT IN CASE WE NEED IT
4955         LD      C,L
4956         LD      DE,(BYTES_TO_GO)            ; GET BYTES LEFT TO TRANSFER
4957         OR      A
4958         SBC     HL,DE                       ; SEE IF WE HAVE ROOM FOR IT ALL
4959         JP      NC,ROOM_OK                  ; BRANCH IF YES
4960
4961 ; WE FALL THROUGH HERE IF THE NUMBER OF BYTES TO BE WRITTEN
4962 ; IS MORE THAN THE SPACE WE HAVE IN THE CURRENT BLOCK.
4963 ; FILL THE BLOCK. WRITE IT OUT. THEN DECIDE WHAT TO DO.
4964
4965         LD      A,B                         ; CHECK IF ANY BYTES AVAILABLE
4966         OR      C
4967         JR      Z,CHECK_LAST_BLOCK          ; NOPE. EMPTY BUFFER FIRST
4968
4969         LD      HL,(BYTES_TO_GO)            ; GET THE COUNT
4970         OR      A
4971         SBC     HL,BC                       ; SUB THE ROOM IN BUFFER TO UPDATE IT
4972         LD      (BYTES_TO_GO),HL
4973
4974         LD      E,(IX+FCB_POINTER)          ; GET POINTER INTO BUFFER
4975         LD      D,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
4976         LD      HL,(USER_BUF)               ; GET POINTER TO USER DATA
4977                                             ; BC STILL HAS COUNT FROM ABOVE
4978         LDIR                                ; MOVE DATA INTO THE BUFFER
4979
4980         LD      (USER_BUF),HL               ; SAVE WHERE WE LEFT OFF
4981
4982
4983         SET     MODE_DIRTY_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ; SET THE DIRTY BIT
4984
4985 CHECK_LAST_BLOCK:
4986         BIT     MODE_LAST_BLOCK_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ; CHECK FOR LAST BLOCK
4987         JR      Z,MORE_FILE                 ; JUMP IF THIS ISN'T LAST BLOCK
4988
4989 ; IF THIS IS THE LAST BLOCK (AND IT'S NOW FULL) THERE'S NOTHING
4990 ; MORE WE CAN DO.  WE LEAVE THE DATA IN PLACE (IT WILL GET
4991 ; FLUSHED WHEN THE FILE IS CLOSED) AND REPORT AN EOF ERROR,
4992 ; MEANING THAT WE COULDN'T WRITE ALL THE DATA.
4993
4994         POP     IX                          ; RESTORE REGISTERS
4995         POP     HL
4996         POP     DE
4997         POP     BC
4998         LD      A,EOF_ERR                   ; SHOW THE ERROR
4999         OR      A                           ; SET CONDITIONS
5000         RET
5001
5002 ; WE COME HERE IF WE HAVE A FULL BUFFER AND THERE'S MORE
5003 ; DATA TO WRITE AND THERE ARE MORE BLOCKS TO THE FILE.
5004
5005 MORE_FILE
5006         INC     (IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH)        ; INCREMENT SECTORS WRITTEN TO
5007         JR      NZ,MORE_FILE1
5008         INC     (IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH+1)
5009 MORE_FILE1:
5010         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)           ; GET THE DEVICE NUMBER
5011         LD      HL,(BUF_START)              ; GET BUFFER ADDRESS
5012         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)            ; GET BLOCK NUMBER
5013         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
5014         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
5015         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
5016         CALL    WRITE_BLOCK                 ; WRITE OUT THE FULL BLOCK
5017
5018         JP      NZ,WRITE_ERR1               ; BRANCH IF ERROR
5019
5020 CLEAR_DIRTY_BIT:
5021         RES     MODE_DIRTY_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ; CLEAR THE DIRTY BIT
5022         LD      HL,(BUF_START)              ; RESET THE BUFFER POINTER
5023         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),L
5024         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),H
5025
5026         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_COUNT),0
5027         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_COUNT+1),0
5028
5029         INC     (IX+FCB_BLOCK)              ; INC THE BLOCK NUMBER
5030         JR      NZ,WRITE4                   ; BRANCH IF NO CARRY OUT
5031         INC     (IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
5032         JR      NZ,WRITE4
5033         INC     (IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
5034         JR      NZ,WRITE4
5035         INC     (IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
5036
5037 WRITE4
5038         LD      A,(IX+FCB_MODE)             ; LOOK AT THE MODE BYTE
5039         AND     MODE_MODE                   ; GET RID OF EXTRA BITS
5040         CP      MODE_UPDATE                 ; DO WE HAVE TO PRE-READ THE NEXT BLOCK?
5041         JP      NZ,WRITE3                   ; LOOP TO TOP IF NOT
5042
5043 WRITE_AGAIN:
5044         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)           ; GET THE DEVICE NUMBER
5045         LD      HL,(BUF_START)              ; GET BUFFER ADDRESS
5046         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)            ; GET BLOCK NUMBER
5047         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
5048         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
5049         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
5050         CALL    READ_BLOCK                  ; PRE-READ THE NEXT FILE BLOCK
5051
5052         JR      NZ,WRITE_ERR1               ; BRANCH IF ERROR
5053
5054
5055 ; ARRIVE HERE IF THE DATA BEING WRITTEN WILL NOT FILL THE BUFFER.
5056 ; EVERYTHING IS VERY EASY -- JUST COPY IT FROM USER'S ADDRESS
5057 ; INTO THE BUFFER AND GO AWAY.
5058
5059 ROOM_OK
5060         LD      BC,(BYTES_TO_GO)            ; GET THE BYTE COUNT
5061         LD      L,(IX+FCB_LAST_COUNT)
5062         LD      H,(IX+FCB_LAST_COUNT+1)
5063         ADD     HL,BC
5064         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_COUNT),L
5065         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_COUNT+1),H
5066         LD      E,(IX+FCB_POINTER)          ; GET POINTER INTO BUFFER
5067         LD      D,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
5068         LD      HL,(USER_BUF)               ; GET POINTER TO USER'S DATA
5069         LDIR                                ; MOVE THE DATA
5070
5071         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),E          ; UPDATE THE POINTER
5072         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),D
5073         SET     MODE_DIRTY_BIT,(IX+FCB_MODE) ; SET THE DIRTY BIT
5074
5075 ROOM1_OK:
5076         XOR     A
5077         JR      PAST_WRITE_ERR
5078 WRITE_ERR2:
5079         LD      A,BAD_FNUM_ERR
5080 WRITE_ERR1:
5081         OR      A
5082 PAST_WRITE_ERR:
5083         POP     IX                          ; RESTORE REGISTERS
5084         POP     HL
5085         POP     DE
5086         POP     BC
5087         RET
5088 *WRITE_ERR1
5089 *       POP     IX                         ; RESTORE REGISTERS
5090 *       POP     HL
5091 *       POP     DE
5092 *       POP     BC
5093 *       OR      A                          ; SET CONDITIONS
5094 *       RET
5095 *WRITE_ERR2
5096 *       POP     IX                         ; RESTORE REGISTERS
5097 *       POP     HL
5098 *       POP     DE
5099 *       POP     BC
5100 *       LD      A,BAD_FNUM_ERR             ; SHOW AN ERROR
5101 *       OR      A                          ; SET CONDITIONS
5102 *       RET
5103
5104 ;=======================================================================
5105 ;=======================================================================
5106 ;=======================================================================
5107 ;
5108
5109
5110         ;GLB    __SET_DATE,__GET_DATE
5111         ;EXT    EOS_YEAR,EOS_MONTH,EOS_DAY
5112
5113
5114 ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
5115 ;
5116 ; __SET_DATE -- Set the current date.
5117 ;
5118 ; ENTRY PARAMETERS: B = day (1..31)
5119 ;                   C = month (1..12)
5120 ;                   D = year (83..99)
5121 ;
5122 ; EXIT PARAMETERS: none
5123 ;
5124 ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
5125
5126 __SET_DATE
5127         PUSH    AF                          ; SAVE THE CURRENT A
5128
5129         LD      A,B                         ; STORE THE DAY
5130         LD      (EOS_DAY),A
5131         LD      A,C                         ; STORE THE MONTH
5132         LD      (EOS_MONTH),A
5133         LD      A,D                         ; STORE THE YEAR
5134         LD      (EOS_YEAR),A
5135
5136         POP     AF                          ; RESTORE A
5137         RET
5138
5139 ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
5140 ;
5141 ; __GET_DATE -- Reads the current date.
5142 ;
5143 ; ENTRY PARAMETERS: none
5144 ;
5145 ; EXIT PARAMETERS: no errors -- Z = 1; A = 0
5146 ;                  B = day
5147 ;                  C = month
5148 ;                  D = year
5149 ;
5150 ;                  error (date never set) -- Z = 0; A = error code
5151 ;                  B = 0
5152 ;                  C = 0
5153 ;                  D = 0
5154 ;
5155 ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
5156
5157 __GET_DATE
5158         LD      A,(EOS_DAY)
5159         LD      B,A                         ; GET THE DAY
5160         LD      A,(EOS_MONTH)
5161         LD      C,A                         ; GET THE MONTH
5162         LD      A,(EOS_YEAR)
5163         LD      D,A                         ; GET THE YEAR
5164
5165         OR      B
5166         OR      C                           ; CHECK FOR ALL = 0
5167         JR      Z,GET_ERROR                 ; BRANCH IF ERROR
5168         XOR     A                           ; SHOW NO ERROR
5169         RET
5170
5171 GET_ERROR
5172         LD      A,NO_DATE_ERR               ; SHOW AN ERROR OCCURRED
5173         OR      A                           ; CLEAR Z FLAG
5174         RET
