5177 ;=======================================================================
5178 ;=======================================================================
5179 ;
5180
5181
5182 ;
5183 ; Rev. 1   07oct515p  RPD  made __DELETE_FILE check for protected file
5184 ;
5185
5186         ;GLB    __FMGR_INIT,SCAN_FOR_FILE,STRCMP,MODE_CHECK
5187         ;GLB    READ_BLOCK,WRITE_BLOCK,BASECMP,__WRITE_BLOCK
5188         ;GLB    __MODE_CHECK,__READ_BLOCK,SCAN_FOR_FILE
5189         ;EXT    __RD_1_BLOCK,__WR_1_BLOCK,__REQUEST_STATUS
5190         ;EXT    __QUERY_FILE,__SET_FILE
5191         ;EXT    QUERY_BUFFER,FILENAME_CMPS
5192         ;EXT    FCB_HEAD_ADDR,FCB_DATA_ADDR
5193         ;EXT    USER_NAME,BUF_START
5194         ;EXT    RETRY_COUNT
5195         ;EXT    FILE_COUNT,MOD_FILE_COUNT
5196
5197
5198 ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
5199 ;
5200 ; __FMGR_INIT: sets up everything to start with.
5201 ;
5202 ; ENTRY PARAMETERS: DE contains address to place FCB buffers.
5203 ;                   and HL contains address to place FCB headers.
5204 ;
5205 ; EXIT PARAMETERS: none.
5206 ;
5207 ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
5208
5209 __FMGR_INIT
5210         LD      (FCB_DATA_ADDR),DE          ; SAVE BUFFER ADDRESS
5211         LD      (FCB_HEAD_ADDR),HL          ; SAVE HEADER ADDRESS
5212
5213         PUSH    BC                          ; SAVE REGISTERS
5214         PUSH    DE
5215         PUSH    IX
5216
5217         LD      B,NUM_FCBS                  ; SET TO # FCBS
5218         LD      DE,FCB_LENGTH               ; SET FOR ADDITION
5219         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)          ; GET ADDR OF 0TH FCB
5220
5221 INIT_LOOP
5222         LD      (IX+FCB_MODE),MODE_UNUSED   ; SHOW THE FCB IS EMPTY
5223         ADD     IX,DE                       ; ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT FCB
5224         DJNZ    INIT_LOOP                   ; LOOP FOR ALL FCBS
5225
5226         POP     IX                          ; RESTORE REGISTERS
5227         POP     DE
5228         POP     BC
5229         RET
5230
5231 ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
5232 ;
5233 ; SCAN_FOR_FILE -- Look in the directory for a file name.
5234 ;                  Reads each block of the directory into a buffer
5235 ;                  (in the system's FCB) and scans through for a
5236 ;                  match with the caller's string.
5237 ;
5238 ; ENTRY PARAMETERS: address of name string in HL; device # in A
5239 ;                   ASSUMES FILENAME_CMP SET UP FOR FILE COMPARES
5240 ;                   0 - USE STRCMP .  NOT 0 - USE BASECMP  FOR FILE NAME COMPARISONS
5241 ;
5242 ; EXIT PARAMETERS: if match found -- Z = 1; A = 0; file's start
5243 ;                  block in BCDE; directory block left in buffer.
5244 ;                  address of entry left in FCB_POINTER.
5245 ;
5246 ;                  if no match found -- Z = 0; A = error code;
5247 ;                  BCDE = junk; other stuff = junk.
5248 ;
5249 ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
5250
5251 __SCAN_FOR_FILE:
5252 SCAN_FOR_FILE
5253         PUSH    HL                          ; SAVE REGISTERS
5254         PUSH    IX
5255         PUSH    IY
5256
5257         LD      (USER_NAME),HL              ; SAVE USER'S STRING ADDRESS
5258
5259         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)          ; GET ADDRESS OF SYSTEM'S FCB
5260
5261 ; First, set up as much of the FCB as we can.
5262         LD      (IX+FCB_DEVICE),A           ; SET THE DEVICE ADDRESS PASSED TO US
5263
5264         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK),1            ; SET BLOCK TO 1
5265         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+1),0
5266         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+2),0
5267         LD      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+3),0
5268
5269         LD      DE,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)          ; SET BUFFER ADDRESS
5270         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),E
5271         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),D
5272
5273 RD_TIMEOUT:
5274         LD      HL,FILE_COUNT
5275         LD      (HL),0
5276
5277         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)           ; GET THE DEVICE NUMBER
5278         LD      HL,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)          ; GET BUFFER ADDRESS
5279         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)            ; GET BLOCK NUMBER
5280         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
5281         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
5282         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
5283         CALL    READ_BLOCK                  ; READ FIRST BLOCK OF DIRECTORY
5284         JP      NZ,SCAN_ERROR               ; BRANCH IF ERROR
5285
5286 GET_BUFF_ADDR:
5287
5288         PUSH    HL                          ; GET BUFFER ADDRESS INTO IY TOO
5289         POP     IY
5290
5291         LD      A,(IY+VOL_DIRSIZE)          ; GET THE DIRECTORY SIZE
5292         AND     07FH                        ; ZERO PERM BIT
5293 ;       INC     A                           ; ADD 1 'CAUSE WE START IN BLOCK 1
5294 ;       DEC     A                           ; SUB 1 'CAUSE OF PICKET FENCE PROBLEM
5295         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK),A       ; SET LAST BLOCK IN FCB
5296         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+1),0     ; (ALL MSBYTES WILL BE 0)
5297         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+2),0
5298         LD      (IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+3),0
5299
5300         CALL    CHECK_IF_DIRECTORY          ; CHECK IF DIRECTORY EXIST
5301         JP      NZ,SCAN_ERROR
5302
5303         LD      A,(IX+FCB_POINTER)          ; ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT ENTRY
5304         ADD     A,DIR_ENT_LENGTH
5305         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),A
5306         LD      A,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
5307         ADC     A,0
5308         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),A
5309
5310         LD      HL,FILE_COUNT
5311         INC     (HL)
5312
5313         LD      B,ENT_PER_BLOCK-1           ; SET DIR ENTRY COUNT FOR FIRST BLOCK
5314         JR      SCAN2                       ; JUMP INTO THE LOOP
5315
5316 SCAN1
5317         INC     (IX+FCB_BLOCK)              ; INC THE BLOCK NUMBER
5318                                             ; WE CAN ASSUME THERE'S NO CARRY OUT
5319
5320 ; SEE IF THERE IS MORE DIRECTORY: I.E. IS FCB_BLOCK > FCB_LAST_BLOCK?
5321 ; IF TRUE. GO TO NO_ENT.
5322
5323         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+3)
5324         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
5325         JP      C,NO_ENT                    ; BRANCH IF NO MORE DIRECTORY
5326         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+2)
5327         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
5328         JP      C,NO_ENT                    ; BRANCH IF NO MORE DIRECTORY
5329         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK+1)
5330         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
5331         JP      C,NO_ENT                    ; BRANCH IF NO MORE DIRECTORY
5332         LD      A,(IX+FCB_LAST_BLOCK)
5333         CP      (IX+FCB_BLOCK)
5334         JP      C,NO_ENT                    ; BRANCH IF NO MORE DIRECTORY
5335
5336 RD_TIMEOUT1:
5337         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)           ; GET THE DEVICE NUMBER
5338         LD      HL,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)          ; GET BUFFER ADDRESS
5339         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)            ; GET BLOCK NUMBER
5340         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
5341         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
5342         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
5343         CALL    READ_BLOCK                  ; READ THE NEXT BLOCK
5344         JP      NZ,SCAN_ERROR               ; BRANCH IF ERROR
5345
5346 GET_BUFF_ADDR1:
5347
5348
5349         LD      DE,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)          ; GET BUFFER ADDRESS
5350         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),E          ; PUT IT INTO THE FCB
5351         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),D
5352
5353         LD      B,ENT_PER_BLOCK             ; SET ENTRY COUNT
5354
5355 SCAN2
5356         LD      HL,FILE_COUNT
5357         INC     (HL)
5358
5359         LD      E,(IX+FCB_POINTER)          ; GET POINTER INTO DE
5360         LD      D,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
5361
5362         LD      L,DIR_ATTR                  ; OFFSET (FOR ATTR) INTO DIR ENTRY
5363         LD      H,0                         ; NOT USED
5364         ADD     HL,DE                       ; SET UP HL TO POINT TO IT
5365         BIT     ATTR_HOLE_BIT,(HL)          ; IS IT THE HOLE?
5366         JR      NZ,NO_ENT                   ; YUP. DON'T BOTHER SEARCHING ANY MORE
5367         BIT     ATTR_DEL_BIT,(HL)           ; IS THIS FILE DELETED?
5368         JR      NZ,FILE_DELETED             ; YUP. DON'T EVEN BOTHER CHECK FILE NAMES
5369
5370         LD      HL,(USER_NAME)
5371
5372         LD      A,(FILENAME_CMPS)
5373         OR      A                           ; IS IT ZERO?
5374         JR      Z,FULL_FILENAME             ; YUP. COMPARE THE FULL FILE NAME
5375         CALL    BASECMP                     ; NOPE. COMPARE JUST THE BASE
5376         JR      ERROR_CHECK
5377 FULL_FILENAME:
5378         CALL    STRCMP                      ; COMPARE THE FULL FILE NAME (BASE PLUS EXTENSION)
5379 ERROR_CHECK:
5380         JR      Z,SCAN3                     ; BRANCH IF YES
5381
5382 FILE_DELETED:
5383         LD      A,(IX+FCB_POINTER)          ; ADVANCE POINTER TO NEXT ENTRY
5384         ADD     A,DIR_ENT_LENGTH
5385         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER),A
5386         LD      A,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
5387         ADC     A,0
5388         LD      (IX+FCB_POINTER+1),A
5389
5390         DJNZ    SCAN2                       ; LOOP IF MORE ENTRIES TO CHECK IN THIS BLOCK
5391
5392         JP      SCAN1                       ; ELSE GET NEXT BLOCK
5393
5394 SCAN3
5395         PUSH    DE                          ; GET DIR ENTRY PTR INTO IY
5396         POP     IY
5397
5398         LD      E,(IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+0)
5399         LD      D,(IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+1)
5400         LD      C,(IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+2)
5401         LD      B,(IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+3)
5402
5403         POP     IY                       ; RESTORE REGISTERS
5404         POP     IX
5405         POP     HL
5406         XOR     A                        ; SHOW NO ERROR
5407         RET
5408
5409 SCAN_ERROR
5410         POP     IY                       ; RESTORE REGISTERS
5411         POP     IX
5412         POP     HL
5413         RET
5414
5415 NO_ENT
5416         POP     IY                       ; RESTORE REGISTERS
5417         POP     IX
5418         POP     HL
5419         LD      A,NO_FILE_ERR
5420         OR      A
5421         RET
5422
5423
5424
5425
5426 ;***************************************************************
5427 ;***************************************************************
5428 ;
5429 ;     CHECK_IF_DIRECTORY VERIFIES THE EXISTENCE OF A DIRECTORY
5430 ;             ON THE MEDIA IN THE DEVICE
5431 ;
5432 ;     INPUT:   IY  -  POINTS TO THE VOLUME ENTRY (FIRST ENTRY INTO THE DIRECTORY)
5433 ;
5434 ;     OUTPUT:  CONDITION FLAGS
5435 ;             Z  - DIRECTORY EXISTS
5436 ;             NZ - NO DIRECTORY
5437 ;                  A = ERROR CODE
5438 ;
5439 ;
5440 ;             ALL REGISTERS PRESERVED EXCEPT AF
5441 ;
5442 ;***************************************************************
5443 ;***************************************************************
5444
5445         ;GLB    CHECK_IF_DIRECTORY
5446         ;EXT    DIRECTORY_CHECK
5447
5448 CHECK_IF_DIRECTORY:
5449         PUSH    IY
5450         PUSH    BC
5451         PUSH    HL
5452
5453         LD      HL,DIRECTORY_CHECK        ; 4 UNIQUE BYTES PLACE IN VOLUME ENTRY
5454                                          ; AT CREATION TIME (BYTES DEFINED IN EOS_MKDIR)
5455
5456         LD      B,4                       ; COMPARE 4 BYTES
5457 NEXT_CHECK:
5458         LD      A,(HL)                    ; GET THE FIRST UNIQUE BYTE
5459         CP      (IY+VOL_DIR_CHECK)        ; SAME AS ON TAPE?
5460         JR      NZ,NOT_DIR                ; NOPE
5461
5462         INC     HL                       ; YUP, CHECK NEXT BYTE
5463         INC     IY
5464         DJNZ    NEXT_CHECK
5465
5466         XOR     A                         ; DIRECTORY EXISTS
5467
5468 GET_OUT_HERE:
5469         POP     HL
5470         POP     BC
5471         POP     IY
5472
5473         RET
5474
5475 NOT_DIR:
5476         LD      A,NO_DIR_ERR
5477         JR      GET_OUT_HERE
5478
5480 ;---------------------------------------------------------------
5481 ;
5482 ; STRCMP -- compare two strings (file names).
5483 ;
5484 ; ENTRY PARAMETERS: address of name string in HL; address of
5485 ;                   name within dir entry in DE. NOTE: string
5486 ;                   pointed to by HL must be terminated with ETX.
5487 ;                   Maximum of NAME_LENGTH characters are compared.
5488 ;
5489 ; EXIT PARAMETERS:  Z = 1; A = 0 if match or
5490 ;                   Z = 0; A = error code if no match.
5491 ;
5492 ;---------------------------------------------------------------
5493
5494 BASECMP: EQU     $+1                      ; EXECUTES SCF (37H) - COMPARE ONLY THE BASE
5495                                          ; OF THE FILE NAME
5496 STRCMP:                                  ; "AND" INSTRUCTION CLEARS CARRY - COMPARE BASE &
5497         AND     037H                     ; EXTENSION
5498         PUSH    HL                       ; PRESERVE REG'S
5499         PUSH    DE
5500         PUSH    BC
5501         PUSH    AF                       ; SAVE CARRY FLAGGING EXECUTED ABOVE
5502
5503         LD      B,NAME_LENGTH            ; INIT MAX COUNT
5504 NEXT_CHAR:
5505         LD      A,(HL)                   ; GET CHAR FROM STRING
5506         CP      ETX                      ; HAVE WE REACHED END OF STRING?
5507         JR      Z,EOS                    ; BRANCH IF YES
5508         LD      A,(DE)                   ; GET CHAR FROM DIR ENTRY
5509         CP      (HL)                     ; COMPARE TO STRING
5510         JR      NZ,MATCHLESS             ; BRANCH IF DIFFERENT
5511         INC     HL                       ; ELSE INC. POINTERS
5512         INC     DE
5513         DJNZ    NEXT_CHAR                ; LOOP IF MORE CHARS TO LOOK AT
5514         POP     BC                       ; POP AF INTO BC
5515         JR      MATCH                    ; ALL 12 MATCH IF WE GET HERE!
5516 MATCHLESS:
5517         POP     AF                       ; COMPARE BASE OR BASE&EXTENSION
5518         JR      NC,NO_MATCH              ; NEEDED TO COMPARE THE WHOLE FILE NAME
5519                                          ; ONLY COMPARE THE BASE
5520         INC     HL                       ; POINT TO NEXT CHAR OF NAME STRING
5521         INC     DE                       ; AND DIR ENTRY NAME
5522         LD      A,(HL)
5523         CP      ETX                      ; IS IT ETX?
5524         JR      NZ,NO_MATCH              ; NOPE, DOES NOT MATCH
5525         JR      AROUND_POP
5526
5527 EOS:
5528         POP     BC                       ; AF INTO BC TO PRESERVE AF
5529 AROUND_POP:
5530         LD      A,(DE)                   ; LOOK AT CHAR IN DIR ENTRY
5531                                          ; DLS(8/29/83) CHANGED SPACE TO ETX.
5532         CP      ETX                      ; END OF FILE NAME?
5533         JR      NZ,NO_MATCH              ; BRANCH IF NOT
5534 MATCH:
5535         POP     BC                       ; RESTORE REG'S
5536         POP     DE
5537         POP     HL
5538         XOR     A                        ; SHOW THE MATCH
5539         RET
5540 NO_MATCH
5541         POP     BC                       ; RESTORE REG'S
5542         POP     DE
5543         POP     HL
5544         LD      A,MATCH_ERR
5545         OR      A
5546         RET                              ; RETURN WITH NZ CONDITION
5547
5548
5549 ;***************************************************************
5550 ;
5551 ;
5552 ; THIS ROUTINE CHECKS TO SEE IF THE FILENAME (POINTED TO BY HL)
5553 ; IS ALREADY OPEN AND EXIST IN THE FCB
5554 ;
5555 ;             INPUT:   HL        POINTER TO FILENAME (ETX TERMINATED)
5556 ;
5557 ;             OUTPUT:  CONDITION FLAGS
5558 ;                      NZ        NOT IN FCB (NOT OPENED)
5559 ;                                A = ERROR CODE
5560 ;                                HL = ORIGINAL POINTER TO FILENAME
5561 ;                      Z         IN FCB
5562 ;                                A = FILE NUMBER
5563 ;                                B = MODE
5564 ;                                HL = POINTER TO START OF FCB
5565 ;
5566 ;***************************************************************
5567 ;
5568 ; GET_OUT                  U_GET_OUT
5569 ; NEXT_FCB CHANGED TO U_NEXT_FCB TO PREVENT CONFLICT WITH
5570 ; IDENTICAL LABEL ELSEWHERE
5571
5572         ;GLB    __CHECK_FCB
5573         ;EXT    FILE_NUMBR
5574
5575 __CHECK_FCB:
5576         PUSH    IX
5577         PUSH    DE
5578 ;       PUSH    BC
5579         PUSH    HL
5580
5581         LD      HL,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)
5582         XOR     A                         ; ZERO OUT A
5583 U_NEXT_FCB:
5584         INC     A                         ; CONTAINS THE FILE NUMBER
5585         LD      (FILE_NUMBR),A            ; (THE POSITION ON THE TAPE)
5586
5587         LD      DE,FCB_LENGTH             ; NUMBER OF BYTES IN EACH FCB
5588         ADD     HL,DE                     ; POINT TO NEXT FCB ENTRY
5589
5590         PUSH    HL
5591         POP     IX                       ; IX POINTS TO START OF FCB
5592
5593         LD      A,(IX+FCB_MODE)           ; GET MODE
5594         AND     MODE_MODE
5595         CP      0                         ; IT IS UNUSED?
5596
5597         JR      Z,END_FCB                 ; YUP, LOOK AT NEXT FCB
5598         EX      DE,HL                     ; DE = POINTER TO FCB NAME STRING
5599         POP     HL
5600         PUSH    HL                       ; HL = POINTER TO FILENAME TO SEARCH FOR
5601
5602
5603         PUSH    DE                       ; DE = POINTER TO FCB FILE NAME
5604
5605         LD      BC,NAME_LENGTH           ; NO. OF CHARACTERS IN NAME
5606                                          ; HL = FILE NAME STRING
5607 NEXT_CHAR1:
5608         LD      A,(DE)                   ; DE = FCB FILE NAME
5609         CP      ETX                      ; IS IT ETX?
5610
5611         JR      Z,ITS_ETX1               ; YUP
5612
5613         CPI                              ; DO CHARS MATCH ((HL) = A)?
5614
5615         INC     DE
5616         JR      Z,NEXT_CHAR1             ; YUP,CHECK NEXT CHAR OF FILE NAME
5617
5618         POP     HL                       ; pointer to FCB
5619 END_FCB:
5620         LD      A,(FILE_NUMBR)           ; ARE WE AT 2ND FILE(FCB)?
5621         CP      NUM_FCBS-1               ; NO. OF FCB'S - SYSTEM FCB
5622
5623         JR      NZ,U_NEXT_FCB            ; NOPE, NOT YET,
5624
5625 NO_MATCH1:
5626         POP     HL                       ; HL = ORIGINAL POINTER TO FILE NAME
5627
5628         LD      A,NO_FILE_ERR            ; NO MATCH FOUND
5629         OR      A
5630         JR      GET_OUT_ERR
5631
5632 ITS_ETX1:
5633         LD      A,(HL)                   ; GET ASCII CHAR
5634         CP      ETX                      ; IS IT ETX?
5635         POP     HL                       ; *pointer to FCB
5636         JR      NZ,NO_MATCH1             ; NOPE
5637
5638 ;       PUSH    HL                       ; copy pointer to FCB so can index to mode
5639 ;       POP     IX
5640
5641         LD      A,(IX+FCB_MODE)          ; GET MODE THIS FILE WAS ORIGINALLY
5642         AND     MODE_MODE                ; OPENED FOR AND MASK OFF FLAG BITS
5643         LD      B,A                       ; PASS IT BACK IN B
5644
5645         XOR     A                        ; INDICATE Z CONDITION
5646         LD      A,(FILE_NUMBR)           ; GET FILE NUMBER
5647
5648 U_GET_OUT:
5649         POP     DE                       ; *Clear off pointer to name
5650 ;       POP     BC
5651 GET_OUT_ERR:
5652         POP     DE
5653         POP     IX
5654
5655         RET
5656
5658
5659 ;---------------------------------------------------------------
5660 ;
5661 ; MODE_CHECK: sees if an attribute byte matches a permission request.
5662 ;
5663 ; ENTRY PARAMETERS: IX- FCB POINTER
5664 ;                   HL- DIR ENTRY
5665 ; EXIT PARAMETERS:   Z=1=OK
5666 ;                    Z=0=NO WAY
5667 ;                    A=TRASH
5668 ;                    IX AND HL PRESERVED
5669 ; DLS(8/30/83)
5670 ;---------------------------------------------------------------
5671
5672 ; OK CHANGED TO U_OK TO PREVENT CONFLICT WITH IDENTICAL
5673 ;LABEL ELSEWHERE
5674
5675 ;MODE_CHECK:
5676 MODE_CHECK:
5677
5678         PUSH    IY                       ; SAVE REG IY
5679         PUSH    HL                       ; SAVE DIR ENTRY
5680         LD      A,(IX+FCB_MODE)          ; GET THE MODE THE USER PASSED IN
5681 ;
5682 ; RANGE CHECK THIS REQUEST
5683 ;
5684         AND     MODE_MODE                ; AND OFF TEST BITS
5685         CP      0                         ; MAKE SURE HE'S IN RANGE
5686         JR      Z,RANGE_NONO
5687         CP      MODE_MAX+1
5688         JR      NC,RANGE_NONO
5689 ;
5690         LD      HL,ATTRIB_TBL            ; INDEX INTO THE MASK TABLE
5691         ADD     A,L
5692         LD      L,A
5693         JR      NC,ADJUST
5694         INC     H
5695 ADJUST
5696         POP     IY                       ; GET THE DIR ENTRY POINTER INTO IY
5697         PUSH    IY
5698         LD      A,(IY+DIR_ATTR)          ; GRAB HIS ATTRIBUTE BYTE
5699         AND     (HL)                     ; COMPARE IT TO THE USER'S REQUEST
5700         JR      NZ,PROTECT_FAULT
5701 U_OK
5702         POP     HL
5703         POP     IY
5704         XOR     A                        ; INDICATE ALL IS WELL
5705         RET
5706 PROTECT_FAULT
5707         LD      A,PRT_ERR
5708         JR      PROTECT
5709 RANGE_NONO
5710         LD      A,RANGE_ERR
5711 PROTECT
5712         POP     HL
5713         POP     IY
5714         OR      A                        ; INDICATE ERROR
5715         RET
5716 ATTRIB_TBL     EQU     $-1
5717         DEFB    ATTR_READ_PROT
5718         DEFB    ATTR_WRITE_PROT
5719         DEFB    ATTR_PERMANENT
5720         DEFB    ATTR_EXECUTE
5721
5722
5724
5725 ;***************************************************************
5726 ;******************** EOS_RENAME_FILE ***************************
5727 ;
5728 ; ON ENTRY:   A  = DEVICE NUMBER (8=TAPE1)
5729 ;             DE = POINTER TO OLD FILENAME
5730 ;             HL = POINTER TO NEW FILENAME
5731 ;
5732 ; ON EXIT:    Z = 1, FILE RENAMED.  A = 0
5733 ;             Z = 0, FILE NOT RENAMED. A = ERROR CODE
5734 ;
5735
5736         ;GLB    __RENAME_FILE
5737
5738 __RENAME_FILE:
5739         PUSH    BC
5740         PUSH    AF                       ; AF = DEVICE ID
5741         PUSH    DE                       ; DE = POINTER TO OLD FILENAME
5742         PUSH    HL                       ; HL = POINTER TO NEW FILENAME
5743         EX      DE,HL                    ; FIRST CHECK IF A FILE EXIST W/NEW FILE NAME
5744         LD      HL,QUERY_BUFFER
5745         CALL    __QUERY_FILE
5746         JR      Z,NOT_RENAMED            ; YUP, THE NEW FILE NAME ALREADY EXISTS
5747         POP     HL
5748         POP     DE
5749         POP     AF
5750         PUSH    AF
5751         PUSH    DE
5752         PUSH    HL
5753
5754         LD      HL,QUERY_BUFFER
5755         CALL    __QUERY_FILE             ; NOW CHECK IF OLD FILE EXISTS
5756         JR      NZ,NOT_RENAMED           ; NOPE
5757         LD      DE,QUERY_BUFFER          ; DE = DESTINATION
5758         POP     HL                       ; HL = PTR TO NEW FILENAME
5759         PUSH    HL
5760         LD      BC,12                    ; NUMBER OF BYTES TO REPLACE
5761         LDIR
5762         POP     HL
5763         POP     DE                       ; DE = POINTER TO OLD FILENAME
5764         POP     AF                       ; A = DEVICE ID
5765         PUSH    AF
5766         PUSH    DE                       ; RESTORE STACK
5767         PUSH    HL
5768         LD      HL,QUERY_BUFFER
5769         CALL    __SET_FILE               ; CHANGE FILENAME
5770         JR      NZ,NOT_RENAMED
5771         XOR     A                        ; GOOD RETURN
5772         POP     HL
5773         POP     DE
5774         POP     BC                       ; REALLY AF BUT NEED TO PRESERVE FLAGS FOR RET
5775         POP     BC
5776         RET
5777 NOT_RENAMED:
5778         LD      A,RENAME_ERR             ; ERROR RETURN
5779         OR      A
5780         POP     HL
5781         POP     DE
5782         POP     BC                       ; REALLY AF BUT NEED TO PRESERVE ERR MSG.
5783         POP     BC
5784         RET
5785
5787
5788 ;
5789 ;***************************************************************
5790 ;******************** EOS_DELETE_FILE ***************************
5791 ;
5792 ; ON ENTRY:   A  = DEVICE NUMBER (8=TAPE1)
5793 ;             HL = POINTER TO FILENAME
5794 ;
5795 ; ON EXIT:    Z = 1, FILE DELETED. A = 0
5796 ;             Z = 0, FILE NOT DELETED. A = ERROR CODE
5797 ;
5798
5799         ;GLB    __DELETE_FILE
5800
5801 __DELETE_FILE:
5802         PUSH    DE
5803         PUSH    HL                       ; HL = POINTER TO FILENAME
5804         PUSH    AF                       ; A = DEVICE ID
5805         EX      DE,HL                    ; DE = POINTER TO FILENAME
5806         LD      HL,QUERY_BUFFER          ; HL = POINTER TO BUFFER
5807         CALL    __QUERY_FILE
5808         JR      NZ,OTHER_ERR
5809         LD      A,(QUERY_BUFFER+FCB_ATTR) ; GET ATTRIBUTE BYTE
5810         BIT     7,A                       ; see if permanent protected
5811         JR      NZ,NOT_DELETED
5812         OR      ATTR_DELETED              ; SET "DELETED" BIT
5813         LD      (QUERY_BUFFER+FCB_ATTR),A
5814         POP     AF                       ; DEVICE ID
5815         POP     DE                       ; GET FILENAME POINTER
5816         PUSH    DE
5817         PUSH    AF
5818         CALL    __SET_FILE
5819         JR      NZ,OTHER_ERR
5820         XOR     A                        ; GOOD RETURN
5821         POP     HL                       ; REALLY AF BUT PRESERVE NEW AF
5822         POP     HL
5823         POP     DE
5824         RET
5825 NOT_DELETED:
5826         LD      A,DELETE_ERR             ; ERROR RETURN
5827         OR      A
5828 OTHER_ERR:
5829         POP     HL                       ; REALLY AF BUT PRESERVE ERR CONDITION
5830         POP     HL
5831         POP     DE
5832         RET
5833
5834
5836 ;***************************************************************
5837 ;***************************************************************
5838 *
5839 *
5840 *     READ_BLOCK:
5841 *             1)  READS 1 BLOCK OFF OF SPECIFIED DEVICE
5842 *             2)  REQUESTS FOR THE STATUS
5843 *             3)  AND SENDS ANOTHER READ TO THE DEVICE
5844 *
5845 *     INPUT:  SAME AS FOR __RD_1_BLOCK
5846 *             A       DEVICE ID
5847 *                     LOW NIBBLE - DEVICE ADDRESS
5848 *                     HI NIBBLE  - SECONDARY DEVICE ID
5849 *
5850 *             HL      DESTINATION IN RAM
5851 *
5852 *             REGISTER PAIR  BC DE
5853 *                            SECTOR NUMBER ON DEVICE
5854 *
5855 *
5856 *     OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
5857 *                     Z:  NO ERRORS
5858 *                    NZ:  ERROR OCCURED
5859 *                         A - ERROR CODE
5860 *
5861 *     ALL REGISTERS ARE PRESERVED EXCEPT FOR AF
5862 *
5863 ;***************************************************************
5864 ;***************************************************************
5865
5866 __READ_BLOCK:
5867 READ_BLOCK:
5868         PUSH    BC
5869         PUSH    DE
5870         PUSH    HL
5871         PUSH    IX
5872         PUSH    IY
5873         PUSH    AF
5874
5875         LD      A,ERROR_RETRY            ; RETRY COUNT FOR ERRORS
5876         LD      (RETRY_COUNT),A
5877 READ_LOOP:
5878         POP     AF                       ; GET DEVICE ID BACK IN A
5879         PUSH    AF
5880
5881         CALL    __RD_1_BLOCK
5882
5883         JR      Z,NO_READ_ERRORS         ; READ WENT OK
5884
5885         CP      TIMEOUT                  ; WAS IT A TIMEOUT?
5886
5887         JR      Z,NO_READ_ERRORS         ; YUP,
5888
5889         PUSH    HL
5890         LD      HL,RETRY_COUNT
5891         DEC     (HL)                     ; DEC THE NO OF RETRY'S LEFT
5892         POP     HL
5893         JR      Z,READ_ERROR             ; NO RETRY'S LEFT
5894         JR      READ_LOOP                ; TRY AGAIN
5895
5896 NO_READ_ERRORS:
5897         LD      B,ERROR_RETRY            ; NUMBER OF TIMES TO RETRY
5898
5899 RETRY_FOR_ERRORS:
5900         POP     AF                       ; RESTORE DEVICE ID
5901         PUSH    AF
5902
5903         CALL    __REQUEST_STATUS         ; GET STATUS
5904
5905         JR      Z,READ_COMMAND_COMPLETED ; READ ALL DONE
5906
5907         CP      TIMEOUT                  ; WAS IT A TIMEOUT?
5908         JR      Z,RETRY_FOR_ERROR        ; NOT A REAL ERROR, DON'T NEED TO DEC COUNTER
5909
5910         DJNZ    RETRY_FOR_ERROR          ; IT'S A REAL ERROR, TRY AGAIN
5911
5912         JR      READ_ERROR               ; CAN'T GET STATUS, GET OUT
5913
5914 READ_COMMAND_COMPLETED:
5915         POP     AF                       ; RESTORE DEVICE ID
5916         PUSH    AF
5917
5918         CALL    GET_STATUS_FLAG          ; GET THE STATUS FOR THE PROPER
5919                                          ; DEVICE IN THE A REGISTER
5920
5921         CP      0                         ; ANY ERRORS WITH DRIVE?
5922
5923         JR      NZ,READ_ERROR            ; YUP
5924
5925         LD      A,ERROR_RETRY
5926         LD      (RETRY_COUNT),A
5927
5928         POP     AF
5929         POP     IY
5930         POP     IX
5931         POP     HL
5932         POP     DE
5933         POP     BC
5934         PUSH    BC
5935 _2ND_READ:
5936         PUSH    AF
5937
5938         CALL    __RD_1_BLOCK             ; SEND ANOTHER READ COMMAND
5939         JR      Z,NO_ERRORS
5940         PUSH    HL
5941         LD      HL,RETRY_COUNT
5942         DEC     (HL)                     ; THE NO OF RETRY'S
5943         POP     HL
5944         JR      Z,_2ND_READ_ERROR         ; NO RETRY'S LEFT
5945         POP     AF
5946
5947         JR      _2ND_READ                ; GO TRY AGAIN
5948
5950         OR      A                        ; NZ CONDITION
5951 NO_ERRORS:
5952         POP     BC
5953         POP     BC
5954
5955         RET
5956
5957 READ_ERROR:
5958         POP     IY                       ; POP AF INTO IY TO PRESERVE FLAGS
5959         POP     IY
5960         POP     IX
5961         POP     HL
5962         POP     DE
5963         POP     BC
5964
5965         LD      A,DEVICE_DEPD_ERR        ; INDICATE ERROR, LET APPL. HANDLE IT
5966
5967         RET
5968
5969
5971
5972 ;***************************************************************
5973 ;***************************************************************
5974 *
5975 *
5976 *     WRITE_BLOCK:
5977 *             1)  WRITES A BLOCK OUT TO TAPE
5978 *             2)  AND CHECKS STATUS AFTER WRITE IS COMPLETED
5979 *
5980 *     INPUT:  SAME AS FOR __WR_1_BLOCK
5981 *
5982 *             A       DEVICE ID
5983 *                     LOW NIBBLE - DEVICE ADDRESS
5984 *                     HI NIBBLE  - SECONDARY DEVICE ID
5985 *
5986 *             HL      SOURCE ADDRESS OF BUFFER
5987 *
5988 *             REGISTER PAIR  BC DE
5989 *                            SECTOR NUMBER ON DEVICE
5990 *
5991 *
5992 *     OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
5993 *                     Z:  NO ERRORS
5994 *                    NZ:  ERRORS OCCURED
5995 *                         A - ERROR CODE
5996 *
5997 *     ALL REGISTERS PRESERVED EXCEPT FOR AF
5998 *
5999 ;***************************************************************
6000 ;***************************************************************
6001
6002
6003
6004 __WRITE_BLOCK:
6005 WRITE_BLOCK:
6006         PUSH    BC
6007         PUSH    IY
6008         PUSH    AF
6009
6010         LD      A,ERROR_RETRY
6011         LD      (RETRY_COUNT),A
6012 TRY_WRITE_AGAIN:
6013         POP     AF
6014         PUSH    AF
6015
6016         CALL    __WR_1_BLOCK
6017
6018         JR      Z,NO_WRITE_ERRORS         ; NO ERRORS
6019
6020         CP      TIMEOUT
6021
6022         JR      Z,NO_WRITE_ERRORS         ; ERROR OCCURED IN TRANSMISSION
6023
6024         PUSH    HL
6025         LD      HL,RETRY_COUNT
6026         DEC     (HL)
6027         POP     HL
6028         JR      Z,WRITE_ERROR
6029
6030         JR      TRY_WRITE_AGAIN
6031
6032 NO_WRITE_ERRORS:
6033
6034         LD      B,ERROR_RETRY            ; MAX NO OF RETRIES
6035
6036 RETRY_WRITE:
6037         POP     AF                       ; RESTORE DEVICE ID
6038         PUSH    AF
6039
6040         CALL    __REQUEST_STATUS
6041
6042         JR      Z,WRITE_COMMAND_COMPLETE ; STATUS BACK
6043
6044         CP      TIMEOUT                  ; CHECK FOR TIMEOUT
6045
6046         JR      Z,RETRY_WRITE            ; TIMEOUT. NOT A 'REAL' ERROR
6047
6048         DJNZ    RETRY_WRITE              ; REAL ERROR, TRY AGAIN
6049
6050         JR      WRITE_ERROR              ; DONE RETRYING, IT'S AN ERROR
6051
6052 WRITE_COMMAND_COMPLETE:
6053         POP     AF                       ; RESTORE DEVICE ID
6054         PUSH    AF
6055
6056         CALL    GET_STATUS_FLAG          ; GET STATUS FOR SPECIFIED DEVICE
6057
6058         CP      0                         ; WRITE OK?
6059         JR      Z,WRITE_OK                ; YUP
6060
6061 WRITE_ERROR:
6062         LD      A,DEVICE_DEPD_ERR
6063
6064 WRITE_OK:
6065         POP     BC                       ; REALLY AF, BUT PRESERVE CURRENT FLAGS
6066         POP     IY
6067         POP     BC
6068
6069
6070         RET
6071
6072
6074
6075 GET_STATUS_FLAG:
6076
6077         SRL     A                        ; GET SECONDARY DEVICE ID (HI NIBBLE)
6078         SRL     A
6079         SRL     A
6080         SRL     A
6081
6082         CP      0                         ; WAS IT UNIT 0?
6083
6084         LD      A,(IY+D_STATUS_FLAGS)    ; BY THE WAY, GET THE STATUS
6085
6086         JR      NZ,UNIT1                 ; NOPE, UNIT 1
6087
6088         AND     0FH                      ; MASK OUT STATUS OF UNIT 1
6089
6090         JR      CHECK_STATUS
6091
6092 UNIT1:
6093         SRL     A
6094         SRL     A
6095         SRL     A
6096         SRL     A
6097
6098 CHECK_STATUS:                            ; RETURN WITH THE STATUS NIBBLE IN A
6099
6100         RET
6101
6103 ;***************************************************************
6104 ;***************************************************************
6105 *
6106 *
6107 *     TRIM_FILE RELEASES THE UNUSED BLOCKS IN THE SPECIFIED FILE
6108 *
6109 * NOTE:  IF FILE BEING TRIMMED IS THE LAST FILE, THE UNUSED BLOCKS ARE
6110 *        ALLOCATED AS PART OF THE REMAINING AVAILABLE BLOCKS (RETURNED
6111 *        TO THE HOLE).
6112 *
6113 *     INPUT:  A       DEVICE ID
6114 *             DE      POINTER TO FILE NAME STRING
6115 *
6116 *     OUTPUT:
6117 *             CONDITION FLAGS
6118 *             Z:      NO ERRORS
6119 *             NZ:     ERRORS
6120 *                     A - ERROR CODE
6121 *
6122 ;***************************************************************
6123 ;***************************************************************
6124
6125         ;GLB    __TRIM_FILE
6126
6127         ;EXT    FCB_BUFFER
6128         ;EXT    NEW_HOLE_SIZE
6129         ;EXT    HOLE_FILE_NAME
6130
6131 __TRIM_FILE:
6132
6133         PUSH    HL
6134         PUSH    IX
6135         PUSH    IY
6136         PUSH    DE
6137         PUSH    AF
6138
6139         LD      HL,FCB_BUFFER
6140
6141         CALL    __QUERY_FILE             ; FIND FILE IN DIRECTORY
6142
6143         JP      NZ,ERROR                 ; HAD TROUBLE FINDING IT
6144
6145         LD      IX,FCB_BUFFER            ; POINT TO THE BUFFER
6146
6147         LD      E,(IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH)
6148         LD      D,(IX+FCB_USED_LENGTH+1) ; GET THE NO OF USED BLOCKS
6149
6150         LD      L,(IX+FCB_MAX_LENGTH)
6151         LD      H,(IX+FCB_MAX_LENGTH+1)  ; GET THE NO ALLOCATED
6152
6153         OR      A
6154         SBC     HL,DE                    ; FIND OUT HOW MANY EMPTY
6155
6156         LD      A,H
6157         OR      L
6158
6159         JP      Z,NO_TRIM                ; ALL USED
6160
6161         LD      (NEW_HOLE_SIZE),HL       ; SAVE THE NUMBER OF EMPTY BLOCKS
6162         LD      (IX+FCB_MAX_LENGTH),E    ; ALLOCATED = USED,  NOW
6163         LD      (IX+FCB_MAX_LENGTH+1),D
6164
6165         LD      A,(FILE_COUNT)           ; FILE ENTRY POSITION IN DIRECTORY
6166         LD      B,ENT_PER_BLOCK
6167 TRY_AGAIN:
6168         SUB     B                         ; IS IT THE LAST ENTRY IN THIS BLOCK
6169         JR      Z,LAST_ENTRY              ; YUP
6170         JR      NC,TRY_AGAIN             ; NOPE, MUST BE IN NEXT BLOCK
6171
6172 NOT_LAST_ENTRY:
6173         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)        ; GET START OF FCB'S
6174         LD      E,(IX+FCB_POINTER)        ; GET ADDRESS OF THE MODIFIED FCB
6175         LD      D,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
6176         PUSH    DE
6177
6178         LD      BC,DIR_ENT_LENGTH        ; OFFSET TO NEXT DIR ENTRY'S ATTR BYTE
6179         PUSH    BC
6180
6181         LD      HL,FCB_BUFFER
6182         LDIR
6183
6184         POP     DE
6185         POP     HL
6186
6187         ADD     HL,DE                    ; POINT TO ATTR BYTE
6188
6189         PUSH    HL
6190         POP     IY
6191
6192         BIT     ATTR_HOLE_BIT,(IY+DIR_ATTR) ; IS IT A HOLE?
6193         JR      Z,NOT_HOLE               ; NOPE
6194
6195         JR      ITS_HOLE
6196
6197
6198 LAST_ENTRY:
6199         LD      A,(FILE_COUNT)
6200         LD      (MOD_FILE_COUNT),A        ; SAVE IT
6201
6202         POP     AF
6203         POP     DE
6204
6205         PUSH    DE
6206         PUSH    AF
6207
6208         LD      HL,FCB_BUFFER
6209
6210         CALL    __SET_FILE               ; PUT CHANGED DIRECTORY AWAY
6211
6212         JR      NZ,ERROR
6213
6214         POP     AF
6215         POP     DE
6216
6217         LD      DE,HOLE_FILE_NAME        ; NAME OF HOLE
6218
6219         PUSH    DE
6220         PUSH    AF                       ; SAVE DEVICE ID
6221
6222         LD      HL,FCB_BUFFER
6223
6224         CALL    __QUERY_FILE
6225         LD      IX,(FCB_HEAD_ADDR)
6226         LD      L,(IX+FCB_POINTER)       ; GET ADDRESS OF HOLE IN DIR BLOCK
6227         LD      H,(IX+FCB_POINTER+1)
6228
6229         PUSH    HL
6230         POP     IY
6231
6232         BIT     ATTR_HOLE_BIT,(IY+DIR_ATTR) ; WAS IT A HOLE
6233         JR      Z,ERROR
6234
6235         LD      HL,FILE_COUNT
6236         LD      A,(MOD_FILE_COUNT)
6237
6238         INC     A
6239
6240         CP      (HL)
6241         JR      NZ,NO_TRIM
6242
6243
6244 ITS_HOLE:
6245         LD      L,(IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH)    ; GET CURRENT HOLE SIZE
6246         LD      H,(IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH+1)
6247
6248         LD      DE,(NEW_HOLE_SIZE)        ; BLOCKS TO BE RETURNED TO HOLE
6249         ADD     HL,DE                    ; NEW HOLE SIZE
6250
6251         LD      (IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH),L
6252         LD      (IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH+1),H
6253
6254         LD      L,(IY+DIR_START_BLOCK)
6255         LD      H,(IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+1)
6256
6257         OR      A
6258         SBC     HL,DE
6259
6260         LD      (IY+DIR_START_BLOCK),L
6261         LD      (IY+DIR_START_BLOCK+1),H
6262
6263 NOT_HOLE:
6264
6265         LD      A,(IX+FCB_DEVICE)         ; DEVICE NUMBER
6266         LD      HL,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)        ; GET ADDR OF MY BUFFER
6267         LD      E,(IX+FCB_BLOCK)          ; GET BLOCK ADDRESS IN BCDE
6268         LD      D,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+1)
6269         LD      C,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+2)
6270         LD      B,(IX+FCB_BLOCK+3)
6271
6272         CALL    WRITE_BLOCK              ; RE-WRITE THE DIRECTORY BLOCK
6273         JR      NZ,ERROR
6274
6275 NO_TRIM:
6276         POP     DE                       ; REALLY WAS AF
6277         POP     DE
6278         POP     IY
6279         POP     IX
6280         POP     HL
6281
6282         XOR     A
6283
6284         RET
6285
6286 ERROR:
6287         OR      A
6288         POP     DE                       ; REALLY AF
6289         POP     DE
6290         POP     IY
6291         POP     IX
6292         POP     HL
6293
6294         RET
6295
6296
6298 ;***************************************************************
6299 *
6300 *     __INIT_TP_DIR INITIALIZES THE TAPE DIRECTORY
6301 *
6302 *     INPUT:  A       DEVICE ID
6303 *                     LOW NIBBLE - DEVICE ADDRESS
6304 *                     HI NIBBLE  - SECONDARY DEVICE ID
6305 *
6306 *             C       NUMBER OF SECTORS IN DIRECTORY
6307 *             DE      NUMBER OF SECTORS ON DEVICE
6308 *
6309 *             HL      POINTER TO ASCII ETX TERMINATED STRING
6310 *                     ( VOLUME ID) (SHOULD BE BLANK-PADDED IF
6311 *                     LESS THAN 12 CHARACTERS)
6312 *     OUTPUT:
6313 *             CONDITION FLAGS
6314 *             Z       NO ERROR
6315 *             NZ      ERROR
6316 *                     A = ERROR CODE
6317 *
6318 ;***************************************************************
6319
6320         ;GLB    __INIT_TAPE_DIR
6321         ;GLB    HOLE_FILE_NAME
6322         ;GLB    DIRECTORY_CHECK
6323
6324         ;EXT    SECTOR_NO
6325         ;EXT    DEVICE_ID
6326         ;EXT    FCB_DATA_ADDR
6327         ;EXT    SECTORS_TO_INIT
6328         ;EXT    WRITE_BLOCK
6329
6330 __INIT_TAPE_DIR:
6331
6332         PUSH    IY
6333         PUSH    BC
6334         PUSH    DE
6335         PUSH    HL
6336
6337         LD      (DEVICE_ID),A             ; SAVE THE DEVICE ID
6338
6339         LD      A,C
6340         LD      (SECTORS_TO_INIT),A       ; NO OF SECTORS TO INITIALIZE
6341
6342         PUSH    HL
6343
6344         CALL    ZERO_OUT_BUFFER           ; ZERO OUT THE 1K BUFFER
6345
6346         LD      HL,INITIAL_DIRECTORY      ; POINTER TO INITIAL SETUP
6347         LD      DE,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)        ; ADDRESS OF 1K BUFFER
6348         LD      BC,DIR_ENT_LENGTH+3+VOL_DES_LENGTH ; # BYTES TO MOVE INTO BUFFER
6349         LDIR
6350
6351         POP     HL                       ; GET ADDR OF ETX TERMINATED ASCII STRING
6352
6353         LD      DE,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)        ; GET ADDRESS OF 1K BUFFER
6354         LD      B,12                      ; MAX OF 12 BYTES IN STRING
6355
6356 LOAD_DIR:
6357         LD      A,(HL)                   ; GET ASCII CHAR OF VOLUME ID
6358         CP      ETX                      ; IS IT ETX?
6359
6360         JR      Z,ITS_AN_ETX             ; YUP
6361
6362         LD      (DE),A                   ; NOPE,PUT IN THE 1K BUFFER
6363
6364         INC     HL
6365         INC     DE
6366
6367         DJNZ    LOAD_DIR
6368
6369         LD      A,ETX
6370         DEC     DE
6371
6372 ITS_AN_ETX:
6373         LD      (DE),A                   ; ETX TERMINATED DIRECTORY NAME
6374         LD      IY,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)       ; POINTER TO 1K BUFFER
6375
6376         LD      A,(SECTORS_TO_INIT)      ; GET THE NO OF SECTORS TO INITIALIZE
6377         OR      080H                     ; SET HI BIT
6378         LD      (IY+VOL_DIRSIZE),A       ; SAVE IN FIRST BYTE OF BUFFER
6379
6380         POP     HL
6381         POP     DE
6382         POP     BC
6383
6384         PUSH    BC
6385         PUSH    DE
6386         PUSH    HL
6387
6388         LD      (IY+VOL_SIZE+0),E
6389         LD      (IY+VOL_SIZE+1),D
6390
6391         PUSH    DE                       ; GET TO FIRST DIR ENTRY
6392         LD      DE,VOL_DES_LENGTH+DIR_ENT_LENGTH+2 ; POINT TO FIRST FILE ENTRY
6393         ADD     IY,DE
6394         POP     DE
6395
6396         LD      (IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH-DIR_ENT_LENGTH),C ; BLOCKS ALLOCATED FOR DIRECTORY
6397         LD      (IY+DIR_USED_LENGTH-DIR_ENT_LENGTH),C ; MAKE USED BLOCKS AS LARGE AS POSSIBLE
6398
6399         INC     C                        ; FIRST "FILE" STARTS AFTER
6400         LD      (IY+DIR_START_BLOCK),C   ; LAST SECTOR OF DIR
6401
6402         LD      B,0                      ; WANT BC = # SECTORS USED
6403                                          ; (# DIR SECT + 1 TO COVER
6404                                          ; SECT 0)
6405
6406         EX      DE,HL                    ; HL = # SECTORS ON DEVICE
6407         OR      A                        ; CLEAR CARRY
6408         SBC     HL,BC                    ; HL = # SECTORS LEFT FOR FILES ?????
6409
6410         LD      (IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH+0),L
6411         LD      (IY+DIR_MAX_LENGTH+1),H
6412
6413         LD      A,1                      ; FIRST SECTOR
6414         LD      (SECTOR_NO),A
6415
6416         CALL    WRITE_1_BLOCK            ; WRITE BLOCK TO TAPE
6417
6418         JR      NZ,WE_GOT_ERRORS         ; CAME BACK WITH ERROR
6419
6420         LD      A,(SECTORS_TO_INIT)      ; GET NO OF SECTORS TO INITIALIZE
6421
6422         LD      B,A
6423         DEC     B                        ; ONE LESS BECAUSE ONE WAS JUST INITIALIZED
6424
6425         JR      Z,NO_SECTORS_TO_INIT     ; WAS ONLY ONE TO INIT
6426
6427         CALL    ZERO_OUT_BUFFER          ; CLEAR 1K BUFFER
6428
6429 SECT_INIT_LOOP:
6430         LD      HL,SECTOR_NO             ; SECTOR NO.
6431         INC     (HL)                     ; NEXT SECTOR
6432
6433         CALL    WRITE_1_BLOCK            ; WRITE BLOCK OF ZEROS OUT
6434         JR      NZ,WE_GOT_ERRORS         ; GOT A ERROR IN WRITE
6435
6436         DJNZ    SECT_INIT_LOOP
6437
6438 NO_SECTORS_TO_INIT:
6439
6440         XOR     A                        ; ZERO CONDITION
6441
6442 WE_GOT_ERRORS:
6443
6444         POP     HL
6445         POP     DE
6446         POP     BC
6447         POP     IY
6448
6449         RET
6450
6451
6453
6454 ZERO_OUT_BUFFER:
6455
6456         PUSH    BC
6457         PUSH    DE
6458         PUSH    HL
6459
6460         LD      HL,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)       ; POINTER TO 1K BUFFER
6461         LD      DE,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)
6462         INC     DE
6463         LD      BC,1023
6464
6465         LD      (HL),0                   ; ZERO IT OUT
6466
6467         LDIR
6468
6469         POP     HL
6470         POP     DE
6471         POP     BC
6472
6473         RET
6474
6475
6476 WRITE_1_BLOCK:
6477         PUSH    BC
6478
6479         LD      HL,(FCB_DATA_ADDR)       ; ADDRESS OF 1K BUFFER
6480         LD      A,(SECTOR_NO)            ; GET SECTOR NUMBER
6481         LD      E,A
6482         LD      D,0
6483         LD      BC,0
6484
6485         LD      A,(DEVICE_ID)
6486
6487         CALL    WRITE_BLOCK              ; WRITE IT OUT
6488
6489         POP     BC
6490
6491         RET
6492
6493
6494 INITIAL_DIRECTORY:
6495         DEFB    '
6496         DEFB    10000000B
6497 DIRECTORY_CHECK:
6498         DEFB    055H,0AAH,000H,0FFH
6499         DEFB    0,0
6500         DEFB    0,0
6501         DEFB    0,0
6502         DEFB    0,0,0
6503
6504         DEFB    'BOOT',ETX,'
6505         DEFB    ATTR_PERMANENT OR ATTR_SYS
6506         DEFB    0,0,0,0
6507         DEFW    1
6508         DEFW    1
6509         DEFW    0
6510         DEFB    0,0,0
6511
6512         DEFB    'DIRECTORY',ETX,'  '
6513         DEFB    ATTR_PERMANENT.OR.ATTR_WRITE_PROT.OR.ATTR_SYSTEM
6514         DEFB    1,0,0,0
6515         DEFW    128
6516         DEFW    1
6517         DEFW    1024
6518         DEFB    0,0,0
6519
6520 HOLE_FILE_NAME:
6521         DEFB    'BLOCKS LEFT',ETX
6522         DEFB    ATTR_HOLE
6523         DEFB    0,0,0,0
6524         DEFB    0,0
6525         DEFB    0,0
6526         DEFB    0,0
6527         DEFB    57H,07H,11H
6528
6529 INIT_INFO_SIZE EQU $-INITIAL_DIRECTORY

6537 NAME "REV 1 - RPD"
6538 *
6539 *       RETURN ERROR CODES UNTIL THESE ROUTINES ARE WRITTEN
6540 *
6541 *       10/12/83  VSB
6542 *       10/14/83  RPD changed __EOS_4 to __CV_A
6543 *
6544
6545         ;GLB    __POSIT_FILE,__EOS_1,__EOS_2,__EOS_3,__CV_A
6546 __POSIT_FILE:
6547 __EOS_1:
6548 __EOS_2:
6549 __EOS_3:
6550 __CV_A:
6551         LD      A,PROG_NON_EXIST    ; SIGNIFY PROGRAM DOES NOT EXIST
6552         OR      A                   ; NZ CONDITION
6553         RET

6561 ;REV 0  (V/D 8/24/83)
6562
6563
6564 ;****************************************************************
6565 ; THIS ROUTINE WILL FIND THE DCB FOR THE DEVICE DESIRED
6566 *
6567 *       INPUT:  A ==> POINTS TO THE DEVICE ID
6568 *       OUTPUT:
6569 *               CONDITION FLAG:
6570 *                       Z : NO ERRORS
6571 *                           IY ==> POINTS TO THE START OF THE DEVICE DCB
6572 *                       NZ: ERROR OCCURED
6573 *                           IY ==> IS DESTROYED
6574 *
6575 ;****************************************************************
6576
6577
6578         ;GLB    __GET_DCB_ADDR
6579         GLOBAL  __FIND_DCB
6580         ;EXT    CURRENT_PCB
6581
6582 __GET_DCB_ADDR:
6583 __FIND_DCB:
6584         PUSH    BC
6585         PUSH    DE
6586
6587         LD      C,A                         ; SAVE THE DEVICE ID
6588         LD      IY,(CURRENT_PCB)            ; GET THE START ADDRESS OF PCB
6589         LD      B,(IY+P_NUM_DCBS)           ; GET THE NUMBER OF DCB'S
6590         XOR     A
6591         CP      B                           ; ARE THERE ZERO DCBS?
6592         JR      Z,DCB_NO_FIND               ; YES
6593                                             ; NO
6594         LD      DE,P_SIZE                   ; GET THE SIZE OF THE PCB
6595         ADD     IY,DE                       ; ADD TO IY TO GET TO THE START OF THE DCB
6596         LD      DE,D_SIZE                   ; GET THE DCB SIZE
6597         LD      A,C                         ; RELOAD THE DEVICE ID
6598         AND     0FH                         ; AND OF THE HIGH BITS TO GET ONLY
6599                                             ; THE DEVICE ID
6600
6601 DCB_LOOP:
6602         CP      (IY+D_DEV_ADDR)             ; IS THIS THE CORRECT DEVICE DCB?
6603         JR      Z,FOUND_IT                  ; YES
6604                                             ; NO
6605         ADD     IY,DE                       ; GET TO THE NEXT DCB AND CHECK IT
6606         DJNZ    DCB_LOOP                    ; GO CHECK THEM ALL UNTIL ONE IS FOUND
6607
6608         JR      DCB_NO_FIND
6609
6610
6611 FOUND_IT:
6612         LD      A,C                         ; RESTORE A TO ITS ORIGINAL STATE
6613         JR      FINISHED
6614
6615 DCB_NO_FIND:
6616         LD      A,DCB_NOT_FOUND
6617         OR      A
6618
6619 FINISHED:
6620         POP     DE
6621         POP     BC
6622
6623         RET
6624
6625
6626
6627
6628 ;****************************************************************
6629 * THIS ROUTINE WILL INITIATE A STATUS REQUEST COMMAND, AND WILL RETURN
6630 * THE RESULT OF THE COMMAND.
6631 *       INPUT:  A ==> DEVICE ID
6632 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION BITS
6633 *               Z :     NO ERRORS
6634 *                       IY CONTAINS START ADDRESS OF DCB
6635 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
6636 *                       A ==> ERROR CODE
6637 *                       IY IS DESTROYED (MAY BE ADDRESS OF DCB
6638 *                       UNDER CERTAIN ERROR CONDITIONS)
6639 *
6640 ;****************************************************************
6641
6642         ;GLB    __REQUEST_STATUS
6643
6644
6645 __REQUEST_STATUS:
6646         CALL    __FIND_DCB                  ; GET THE CORRECT DEVICE DCB
6647
6648         JR      NZ,END_R_S
6649
6650         LD      (IY+D_COM_STAT),DCB_STATUS  ; SEND THE COMMAND TO REQUEST
6651                                             ; A STATUS
6652
6653 REQUEST_LOOP:
6654         BIT     CMND_COMPLETE_BIT,(IY+D_COM_STAT)    ; HAS THE COMMAND COMPLETED?
6655         JR      Z,REQUEST_LOOP              ; NO LOOP TILL IT DOES
6656
6657         LD      A,(IY+D_COM_STAT)           ; CHECK THE STATUS
6658         CP      CMND_FIN_STATUS             ; DID IT FINISH WITH NO ERRORS?
6659                                             ; IF ZERO EVERYTHING IS OK
6660                                             ; IF NZ THERE WAS AN ERROR
6661
6662 END_R_S:
6663         RET
6664
6665
6666
6667
6668         ;GLB    __RD_DEV_DEP_STAT
6669
6670 __RD_DEV_DEP_STAT:
6671         PUSH    IY                          ; SAVE THIS REGISTER
6672         CALL    __FIND_DCB                  ; GET THE CORRECT DEVICE DCB
6673
6674         JR      NZ,END_R_D_D_S
6675
6676         XOR     A                           ; NO ERROR
6677
6678         LD      A,(IY+D_STATUS_FLAGS)       ; READ DEVICE-DEPENDENT STATUS FLAGS
6679
6680 END_R_D_D_S:
6681         POP     IY                          ; RESTORE THE REGISTER
6682         RET
6683
6684
6685 ;****************************************************************
6686 * THIS ROUTINE WILL CHECK AND SEE IF THE DEVICE DCB IS CURRENTLY ACTIVE.
6687 * IF IT IS THEN A NZ CONDITION WILL BE SENT BACK TO THE CALLING PROGRAM
6688 *       INPUT:  IY ===> POINTS TO START OF DEVICE DCB
6689 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
6690 *               Z :     DCB IS INACTIVE, IN OTHERWORDS IT IS AVAILABLE
6691 *                       A ===> IS PRESERVED
6692 *               NZ:     DCB IS ACTIVE
6693 *                       A ===> ERROR CODE
6694 *
6695 ;****************************************************************
6696
6697 ; OK CHANGED TO G_OK TO PREVENT CONFLICT WITH IDENTICAL
6698 ; LABEL ELSEWHERE
6699
6700         ;GLB    CHK_IF_INACTIVE
6701
6702 CHK_IF_INACTIVE:
6703         PUSH    BC
6704
6705         LD      C,A                         ; PRESERVE A
6706         LD      A,(IY+D_COM_STAT)           ; IS THE DCB AVAILABLE?
6707         CP      DCB_IDLE
6708         JR      Z,G_OK                      ; YES IT IS INACTIVE
6709                                             ; NO IT IS NOT
6710         BIT     CMND_COMPLETE_BIT,(IY+D_COM_STAT)    ; HAS THE COMMAND BEEN
6711                                             ; PROCESSED
6712         JR      Z,DEVICE_BUSY               ; NO, THE DEVICE IS STILL BUSY
6713                                             ; YES, THE DCB IS AVAILABLE
6714 G_OK:
6715         XOR     A                           ; INSURE ZERO CONDITION
6716         LD      A,C                         ; RESTORE A
6717         JR      DONE
6718
6719 DEVICE_BUSY:
6720         INC     A                           ; INSURE A NON_ZERO CONDITION FOR FLAG
6721         LD      A,DCB_BUSY                  ; SEND BACK THE ERROR CODE
6722
6723 DONE:
6724         POP     BC
6725         RET
6726
6727
6728
6729
6730 ;****************************************************************
6731 *
6732 *       CHK_FOR_IDLE CHECKS IF THE REQUESTED DEVICE IS IDLE
6733 *       SENDING BACK AN ERROR CODE
6734 *
6735 *       INPUT:  IY       POINTER TO THE DCB
6736 *
6737 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
6738 *               Z        DEVICE NOT IDLE
6739 *               NZ       DEVICE IDLE
6740 *
6741 ;****************************************************************
6742
6743
6744         ;GLB    CHK_IF_IDLE
6745
6746 CHK_IF_IDLE:
6747
6748         LD      A,(IY+D_COM_STAT)           ; GET STATUS BYTE
6749
6750         OR      A                           ; IS IT IDLE(ZERO)?
6751         JR      NZ,NOT_IDLE                 ; NOPE
6752
6753         LD      A,DCB_IDLE_ERR              ; ERROR CODE FOR DEVICE IDLE
6754
6755         OR      A                           ; CREATE NZ CONDITION
6756
6757         RET
6758
6759 NOT_IDLE:
6760         XOR     A                           ; CLEAR ZERO FLAG
6761
6762         RET
6763 ;
6764 ;================================================================
6765 ;================================================================
6766 ;================================================================
6767 ;
6768
6769
6770 ;REV 0                  (V/D  8-24-83)
6771
6772 ;****************************************************************
6773 * THIS ROUTINE WILL READ A CHARACTER FROM THE KEYBOARD
6774 *
6775 *       INPUT:  NOTHING
6776 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
6777 *               Z :     NO ERRORS
6778 *                       A ===> CONTAINS THE KEY READ
6779 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
6780 *                       A ===> ERROR CODE
6781 *
6782 *
6783 ;****************************************************************
6784
6785         GLOBAL  __RD_KBD
6786         ;EXT    KEYBOARD_BUFFER
6787         ;EXT    __RD_CH_DEV
6788
6789 __RD_KBD:
6790         PUSH    BC
6791         PUSH    DE
6792
6793 RD_KBD_LOOP:
6794
6795         CALL    __START_RD_KBD
6796
6797         JR      NZ,ERROR_OCCURED
6798
6799         LD      C,A                         ; PRESERVE THE DEVICE ID
6800
6801 WAITING:
6802         LD      A,C                         ; RESTOR THE DEVICE ID
6803                                             ; BC IS SAVED IN ALL CALLED MODULES
6804         CALL    __END_RD_KBD
6805
6806         JR      NC,WAITING                  ; THE OPERATION HAS NOT COMPLETED YET
6807                                             ; AT THIS POINT WE EITHER HAVE THE
6808                                             ; KEY OR AN ERROR CODE IN A
6809 ERROR_OCCURED:
6810         POP     DE
6811         POP     BC
6812
6813         RET
6814
6815
6816 ;****************************************************************
6817 * THIS ROUTINE WILL REQUEST A STATUS ON THE KEYBOARD
6818 *       INPUT:  NOTHING
6819 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAG
6820 *               Z :     NO ERRORS
6821 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
6822 *                       A ===> ERROR CODE
6823 *
6824 ;****************************************************************
6825
6826         GLOBAL  __REQ_KBD_STAT
6827
6828         ;EXT    __REQUEST_STATUS
6829
6830 __REQ_KBD_STAT:
6831         LD      A,KEYBOARD_ID               ; WANT STATUS ON THE KEYBOARD
6832         JP      __REQUEST_STATUS
6833
6834
6835 ;****************************************************************
6836 * THIS ROUTINE WILL START A READ DATA COMMNAD ON THE KEYBOARD
6837 *       INPUT:  NOTHING
6838 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
6839 *               Z :     NO ERROR
6840 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
6841 *                       A ===> ERROR CODE
6842 ;****************************************************************
6843
6844         ;GLB    __START_RD_KBD
6845
6846         ;EXT    __START_RD_CH_DEV
6847
6848 __START_RD_KBD:
6849         PUSH    BC
6850         PUSH    DE
6851
6852 STRT_RD_KBD_LOOP:
6853         LD      A,KEYBOARD_ID
6854         LD      DE,KEYBOARD_BUFFER          ; DESTINATION OF WHERE TO PLACE KEY
6855         LD      BC,1                        ; GET 1 CHARACTER
6856         CALL    __START_RD_CH_DEV           ; GO INITIATE THE COMMAND
6857
6858         POP     DE
6859         POP     BC
6860
6861         RET
6862
6864
6865 ;****************************************************************
6866 * THIS ROUTINE WILL CHECK THE RESULT OF A COMMAND THAT WAS SENT.  IF THE
6867 * RESULT IS A NAK THEN THE ROUTINE WILL ISSUE A NEW READ COMMAND.
6868 *       INPUT:  NOTHING
6869 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAG
6870 *               C :     COMMAND HAS FINISHED
6871 *               NC:     COMMAND HAS NOT BEEN FINISHED
6872 *               Z :     NO ERROR OCCURED
6873 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
6874 *                       A ===> ERROR CODE
6875 ;****************************************************************
6876
6877         ;GLB    __END_RD_KBD
6878         ;EXT    __END_RD_CH_DEV
6879
6880 __END_RD_KBD:
6881         LD      A,KEYBOARD_ID               ; SEND THIS PARM TO __EN_RD_CH_DEV
6882         CALL    __END_RD_CH_DEV             ; CHECK THE RESULT
6883
6884         JR      NC,ROUTINE_DONE             ; CARRY FLAG IS CLEARED, CMND IS NOT COMPLETE
6885                                             ; THE COMMAND IS COMPLETE
6886         JR      Z,HAVE_THE_KEY              ; ROUTINE COMPLETED WITH NO ERRORS,
6887                                             ; CARRY FLAG IS SET, ZERO CONDITION
6888                                             ; THERE WAS AN ERROR
6889         CP      KBD_NAK                     ; WAS THE ERROR A NAK?
6890         JR      NZ,NO_NAK                   ; NO, GO SET C FLAG TO SHOW COMMAND COMPLETED
6891                                             ; YES, TRY THE COMMAND AGAIN
6892         CALL    __START_RD_KBD
6893         JR      Z,SHOW_COMPLETE
6894                                             ; THERE WAS AN ERROR IN THE COMMAND RETRY
6895                                             ; SET THE CARRY FLAG TO SHOW THAT THE
6896                                             ; OPERATION COMPLETED. THE FLAG
6897                                             ; CONDITION WILL BE NON-ZERO TO
6898                                             ; INDICATE AN ERROR OCCURED. A CONTAINS
6899                                             ; THE ERROR CODE
6900 NO_NAK:
6901         SCF
6902         JR      ROUTINE_DONE
6903
6904 HAVE_THE_KEY:
6905         LD      A,(KEYBOARD_BUFFER)         ; PUT THE KEY IN THE KEYBOARD BUFFER
6906         JR      ROUTINE_DONE
6907
6908 SHOW_COMPLETE:
6909         OR      A                           ; CLEAR CARRY FLAG BECAUSE THE RETRY WAS STARTED
6910                                             ; WITH NO PROBLEMS. THE COMMAND IS NOT COMPLETE
6911 ROUTINE_DONE:
6912         RET
6913 ;
6914 ;================================================================
6915 ;================================================================
6916 ;================================================================
6917 ;
6918
6919
6920
6921 ;REV 1                  (V/D  8-24-83)
6922 ;****************************************************************
6923 * PRINT_CHAR ALLOWS THE CALLER TO PRINT ONE CHARACTER
6924 *       INPUT:  A ===> CHARACTER TO BE PRINTED
6925 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
6926 *               Z :     NO ERROR
6927 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
6928 *                       A ===> ERROR CODE
6929 *
6930 ;****************************************************************
6931
6932         ;GLOBAL __PR_CH
6933         ;EXT    PRINT_BUFFER
6934         ;EXT    CHK_IF_INACTIVE
6935
6936 __PR_CH:
6937         PUSH    BC
6938         PUSH    DE
6939         PUSH    HL
6940         PUSH    IY
6941
6942         LD      (PRINT_BUFFER),A
6943         LD      A,ETX                       ; TERMINATE STRIN W/ETX
6944         LD      (PRINT_BUFFER+1),A
6945         LD      HL,PRINT_BUFFER             ; ADDRESS OF PRINT BUFFER
6946
6947         CALL    __PR_BUFF
6948
6949         POP     IY
6950         POP     HL
6951         POP     DE
6952         POP     BC
6953
6954         RET
6955
6956
6957 ;****************************************************************
6958 * PRINT_BUFFER ALLOWS THE CALLER TO PRINT AN ASCII ETX-TERMINATED STRING
6959 *       INPUT:  HL ===> ADDRESS OF ETX-TERMINATED STRING
6960 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
6961 *               Z :     NO ERROR
6962 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
6963 *                       A ===> ERROR CODE
6964 *
6965 ;****************************************************************
6966
6967         GLOBAL  __PR_BUFF
6968
6969         ;EXT    __FIND_DCB,__WR_CH_DEV
6970
6971 __PR_BUFF:
6972         PUSH    BC
6973         PUSH    DE
6974         PUSH    HL
6975         PUSH    IY
6976
6977 PR_BUFF2:
6978         LD      A,PRINTER_ID                ; WANT THE PRINTERS DCB
6979         CALL    __FIND_DCB                  ; GET THE DEVICE'S DCB
6980
6981         JR      NZ,END_PRINT_ROUTINE        ; THERE WAS A PROBLEM FINDING THE DCB
6982
6983         CALL    CHK_IF_INACTIVE
6984         JR      NZ,PRTR_DCB_ACTIVE
6985
6986 CHECK_FOR_ETX:
6987         LD      BC,0                        ; CHARACTER COUNT
6988         LD      E,L                         ; SAVE THE STRING
6989         LD      D,H                         ; DE=HL= PTR TO UNPRINTED STRING
6990
6991 GET_NEXT_ASCII:
6992         LD      A,ETX
6993         CP      (HL)                        ; IS IT AN ETX?
6994         JR      Z,ITS_ETX                   ; YES
6995
6996                                             ; NO
6997         INC     HL                          ; POINT TO NEXT CHARACTER
6998         INC     C                           ; INC COUNT
6999         LD      A,C                         ; HAVE WE LOOKED AT 16 BYTES?
7000         CP      16
7001         JR      NZ,GET_NEXT_ASCII           ; YES
7002 ;                                           DE = PTR TO UNPRINTED STRING, DE=PTR TO UNPRNTED+16
7003         EX      DE,HL                       ; HL = PTR TO UNPRINTED STRING, DE=PTR TO UNPRNTED+16
7004
7005 WRITE_COMMAND_AGAIN:
7006
7007         LD      A,PRINTER_ID
7008         CALL    __WR_CH_DEV
7009
7010         JR      Z,WRITE_DONE                ; WRITE COMPLETED WITH NO ERRORS
7011
7012         CP      PR_NAK                      ; IT A NAK?
7013         JR      NZ,PRINT_CMND_FAILED        ; AN ERROR
7014
7015         JR      WRITE_COMMAND_AGAIN         ; SEND ANOTHER WRITE COMMAND
7016
7017 WRITE_DONE:
7018         EX      DE,HL                       ; DE = PTR TO UNPRINTED STRNG, HL=PTR TO UNPRNTED+16
7019         JR      CHECK_FOR_ETX               ; GO CHECK THE NEXT CHARACTER
7020
7021
7022 ITS_ETX:
7023         EX      DE,HL
7024         XOR     A                           ; ZERO OUT A
7025         CP      C                           ; WAS THE FIRST CHARACTER CHECKED
7026                                             ; AN ETX?
7027         JR      Z,END_PRINT_ROUTINE         ; YUP, DON'T BOTHER PRINTING IT
7028
7029 SEND_ANOTHER_WRITE:
7030         LD      A,PRINTER_ID                ; WILL WRITE TO THE PRINTER
7031         CALL    __WR_CH_DEV                 ; WRITE CHARACTERS
7032
7033         JR      Z,COMMAND_FINISHED_SUCCESSFULLY     ; ALL DONE
7034
7035         CP      PR_NAK                      ; DO WE HAVE A NAK?
7036         JR      Z,SEND_ANOTHER_WRITE        ; YUP
7037
7038                                             ; NOPE, MUST BE AN ERROR
7039
7040
7041 COMMAND_FINISHED_SUCCESSFULLY:
7042 PRINT_CMND_FAILED:
7043 PRNT_CMND_FAILED:
7044 PRTR_DCB_ACTIVE:
7045 END_PRINT_ROUTINE:
7046         POP     IY
7047         POP     HL
7048         POP     DE
7049         POP     BC                          ; ON ERROR, ZERO FLAG SET AND A
7050                                             ; CONTAINS THE ERROR CODE
7051         RET
7052
7053
7054 ;****************************************************************
7055 *
7056 * __SETUP_PR_BUFF SETS UP PRINT_BUFFER AND THE HL REGISTER AND
7057 * CALLS __START_PR_BUFF
7058 *       INPUT:  A = ASCII CHARACTER
7059 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
7060 *               Z:      NO ERROR
7061 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
7062 *                       A = ERROR CODE
7063 *
7064 ;****************************************************************
7065
7066         ;GLB    __SETUP_PR_BUFF
7067
7068 __SETUP_PR_BUFF:
7069         LD      (PRINT_BUFFER),A
7070         LD      A,ETX
7071         LD      (PRINT_BUFFER+1),A
7072
7073         LD      HL,PRINT_BUFFER
7074
7075         CALL    __START_PR_BUFF
7076
7077         RET
7078
7079
7080
7081
7082 ;****************************************************************
7083 *
7084 * __START_PR_CH SETS UP PRINT_BUFFER AND THE HL REGISTER AND
7085 * CALLS __START_PR_BUFF
7086 *       INPUT:  A = ASCII CHARACTER
7087 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
7088 *               Z:      NO ERROR
7089 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
7090 *                       A = ERROR CODE
7091 *
7092 ;****************************************************************
7093
7094         ;GLB    __START_PR_CH
7095
7096 __START_PR_CH:
7097         LD      (PRINT_BUFFER),A
7098         LD      A,ETX
7099         LD      (PRINT_BUFFER+1),A
7100
7101         LD      HL,PRINT_BUFFER
7102
7103         CALL    __START_PR_BUFF
7104
7105         RET
7106
7107
7108
7109
7110 ;****************************************************************
7111 *
7112 *       __END_PR_CH    CALLS __END_PR_BUFF TO CHECK THE DCB STATUS BYTE AND
7113 *       RETURNS THE RESULT TO THE CALLER
7114 *
7115 *       INPUT:  NONE
7116 *
7117 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
7118 *               C:      COMMAND COMPLETED
7119 *               NC:     COMMAND NOT COMPLETED
7120 *               Z:      NO ERRORS OCCURED
7121 *               NZ:     ERRORS
7122 *                       A = ERROR CODE
7123 *
7124 *       ALL     REGISTERS ARE PRESERVED
7125 *
7126 ;****************************************************************
7127
7128         ;GLB    __END_PR_CH
7129 __END_PR_CH:
7130         CALL    __END_PR_BUFF
7131
7132         RET
7133
7134

7135
7136 ;***************************************************************
7137 ; __START_PR_BUFF ALLOWS THE CALLER TO PRINT AN ASCII ETX-TERMINATED STRING
7138 ;        INPUT:   HL ==>  ADDRESS OF ETX-TERMINATED STRING
7139 ;        OUTPUT:  CONDITION FLAGS
7140 ;                 Z :    NO ERROR
7141 ;                 NZ:    ERROR OCCURED
7142 ;                        A ==>  ERROR CODE
7143 ;
7144 ;***************************************************************
7145
7146         ;GLB    __START_PR_BUFF
7147
7148         ;EXT    __START_WR_CH_DEV
7149
7150 __START_PR_BUFF:
7151         PUSH    BC
7152         PUSH    DE
7153         PUSH    HL
7154         PUSH    IY
7155
7156         LD      A,PRINTER_ID             ; WANT THE PRINTERS DCB
7157         CALL    __FIND_DCB               ; GET THE DEVICE'S DCB
7158
7159         JR      NZ,NO_DCB_FOUND1         ; THERE WAS A PROBLEM FINDING THE DCB
7160
7161         CALL    CHK_IF_INACTIVE
7162         JR      NZ,PRTR_DCB_ACTIVE1
7163
7164         LD      BC,0                     ; CHARACTER COUNT
7165         LD      E,L                      ; SAVE THE STRING
7166         LD      D,H                      ; DE=HL= PTR TO UNPRINTED STRING
7167
7168 GET_NEXT_ASCII2:
7169         LD      A,ETX
7170         CP      (HL)                     ; IS IT AN ETX?
7171         JR      Z,ITS_ETX2               ; YES
7172
7173                                             ; NO
7174         INC     HL                       ; POINT TO NEXT CHARACTER
7175         INC     C                        ; INC COUNT
7176         LD      A,C                      ; HAVE WE LOOKED AT 16 BYTES?
7177         CP      16
7178         JR      NZ,GET_NEXT_ASCII2       ; YES
7179 ;                                       DE = PTR TO UNPRINTED STRNG, DE=PTR TO UNPRNTED+16
7180         EX      DE,HL                   ; HL = PTR TO UNPRINTED STRNG, DE=PTR TO UNPRNTED+16
7181         LD      A,PRINTER_ID
7182         CALL    __START_WR_CH_DEV
7183
7184         JR      WRITE_CMND_SENT
7185
7186 ITS_ETX2:
7187         EX      DE,HL
7188         XOR     A                        ; ZERO OUT A
7189         CP      C                        ; WAS THE FIRST CHARACTER CHECKED
7190                                             ; AN ETX?
7191
7192         LD      A,PRINTER_ID             ; WILL WRITE TO THE PRINTER
7193         CALL    NZ,__START_WR_CH_DEV     ; NOPE, WRITE CHARACTER
7194                                             ; IS PASSED BACK TO CALLER
7195 NO_DCB_FOUND1:
7196 WRITE_CMND_SENT:
7197
7198         POP     IY
7199         POP     HL
7200         POP     DE
7201         POP     BC
7202
7203         RET
7204
7205
7206
7207
7208 ;***************************************************************
7209 ;
7210 ; __END_PR_BUFF CHECKS THE DCB COMM/STATUS BYTE AND RETURNS THE RESULT
7211 ; OF THE CHECK
7212 ;
7213 ;        INPUT:   NONE
7214 ;
7215 ;        OUTPUT:  CONDITION FLAGS
7216 ;                 C:     COMMAND COMPLETED
7217 ;                 NC:    COMMAND NOT COMPLETED
7218 ;                 Z:     NO ERRORS OCCURED
7219 ;                 NZ:    ERRORS
7220 ;                        A = ERROR CODE
7221 ;
7222 ;        ALL      REGISTERS ARE PRESERVED
7223 ;
7224 ;***************************************************************
7225
7226         ;GLB    __END_PR_BUFF
7227         ;EXT    __END_WR_CH_DEV
7228
7229 __END_PR_BUFF:
7230
7231         PUSH    IY
7232
7233         LD      A,PRINTER_ID
7234         CALL    __FIND_DCB
7235         CALL    __END_WR_CH_DEV          ; CHECK STATUS BYTE
7236
7237         JR      NC,CMND_NOT_PROCESSED    ; COMMAND NOT COMPLETED
7238
7239         JR      Z,FINISHED_WO_ERROR      ; COMMAND COMPLETED, NO ERROR
7240
7241         CP      PR_NAK                   ; WAS IT A NAK?
7242
7243         JR      NZ,NOT_PR_NAK            ; NOPE, MUST BE AN ERROR
7244
7245         LD      (IY+D_COM_STAT),DCB_WR   ; SEND ANOTHER WRITE COMMAND
7246
7247         OR      A                        ; CLEAR CARRY TO INDICATE COMMAND
7248                                             ; HAS NOT BEEN FINISHED
7249
7250 NOT_PR_NAK:
7251 CMND_NOT_PROCESSED:
7252 FINISHED_WO_ERROR:
7253
7254         POP     IY
7255
7256         RET
7257

7259 ;***************************************************************
7260 ; THIS ROUTINE REQUESTS THE STATUS ON THE PRINTER
7261 ;        INPUT:   NONE
7262 ;        OUTPUT:
7263 ;                 CONDITION FLAGS
7264 ;                 Z :     NO ERROR
7265 ;                 NZ:     ERROR OCCURRED
7266 ;                         A ===>  ERROR CODE
7267 ;
7268 ;***************************************************************
7269         GLOBAL  __REQ_PR_STAT
7270
7271         ;EXT    __REQUEST_STATUS
7272
7273 __REQ_PR_STAT:
7274         LD      A,PRINTER_ID
7275         JP      __REQUEST_STATUS

7345                         ;EXT    Y_MIN
7346                         ;EXT    Y_MAX
