7355 ;
7356 ;Local Equates
7357 ;
7358 LF              EQU     0AH
7359 FF              EQU     0CH
7360 BS              EQU     08H
7361 CR              EQU     0DH
7362 CLR_TO_EOL      EQU     16H
7363 CLR_TO_EOS      EQU     1BH
7364 GOTO_XY_CHAR    EQU     1CH
7365 HOME            EQU     80H
7366 UP              EQU     0A0H
7367 RIGHT           EQU     0A1H
7368 DOWN            EQU     0A2H
7369 LEFT            EQU     0A3H
7370 CURSOR_CHAR     EQU     "_"             ;Char to emit as cursor
7371 BLANKING_CHAR   EQU     "-"             ;Char used when scrolling to fill screen
7372 CURSOR_X        EQU     CURSOR+1
7373
7374
7375 ;
7376 ;__CONS_INIT
7377 ;
7378 ;       Sets up the window sizes for the CONSOLE_OUT routine
7379 ;
7380 ;Input parameters:
7381 ; B - number of columns in the window - 1, 0..31
7382 ; C - number of lines in the window - 1, 0..23
7383 ; D - Absolute (0 based) X coord of upper left corner of window, 0..31
7384 ; E - Absolute (0 based) Y coord of upper left corner of window, 0..23
7385 ; HL - Pattern Name Table (PNT) starting address
7386 ;
7387 ;NOTE- User must initialize the VDP
7388 ;
7389         ;GLB    __CONS_INIT
7390 __CONS_INIT:
7391         INC     B                           ;Adjust for old calling convention
7392         INC     C
7393         LD      [NUM_LINES],BC              ;Set num lines, cols in window
7394         LD      [UPPER_LEFT],DE             ;Set upper left corner of window
7395         LD      [CURSOR],DE
7396         LD      [PTRN_NAME_TBL],HL          ;Save address of Pattern name table
7397
7398         LD      A,D                         ;Calc min/max coords of window
7399         LD      [X_MIN],A
7400         ADD     A,B
7401         DEC     A
7402         LD      [X_MAX],A
7403         LD      A,E
7404         LD      [Y_MIN],A
7405         ADD     A,C
7406         DEC     A
7407         LD      [Y_MAX],A
7408
7409         LD      A,BLANKING_CHAR
7410         LD      [OLDCHAR_],A
7411         LD      A,CURSOR_CHAR
7412         CALL    PUT_CHAR_ON_SCREEN
7413
7414         RET
7415
7416 ;
7417 ;__CONS_OUT  - This entry point will check special chars such as
7418 ;              Carriage Return and performs their normal functions
7419 ;              all other, printable, chars are put onto the screen
7420 ;
7421 ;__CONS_DISP - This entry point will put all chars onto the screen
7422 ;              no matter what, no checking is done for control chars
7423 ;
7424 ;
7425 ;Input Parameters:
7426 ; A - character to emit
7427 ;
7428 ; optionally
7429 ; E - Y location to goto (must be in window)
7430 ; D - X location to goto (must be in window)
7431 ;
7432 ;
7433 ; Saves all registers
7434 ;
7435         ;GLB    __CONS_OUT
7436         ;GLB    __CONS_DISP
7437 __CONS_OUT:
7438         PUSH    AF
7439         PUSH    BC
7440         PUSH    HL
7441         PUSH    IX
7442         PUSH    IY
7443         PUSH    DE                          ;This may contain the X-Y coord of goto xy
7444
7445         LD      HL,SPECIAL_CHARS
7446         LD      BC,NUM_SPCL_CHARS
7447         CPIR
7448         JR      NZ,NOT_SPECIAL
7449
7450         LD      HL,SPCL_VECTOR_TBL
7451         ADD     HL,BC
7452         ADD     HL,BC
7453
7454         LD      B,A                         ;Save char in B
7455
7456         LD      A,[HL]
7457         INC     HL
7458         LD      H,[HL]
7459         LD      L,A
7460         JP      [HL]                        ;Vector to special char handler
7461
7462 __CONS_DISP:
7463         PUSH    AF
7464         PUSH    BC
7465         PUSH    HL
7466         PUSH    IX
7467         PUSH    IY
7468         PUSH    DE
7469 NOT_SPECIAL:
7470         CALL    PUT_CHAR_ON_SCREEN          ;Display char where cursor is at
7471         LD      HL,[CURSOR]
7472         INC     H                           ;Move cursor to right (+1)
7473         LD      A,[X_MAX]                   ; but check for going out of
7474         CP      H                           ; the window
7475         JR      NC,SAVE_CURSOR              ;If taken then stayed in window
7476         LD      A,[X_MIN]                   ;Went out of window so set X
7477         LD      H,A                         ; to left edge
7478         INC     L                           ; and move down one line
7479         LD      A,[Y_MAX]                   ; but check to see if still
7480         CP      L                           ; in the window
7481         JR      NC,SAVE_CURSOR
7482         DEC     L                           ; Put back into window
7483         PUSH    HL
7484         CALL    GET_CHAR_UNDER_CURSOR
7485         CALL    SCROLL_UP
7486         POP     HL
7487
7488 SAVE_CURSOR:    LD      [CURSOR],HL
7489 NEW_CURSOR:     CALL    GET_CHAR_UNDER
7490 PUT_CURSOR_ON:
7491         LD      A,CURSOR_CHAR
7492         CALL    PUT_CHAR_ON_SCREEN
7493
7494 EXIT:           POP     DE
7495         POP     IY
7496         POP     IX
7497         POP     HL
7498         POP     BC
7499         POP     AF
7500         RET
7501
7502 ;
7503 ;Special Character Handlers
7504 ;
7505
7506 ; RETURN_WITH_ERASE - moves cursor to right edge of window on current line
7507 ;                     after clearing to end of line
7508 ;
7509 RETURN_WITH_ERASE:
7510         CALL    ERASE_THIS_LINE
7511 RETURN:         CALL    PUT_OLD_ON_SCREEN          ;Display char cursor was hiding
7512         LD      A,[X_MIN]
7513         LD      [CURSOR_X],A
7514         LD      HL,[CURSOR]
7515         JP      NEW_CURSOR
7516
7517 ;
7518 ; MOVE_UP - Moves the cursor up if not at top of window
7519 ;
7520 MOVE_UP:
7521         LD      HL,[CURSOR]
7522         LD      A,[Y_MIN]
7523         CP      L
7524         JR      Z,EXIT                      ;Forget request
7525         DEC     L                           ;Move up
7526
7527 RESTORE_OLD:    CALL    PUT_OLD_ON_SCREEN  ;Display char cursor was hiding
7528         JP      SAVE_CURSOR                 ;Display cursor in new spot
7529
7530 ;
7531 ; MOVE_DOWN - Moves the cursor down if will stay in window, except
7532 ;             if used by linefeed when the screen is scrolled
7533 ;
7534 MOVE_DOWN:
7535
7536         LD      HL,[CURSOR]
7537         LD      A,[Y_MAX]                   ;See if at bottom of window
7538         CP      L
7539         JR      Z,SCROLL_POSSIBLE
7540         INC     L                           ;Move down
7541         JP      RESTORE_OLD
7542 ;
7543 SCROLL_POSSIBLE: LD      A,LF              ;See if linefeed, and if it is
7544         CP      B                           ; then scroll up. else leave
7545         JR      NZ,EXIT                     ; cursor and screen as is
7546         CALL    SCROLL_UP
7547         LD      HL,[CURSOR]
7548         JP      NEW_CURSOR
7549
7550 ;
7551 ; MOVE_RIGHT - Moves the cursor to the right, and if at window's
7552 ;              edge will move down a line to the left edge, unless
7553 ;              at the lower right corner of the window in which case
7554 ;              nothing is down
7555 ;
7556 MOVE_RIGHT:
7557         LD      HL,[CURSOR]
7558         LD      A,[X_MAX]
7559         CP      H
7560         JR      Z,TRY_MOVE_DOWN
7561         INC     H                           ;Move to right
7562         JP      RESTORE_OLD
7563 ;
7564 TRY_MOVE_DOWN:  LD      A,[Y_MAX]
7565         CP      L
7566         JR      Z,EXIT                      ;At bottom of window so abort
7567         INC     L
7568         LD      A,[X_MIN]
7569         LD      H,A
7570         JP      RESTORE_OLD
7571
7572 ;
7573 ; MOVE_LEFT - Moves the cursor to the left and if at left edge of
7574 ;             window will move up a line an to right edge if not
7575 ;             at upper left corner of window
7576 ;
7577 MOVE_LEFT:      LD      HL,[CURSOR]
7578         LD      A,[X_MIN]
7579         CP      H
7580         JR      Z,TRY_SCROLL_UP
7581         DEC     H                           ;Move to left
7582         JP      RESTORE_OLD
7583 ;
7584 TRY_SCROLL_UP:  LD      A,[Y_MIN]
7585         CP      L
7586         JR      Z,EXIT                      ;Abort if at upper left corner
7587         DEC     L
7588         LD      A,[X_MAX]
7589         LD      H,A
7590         JP      RESTORE_OLD
7591
7592 ;
7593 ; MOVE_HOME - Moves cursor to the upper left corner of the window
7594 ;
7595
7596 ; FORM_FEED - Clears the screen and puts the cursor at the upper left
7597 ;             corner of the window
7598 FORM_FEED:
7599         LD      A,[NUM_LINES]
7600         LD      B,A
7601         LD      HL,[UPPER_LEFT]
7602
7603         CALL    BLANK_SCREEN
7604
7605         LD      A,BLANKING_CHAR
7606         LD      [OLDCHAR_],A
7607
7608 MOVE_HOME:      CALL    PUT_OLD_ON_SCREEN  ;Needed only for MOVE_HOME not FORM_FEED
7609         LD      HL,[UPPER_LEFT]
7610         JP      SAVE_CURSOR
7611
7612 ;
7613 ; ERASE_TO_EOL - From cursor position fills rest of line
7614 ;                with BLANKING_CHAR, to end of window only
7615 ;                Will blank char under cursor always.
7616 ;
7617 ERASE_TO_EOL:
7618         CALL    ERASE_THIS_LINE
7619         JP      PUT_CURSOR_ON
7620
7621 ;
7622 ; ERASE_TO_EOS - Will erase the screen (window) from the current
7623 ;                cursor position to the end of the window. The
7624 ;                cursor position is also blanked.
7625 ;
7626 ;
7627 ERASE_TO_EOS:
7628         CALL    ERASE_THIS_LINE             ;Erase to end of current line
7629         LD      HL,[CURSOR]
7630         INC     L                           ;Move down
7631         LD      A,[Y_MIN]
7632         LD      C,A
7633         LD      A,[NUM_LINES]
7634         ADD     A,C
7635         SUB     L                           ;Number of lines left to blank
7636         JP      Z,PUT_CURSOR_ON
7637         LD      B,A
7638         LD      A,[X_MIN]
7639         LD      H,A
7640         CALL    BLANK_SCREEN
7641         JP      PUT_CURSOR_ON
7642
7643 ;
7644 ; GOTO_XY - Puts cursor to desired X-Y coord if in window
7645 ;
7646 ;Input Parameters:
7647 ; D - X location
7648 ; E - Y location
7649 ;
7650 GOTO_XY:
7651         LD      A,[X_MIN]
7652         CP      D
7653         JR      Z,CHK_X_WITH_MAX
7654         JP      NC,EXIT
7655 CHK_X_WITH_MAX: LD      A,[X_MAX]
7656         CP      D
7657         JR      Z,CHK_Y_COORD
7658         JP      C,EXIT
7659 CHK_Y_COORD:   LD      A,[Y_MIN]
7660         CP      E
7661         JR      Z,CHK_Y_WITH_MAX
7662         JP      NC,EXIT
7663 CHK_Y_WITH_MAX: LD      A,[Y_MAX]
7664         CP      E
7665         JR      Z,SET_CURSOR
7666         JP      C,EXIT
7667 SET_CURSOR:     EX      DE,HL
7668         JP      RESTORE_OLD
7669
7670 ;
7671 ;Internal Subroutines
7672 ;
7673
7674 ; Subroutine
7675 ; BLANK_PORTION - Fills LINEBUFFER_ with number of chars in B
7676 ;                 from where HL points
7677 ;
7678 ; Input Parameters:
7679 ; HL - Pointer into LINEBUFFER
7680 ; B  - Number of the BLANKING_CHAR's to stuff in
7681 ;
7682 ; A is destroyed
7683 ;
7684 BLANK_PORTION:
7685         PUSH    HL
7686         PUSH    BC
7687         JR      BLANK_REST
7688
7689 ;
7690 ; Subroutine
7691 ; BLANKS        - Fills LINEBUFFER_ with 32 BLANKING_CHAR's
7692 ;
7693 ; A is destroyed
7694 ;
7695 BLANKS:
7696         PUSH    HL
7697         PUSH    BC
7698         LD      HL,LINEBUFFER_
7699         LD      B,32
7700 BLANK_REST:     LD      A,BLANKING_CHAR
7701 BLANK_FILL:     LD      [HL],A
7702         INC     HL
7703         DJNZ    BLANK_FILL
7704         POP     BC
7705         POP     HL
7706         RET
7707
7708 ;
7709 ; Subroutine
7710 ; BLANK_SCREEN  - Will blank a given number of lines on the screen
7711 ;
7712 ; Input Parameters:
7713 ; B  - Number of lines to blank
7714 ; HL - Absolute X-Y coords of start position of 1st line to blank
7715 ;
7716 ; Destroys C, A, DE
7717 ; User must reset cursor
7718 ;
7719 BLANK_SCREEN:
7720         CALL    BLANKS                      ;Fill buffer with BLANKING_CHAR
7721         LD      A,[NUM_COLUMNS]
7722         LD      C,A
7723
7724 BLNK_SCRN_LOOP: PUSH    BC
7725         PUSH    HL                          ;Save X-Y coord of line
7726         CALL    CONVERT_XY
7727         LD      B,00H                       ;C has number of bytes to read
7728         LD      HL,LINEBUFFER_
7729         CALL    WRITE_VRAM
7730         POP     HL
7731         INC     L                           ;To next line below
7732         POP     BC
7733         DJNZ    BLNK_SCRN_LOOP
7734         RET
7735
7736 ;
7737 ; Subroutine
7738 ; ERASE_THIS_LINE - This routine will erase the current line
7739 ;                   from the cursor position to the right edge
7740 ;                   of the window, the char under the cursor is
7741 ;                   also blanked.
7742 ;
7743 ;Input Parameters:
7744 ; None
7745 ;
7746 ; Destroys all registers
7747 ; Sets OLDCHAR_ to BLANKING_CHAR
7748 ;
7749 ERASE_THIS_LINE:
7750         CALL    READ_FROM_CURSOR_TO_EOL
7751         PUSH    HL
7752         PUSH    BC
7753         LD      B,C
7754         CALL    BLANK_PORTION
7755         POP     BC
7756         POP     HL
7757
7758         LD      A,BLANKING_CHAR
7759         LD      [OLDCHAR_],A
7760
7761         JP      WRITE_VRAM                  ;Write line with blanks, return thru WRITE_VRAM
7762
7763 ;
7764 ;Subroutine
7765 ;READ_FROM_CURSOR_TO_EOL - reads the line on screen from the
7766 ;                          cursor position to the right edge of the
7767 ;                          screen
7768 ;
7769 ;Input Parameters:
7770 ; None
7771 ;
7772 ;Returns:
7773 ; BC - number of chars read
7774 ; HL - address of LINEBUFFER_
7775 ; DE - VRAM address of place cursor is
7776 ;
7777 READ_FROM_CURSOR_TO_EOL:
7778         LD      HL,[CURSOR]
7779         LD      A,[X_MAX]
7780         INC     A                           ;Calc # chars to read
7781         SUB     H
7782         LD      C,A
7783         CALL    CONVERT_XY
7784         LD      HL,LINEBUFFER_
7785         LD      B,00H
7786         PUSH    BC
7787         PUSH    HL
7788         PUSH    DE
7789         CALL    READ_VRAM
7790         POP     DE
7791         POP     HL
7792         POP     BC
7793         RET

7794 :
7795 :
7796 :Subroutine
7797 : SCROLL_UP     - Scrolls the entire window up 1 line and puts a line
7798 :                 of BLANKING_CHAR's on the last line
7799 :
7800 :   Caller must restore cursor to screen
7801 :
7802 :SCROLL_UP:
7803                 CALL    PUT_OLD_ON_SCREEN
7804                 LD      HL,(UPPER_LEFT)
7805                 PUSH    HL
7806                 CALL    CONVERT_XY
7807                 POP     HL
7808                 LD      A,(NUM_LINES)
7809                 LD      B,A
7810                 DEC     B
7811 :
7812 :SCROLL_LOOP:    PUSH    BC              ;Save number of remaining lines
7813                 PUSH    DE              ;Save next write location
7814                 INC     L               ;Point to next line below
7815                 PUSH    HL
7816                 CALL    CONVERT_XY
7817                 PUSH    DE              ;Save address of this read
7818                 LD      A,(NUM_COLUMNS)
7819                 LD      C,A
7820                 LD      B,00H
7821                 PUSH    BC              ;Save number of chars in line
7822                 LD      HL,LINEBUFFER_
7823                 CALL    READ_VRAM
7824                 POP     BC              ;Get number of bytes that were read
7825                 POP     DE              ;Get address of last read
7826                 POP     HL              ;Get X-Y coords
7827                 EX      (SP),HL          ;Swap prev read address with X-Y coords
7828                 EX      DE,HL            ;Swap last read with previous address
7829                 PUSH    HL              ;  and save previous
7830                 LD      HL,LINEBUFFER_
7831                 CALL    WRITE_VRAM       ;Write line one line up
7832                 POP     DE              ;Get address of next write
7833                 POP     HL              ;Get starting X-Y of last read
7834                 POP     BC
7835                 DJNZ    SCROLL_LOOP
7836 :
7837                 CALL    BLANKS
7838                 LD      HL,LINEBUFFER
7839                 LD      A,(NUM_COLUMNS)
7840                 LD      C,A             ;B is 0 from DJNZ
7841                 JP      WRITE_VRAM       ;Return thru WRITE_VRAM
7842
7843 :
7844 : Subroutines
7845 : PUT_OLD_ON_SCREEN  - This entry point puts the char that the
7846 :                      cursor was on back onto the screen
7847 :
7848 : PUT_CHAR_ON_SCREEN - This entry point will put the char in A
7849 :                      onto the screen where the cursor currently is
7850 :
7851 :
7852 :PUT_OLD_ON_SCREEN:
7853                 LD      A,(OLDCHAR_)
7854 :
7855 :PUT_CHAR_ON_SCREEN:
7856                 PUSH    HL
7857                 LD      HL,(CURSOR)      ;Get location to put char
7858                 CALL    CONVERT_XY       ;Convert to a VRAM address
7859                 LD      HL,LINEBUFFER_
7860                 LD      (HL),A
7861                 LD      BC,0001H
7862                 CALL    WRITE_VRAM
7863                 POP     HL
7864                 RET
7865 :
7866 :
7867 : Subroutine
7868 : GET_CHAR_UNDER_CURSOR - This entry loads HL from CURSOR before
7869 :                         falling into GET_CHAR_UNDER
7870 :
7871 : GET_CHAR_UNDER - Reads the char where the cursor will go
7872 :                  and saves it in the variable OLDCHAR_
7873 :
7874 :Input Parameters:
7875 : HL - Cursor position
7876 :
7877 : Destroys - HL, BC, DE, A
7878 :
7879 :GET_CHAR_UNDER_CURSOR:
7880                 LD      HL,(CURSOR)
7881 :
7882 :GET_CHAR_UNDER:
7883                 CALL    CONVERT_XY
7884                 LD      BC,0001H
7885                 LD      HL,OLDCHAR_
7886                 JP      READ_VRAM        ;Return thru READ_VRAM
7887
7888 :
7889 : Subroutine
7890 :
7891 : CONVERT_XY - Converts X-Y coordinates on the screen to a
7892 :              VRAM address for that char position
7893 :              Calculates ((( Y * 32) + X ) + base of pattern name table
7894 :              to yield the absolute address in VRAM of the char
7895 :
7896 :Input Parameters:
7897 : H - X coord
7898 : L - Y coord
7899 :
7900 :Returns:
7901 : DE - VRAM address
7902 : HL - Address of Pattern Name Table (side effect)
7903 : No Others are altered
7904 :
7905 :CONVERT_XY:
7906                 LD      E,H             ;Save X coord
7907                 LD      H,00H
7908                 ADD     HL,HL           ;Calc Y coord * 32
7909                 ADD     HL,HL
7910                 ADD     HL,HL
7911                 ADD     HL,HL
7912                 ADD     HL,HL
7913                 LD      D,00H
7914                 ADD     HL,DE           ;Add in X coord
7915                 LD      DE,(PTRN_NAME_TBL)
7916                 ADD     HL,DE
7917                 EX      DE,HL
7918                 RET
7919
7920 :
7921 : This is a table of chars that perform some function rather
7922 : than being printed out to the screen as is. There is a
7923 : jump table that is associated with this table, but NOTE that
7924 : it has its entrys in reverse order of this table. It would
7925 : be best to add to the end of this table and to the beginning
7926 : of the jump table.
7927 :
7928 :SPECIAL_CHARS:
7929                 DEFB    BS
7930                 DEFB    CR
7931                 DEFB    LF
7932                 DEFB    FF
7933                 DEFB    HOME
7934                 DEFB    CLR_TO_EOL
7935                 DEFB    CLR_TO_EOS
7936                 DEFB    GOTO_XY_CHAR
7937                 DEFB    UP
7938                 DEFB    DOWN
7939                 DEFB    LEFT
7940                 DEFB    RIGHT
7941 :
7942 :NUM_SPCL_CHARS  EQU     $-SPECIAL_CHARS
7943 :
7944
7945 :
7946 :NOTE - This table is in the reverse order of the chars so
7947 :       any additions to the end of the chars goes at the top
7948 :       of this table
7949 :
7950 :SPCL_VECTOR_TBL:
7951                 DEFW    MOVE_RIGHT
7952                 DEFW    MOVE_LEFT
7953                 DEFW    MOVE_DOWN
7954                 DEFW    MOVE_UP
7955                 DEFW    GOTO_XY
7956                 DEFW    ERASE_TO_EOS
7957                 DEFW    ERASE_TO_EOL
7958                 DEFW    MOVE_HOME
7959                 DEFW    FORM_FEED
7960                 DEFW    MOVE_DOWN
7961                 DEFW    RETURN
7962                 DEFW    MOVE_LEFT
7963 :
7964 :
7965 : CODE FROM HERE TO END OF CONS_OUT WAS INSERTED AFTER THE
7966 : REV. 06 ROM WAS BURNED AND HAS BEEN COMMENTED OUT TO MAINTAIN
7967 : COMPATIBILITY WITH THAT ROM.
7968 :
7969 ;               END
7970 ;       SKIP
7971 ;;
7972 ;; RETURN_CURSOR  -  Will return the current cursor position
7973 ;;                   to the caller.  It is in absolute screen
7974 ;;                   coordinates (0 based).
7975 ;;
7976 ;;Input Parameters:
7977 ;; None
7978 ;;
7979 ;;Returns:
7980 ;; D - X coord of cursor, absolute based on upper left of screen not window
7981 ;; E - Y coord of cursor
7982 ;;
7983 ;               GLB     RETURN_CURSOR
7984 ;RETURN_CURSOR:
7985 ;               LD      DE,(CURSOR)
7986 ;               RET
7987 ;       SKIP
7988 ;;
7989 ;; RETURN_RELATIVE_CURSOR - Returns the current cursor position relative
7990 ;;                          to the window.  0,0 will be returned for the
7991 ;;                          upper left corner of the window.
7992 ;;
7993 ;;Input Parameters:
7994 ;; NONE
7995 ;;
7996 ;;Returns:
7997 ;; D - X cursor position relative to window
7998 ;; E - Y cursor position relative to window
7999 ;;
8000 ;; Destroys A
8001 ;;
8002 ;
8003 ;               GLB     RETURN_RELATIVE_CURSOR
8004 ;
8005 ;RETURN_RELATIVE_CURSOR:
8006 ;               LD      DE,(CURSOR)     ;get absolute position
8007 ;               LD      A,(X_MIN)
8008 ;               NEG
8009 ;               ADD     A,D
8010 ;               LD      D,A
8011 ;               LD      A,(Y_MIN)
8012 ;               NEG
8013 ;               ADD     A,E
8014 ;               LD      E,A
8015 ;               RET
8016 ;;
8017 ;;=====================================================================
8018 ;;=====================================================================
8019 ;;=====================================================================
8020 ;;

8025 Do_EOS_START MACRO                  ;Header Rev. 4
8026                 .GOTO   Ede_EOS_START
8027
8028 Project:        EOS, 83-101
8029

8031 ;&k1S                                      ;&k0S
8032 ;&k1S  EOS_START              DLS          ;&k0S
8033 ;&k1S                                      ;&k0S
8034 ;&k1S                                      ;&k0S
8035
8036             Rev History
8037             Rev.  Date        Name        Change
8038              7    24Oct1951   rfj         Changed value for REV_NUM to 06
8039              6    13Oct1045a  RPD         added init of REV_NUM
8040              5    10Oct1005a  RPD         took out memory bank switch logic for GOTO_WP
8041              4    25sept1000a RPD         reworked for new boot from tape logic
8042              3    24sept1110a RPD         moved clear ram logic
8043              2    23sept1255p RPD         moved FCB's and added calls A_uOS routines
8044              1    03SEP1445   DLS         MORE CODE
8045              0    02SEP0630   DLS         CODE

8047 Ede_EOS_START MEND
8048
8049 ***********************************************************************
8050 *                                                                     *
8051 *  MODULE NAME:                                                       *
8052 *                                                                     *
8053 *    EOS_START                                                        *
8054 *                                                                     *
8055 *  INPUTS:                                                            *
8056 *                                                                     *
8057 *    NONE                                                             *
8058 *                                                                     *
8059 *  FUNCTION(S):                                                       *
8060 *      0. SET STACK.                                                   *
8061 *      1. TO BANK SELECT OS7 AND 24K RAM.                              *
8062 *      2. CALL INIT_EOS TO ALLOW THE 1) THE Z80/6801 SYNCH_UP,         *
8063 *         POLL FOR NET DEVICES AND ESTABLISH DCB'S.                   *
8064 *      3. CHECK FOR THE PRESENCE OF A TAPE. IF PRESENT THEN            *
8065 *         LOAD IN A COLD_START LOADER AND EXECUTE FROM THERE.          *
8066 *      4. IF NO TAPE, THEN BRING UP THE ROM RESIDENT WORD              *
8067 *         PROCESSOR BY JUMPING TO OS7.                                  *
8068 *                                                                     *
8069 *  OUTPUTS:                                                           *
8070 *                                                                     *
8071 *    DEVICE ADDR _ REG B                                               *
8072 *                                                                     *
8073 *  CALLS:                                                             *
8074 *                                                                     *
8075 *    EOS_INIT                                                         *
8076 *    EOS_QUERY_DEVICE                                                 *
8077 *    EOS_READ_BLOCK                                                    *
8078 *                                                                     *
8079 *  CALLED BY:                                                         *
8080 *                                                                     *
8081 *    INVOKED BY EOS_BOOT                                               *
8082 *                                                                     *
8083 *  NOTES:                                                             *
8084 *                                                                     *
8085 *    NONE.                                                            *
8086 *                                                                     *
8087 ***********************************************************************
8088
8089
8090 :
8091 : external routines used
8092 :
8093                 ;EXT    _FIND_DCB
8094                 ;EXT    _REQUEST_STATUS
8095                 ;EXT    _HARD_INIT
8096                 ;EXT    _RD_1_BLOCK
8097                 ;EXT    _FMGR_INIT
8098                 ;EXT    _GOTO_WP
8099                 ;EXT    FILL_VRAM
8100                 ;EXT    TURN_OFF_SOUND
8101                 ;EXT    SWITCH_MEM
8102                 ;EXT    PORT_COLLECTION
8103 :
8104 : external data structures used
8105 :
8106                 ;EXT    REV_NUM
8107                 ;EXT    CLEAR_RAM_START
8108                 ;EXT    CLEAR_RAM_SIZE
8109                 ;EXT    EOS_STACK
8110                 ;EXT    DEFAULT_BT_DEV
8111                 ;EXT    MEM_CNFG01
8112                 ;EXT    MEM_CNFG03
8113 :
8114 : local equates
8115 :
8116 EOS_REV         EQU     005H            ;current EOS revision number
8117                                           ;--NOTE-- this is actually rev.
8118                                           ;06 but to match the production ROM
8119                                           ;which was labeled rev. 05 we fudge
8120                                           ;just a bit (two bits actually)
8121
8122 COLD_START_ADDR EQU     0C800H
8123 DSK4            EQU     4
8124 DSK5            EQU     5
8125 ONE_BLOCK       EQU     1
8126 BLOCK_ZERO      EQU     0
8127
8128
8129                 ;GLB    _EOS_START
8130 _EOS_START:
8131                 LD      SP,EOS_STACK             ; SET THE STACK
8132
8133                 LD      BC,CLEAR_RAM_SIZE        ; clear EOS ram
8134                 LD      DE,CLEAR_RAM_START+1
8135                 LD      HL,CLEAR_RAM_START
8136                 XOR     A
8137                 LD      (HL),A
8138                 LDIR
8139
8140                 LD      A,EOS_REV                ; init REV_NUM
8141                 LD      (REV_NUM),A
8142
8143                 CALL    PORT_COLLECTION          ; collect the port addresses from OS7
8144
8145                 CALL    TURN_OFF_SOUND           ; KILL SOUNDS
8146
8147                 LD      A,0                      ; CLEAR VRAM
8148                 LD      HL,0
8149                 LD      DE,16*1024
8150                 CALL    FILL_VRAM
8151
8152                 LD      A,(MEM_CNFG01)           ;get the data for configuration 1 (64K RAM)
8153                 CALL    SWITCH_MEM               ;select this configuration
8154 :
8155 : EOS_INIT WILL PERFORM THE 6801/Z80 SYNCH_UP, PERFORM A ROLL_CALL
8156 : POLL ON THE NET, AND ESTABLISH THE DCB'S
8157 :
8158                 CALL    _HARD_INIT
8159 :
8160 : setup the FCB's
8161
8162                 LD      DE,THREE1K_BLKS          ; START ADDRESS OF THREE 1K BLOCKS
8163                 LD      HL,FCB_S                 ; START ADDRESS OF 3 FCB BLOCKS
8164                 CALL    _FMGR_INIT               ; TELL THE FILE MANAGER
8165
8166 :
8167 :     CHECK FOR BOOT LOADER IN ANY MASS STORAGE DEVICE
8168 :
8169                 LD      A,TAPE_ID               ;set the default boot device to tape
8170                 LD      (DEFAULT_BT_DEV),A
8171
8172                 LD      A,DSK4                  ;check for DISK 4 device active
8173                 CALL    _REQUEST_STATUS
8174                 JR      NZ,CHK_D5
8175
8176                 LD      A,DSK4
8177                 CALL    _FIND_DCB
8178                 LD      A,(IY+D_STATUS_FLAGS)
8179                 AND     0FH
8180                 CP      3
8181                 LD      A,DSK4
8182                 JR      C,DEV1_OK
8183
8184 CHK_D5:         LD      A,DSK5                  ;check for DISK 5 device active
8185                 CALL    _REQUEST_STATUS
8186                 JR      NZ,CHK_TAPE
8187
8188                 LD      A,DSK5
8189                 CALL    _FIND_DCB
8190                 LD      A,(IY+D_STATUS_FLAGS)
8191                 AND     0FH
8192                 CP      3
8193                 LD      A,DSK5
8194                 JR      C,DEV1_OK
8195 CHK_TAPE:
8196                 LD      A,TAPE_ID                ;check if TAPE device active
8197                 CALL    _REQUEST_STATUS
8198                 JR      NZ,CALL_WP
8199
8200                 LD      A,TAPE_ID
8201                 CALL    _FIND_DCB
8202                 LD      A,(IY+D_STATUS_FLAGS)
8203                 PUSH    AF
8204                 AND     0FH
8205                 CP      3
8206                 JR      C,DEV0_OK
8207
8208                 POP     AF
8209                 SRL     A
8210                 SRL     A
8211                 SRL     A
8212                 SRL     A
8213                 CP      3
8214                 LD      A,TAPE_ID+10H
8215                 JR      C,DEV1_OK
8216
8217 CALL_WP:                                        ;devices disk 4, 5 and tape are not active
8218                 LD      A,(MEM_CNFG03)          ;get the data for configuration 3 (8K OS7 and 56K RAM)
8219                 CALL    SWITCH_MEM              ;select this configuration
8220                 JP      _GOTO_WP                ;default to ALPHA
8221 DEV0_OK:
8222                 POP     AF
8223                 LD      A,TAPE_ID
8224 DEV1_OK:
8225                 LD      (DEFAULT_BT_DEV),A
8226 READ_AGAIN:
8227                 LD      HL,COLD_START_ADDR
8228                 LD      BC,0
8229                 LD      A,(DEFAULT_BT_DEV)
8230                 LD      DE,BLOCK_ZERO
8231                 CALL    _RD_1_BLOCK
8232                 JP      Z,GO_TO_TAPECODE
8233                 LD      C,A
8234                 LD      A,(DEFAULT_BT_DEV)
8235                 AND     0FH
8236                 CP      TAPE_ID
8237                 JR      NZ,READ_AGAIN
8238                 LD      A,C
8239                 CP      TIMEOUT
8240                 JR      Z,READ_AGAIN
8241                 JP      CALL_WP
8242 GO_TO_TAPECODE:
8243
8244                 LD      A,(DEFAULT_BT_DEV)
8245                 LD      B,A
8246                 JP      COLD_START_ADDR
8247 :
8248 :=====================================================================
8249 :=====================================================================
8250 :=====================================================================
8251 :
8252
8254 ;
8255 ;       Rev History
8256 ;       Rev.  Date        Name        Change
8257 ;       3     5oct1135a   RPD         wp equate changed to 100H
8258 ;       2     2oct155p    RPD         added initialization of reserved byte
8259 ;       0     aug/sept    VB          original
8260 ;
8261
8262
8263 ; ENTRY:        __HARD_INIT
8264 ; NEEDS:        NOTHING
8265 ; RETURNS:      DEFAULT PCB ADDRESS SET UP FOR USE ((CURRENT_PCB) = PCB)
8266 ;               HARD RESET APPLIED TO NETWORK
8267 ;               NETWORK MASTER NODE IN SYNC WITH Z80
8268 ;               1 DCB ALLOTTED FOR EACH DEVICE ANSWERING A STATUS REQUEST
8269 ;               CORRECTLY
8270 ;               MASTER NODE SCANNING EACH OF THESE DCB'S FOR WORK TO DO
8271
8272         ;GLB    __HARD_INIT
8273
8274         ;EXT    CURRENT_PCB
8275         ;EXT    PCB
8276
8277 __HARD_INIT
8278         PUSH    BC                          ; SAVE BC
8279         PUSH    DE                          ;      DE
8280         PUSH    HL                          ;      HL
8281         PUSH    IY                          ;      IY
8282
8283         LD      HL,PCB                      ; INIT OUR IDEA OF CURRENT_PCB
8284         LD      (CURRENT_PCB),HL
8285
8286         CALL    __HARD_RESET_NET            ; HARD RESET NETWORK
8287
8288         CALL    __DLY_AFT_HRD_RES           ; GOOD DELAY AFTER HARD
8289                                             ; NET RESET
8290
8291         LD      HL,PCB                      ; ZERO OUT PCB/DCB'S
8292         LD      DE,PCB+1
8293         LD      BC,P_SIZE+(15*D_SIZE)-1
8294         LD      BC,P_SIZE+(15*D_SIZE)        ; this byte count will zero out PCB, DCB'S and the reserved byte
8295         LD      (HL),0
8296         LDIR
8297
8298 REPEAT_SYNC1:
8299         CALL    __SYNC
8300         JR      NZ,REPEAT_SYNC1
8301
8302         CALL    __SCAN_ACTIVE               ; FIND ALL THE ACTIVE DEVICES ON THE NET
8303
8304         POP     IY                          ; RESTORE IY
8305         POP     HL                          ;         HL
8306         POP     DE                          ;         DE
8307         POP     BC                          ;         BC
8308
8309         RET
8310
8312 ;
8313 ; ENTRY:        __SOFT_INIT
8314 ; NEEDS:        HL = PCB ADDRESS SET UP FOR USE
8315 ; RETURNS:      (CURRENT_PCB) = HL
8316 ;               HARD RESET APPLIED TO NETWORK
8317 ;               NETWORK MASTER NODE IN SYNC WITH Z80
8318 ;               1 DCB ALLOTTED FOR EACH DEVICE ANSWERING A STATUS REQUEST
8319 ;               CORRECTLY
8320 ;               MASTER NODE SCANNING EACH OF THESE DCB'S FOR WORK TO DO
8321
8322         ;GLB    __SOFT_INIT
8323
8324 __SOFT_INIT
8325         PUSH    BC                          ; SAVE BC
8326         PUSH    DE                          ;      DE
8327         PUSH    HL                          ;      HL
8328         PUSH    IY                          ;      IY
8329
8330         LD      (CURRENT_PCB),HL            ; ESTABLISH CURRENT_PCB ADDR
8331
8332         CALL    __HARD_RESET_NET            ; HARD RESET NETWORK
8333
8334         CALL    __DLY_AFT_HRD_RES
8335
8336         LD      HL,(CURRENT_PCB)            ; ZERO OUT PCB/DCB'S
8337         LD      E,L
8338         LD      D,H
8339         INC     DE
8340         LD      BC,P_SIZE+(15*D_SIZE)-1
8341         LD      (HL),0
8342         LDIR
8343
8344 REPEAT_SYNC2:
8345         CALL    __SYNC
8346         JR      NZ,REPEAT_SYNC2
8347
8348         CALL    __SCAN_ACTIVE               ; FIND ALL THE ACTIVE DEVICES ON THE NET
8349
8350         POP     IY                          ; RESTORE IY
8351         POP     HL                          ;         HL
8352         POP     DE                          ;         DE
8353         POP     BC                          ;         BC
8354
8355         RET
8356
8358
8359 ; ENTRY:        __HARD_RESET_NET
8360 ; NEEDS:        NOTHING
8361 ; RETURNS:      HARD RESET APPLIED TO NETWORK
8362
8363 NET_DELAY_COUNT EQU     0
8364
8365         ;GLB    __HARD_RESET_NET
8366
8367 __HARD_RESET_NET:
8368         ;EXT    NET_RESET_PORT
8369         LD      A,(NET_RESET_PORT)
8370         LD      C,A
8371         LD      A,0FH                       ; RESET NETWORK
8372         OUT     (C),A
8373
8374         LD      A,NET_DELAY_COUNT           ; DELAY (***** PROBABLY TOO LONG)
8375
8376 NET_RES_DELAY:
8377         NOP
8378         NOP
8379         NOP
8380         DEC     A
8381         JR      NZ,NET_RES_DELAY
8382
8383         XOR     A                           ; HELLLLLLLLOOOOOOO NETWORK .....
8384         OUT     (C),A
8385
8386         RET
8387
8389
8390         ;GLB    __DLY_AFT_HRD_RES
8391
8392 __DLY_AFT_HRD_RES:
8393         PUSH    BC
8394         PUSH    DE
8395
8396         LD      B,1
8397
8398 LOOP8:
8399         LD      DE,0001H
8400
8401 LOOP7:
8402         DEC     DE
8403         LD      A,D
8404         OR      E
8405         JR      NZ,LOOP7
8406
8407         DJNZ    LOOP8
8408
8409         POP     DE
8410         POP     BC
8411
8412         RET
8413
8415
8416 ; ENTRY:        __SYNC
8417 ; NEEDS:        (CURRENT_PCB) SET TO ADDRESS OF CURRENTLY OPERATING PCB
8418 ; RETURNS:      NETWORK MASTER NODE IN SYNC WITH Z80
8419 ;               NUMBER OF DCB'S = 0 (SO MASTER NODE IS NOT SCANNING
8420 ;               ANY DCB'S - SO A __SCAN_ACTIVE NEEDS TO BE DONE
8421 ;               TO ESTABLISH DCB'S)
8422
8423 SYNC_DELAY_DE EQU 0
8424 SYNC_DELAY_B  EQU 2
8425
8426         ;GLB    __SYNC
8427
8428 __SYNC
8429         PUSH    IY                          ; SAVE IY
8430         PUSH    HL                          ;      HL
8431         PUSH    BC
8432         PUSH    DE
8433
8434         LD      IY,(CURRENT_PCB)            ; CURRENT PCB
8435
8436         LD      (IY+P_NUM_DCBS),0           ; 0 DCB'S
8437
8438         LD      (IY+P_COM_STAT),PCB_SYNC1   ; START SYNC WITH FIRST
8439                                             ; BYTE
8440
8441         LD      DE,SYNC_DELAY_DE
8442         LD      B,SYNC_DELAY_B
8443
8444 LOOP1:
8445         DEC     DE
8446         LD      A,D
8447         OR      E
8448         JR      NZ,NOT_TIME1
8449
8450         LD      DE,SYNC_DELAY_DE
8451
8452         DJNZ    NOT_TIME1
8453
8454         LD      A,CANT_SYNC1
8455         OR      A                           ; ERROR
8456
8457         JR      END_SYNC
8458
8459 NOT_TIME1:
8460         LD      A,(IY+P_COM_STAT)           ; DID MASTER NODE ACKNOWLEDGE ?
8461         CP      PCB_SYNC1_ACK
8462         JR      NZ,LOOP1                    ; ***** NEED PROTECTION HERE
8463
8464         LD      (IY+P_COM_STAT),PCB_SYNC2   ; CONTINUE SYNC WITH
8465                                             ; SECOND BYTE
8466
8467         LD      DE,SYNC_DELAY_DE
8468         LD      B,SYNC_DELAY_B
8469
8470 LOOP2:
8471         DEC     DE
8472         LD      A,D
8473         OR      E
8474         JR      NZ,NOT_TIME2
8475
8476         LD      DE,SYNC_DELAY_DE
8477
8478         DJNZ    NOT_TIME2
8479
8480         LD      A,CANT_SYNC2
8481         OR      A                           ; ERROR
8482
8483         JR      END_SYNC
8484
8485 NOT_TIME2:
8486         LD      A,(IY+P_COM_STAT)           ; DID MASTER NODE ACKNOWLEDGE ?
8487         CP      PCB_SYNC2_ACK
8488         JR      NZ,LOOP2                    ; ***** NEED PROTECTION HERE
8489
8490         PUSH    IY                          ; GET PCB ADDR INTO HL
8491         POP     HL
8492
8493         LD      (IY+P_REL_ADDR_LO),L        ; IN CASE SOMEONE ACCIDENTALLY DOES
8494         LD      (IY+P_REL_ADDR_HI),H        ; A PCB RELOCATION COMMAND -
8495                                             ; IT WILL RELOCATE TO THIS SPOT
8496
8497         XOR     A                           ; NO ERROR
8498
8499 END_SYNC:
8500         POP     DE
8501         POP     BC
8502         POP     HL                          ; RESTORE HL
8503         POP     IY                          ;         IY
8504
8505         RET
8506
8508
8509 ; ENTRY:        __SCAN_ACTIVE
8510 ; NEEDS:        (CURRENT_PCB) SET TO CURRENTLY OPERATING PCB
8511 ; RETURNS:      1 DCB ALLOTTED FOR EACH DEVICE ANSWERING A STATUS REQUEST
8512 ;               CORRECTLY
8513 ;               MASTER NODE SCANNING EACH OF THESE DCB'S FOR WORK TO DO
8514
8515         ;GLB    __SCAN_ACTIVE
8516
8517 __SCAN_ACTIVE
8518         PUSH    BC                          ; SAVE BC
8519         PUSH    DE                          ;      DE
8520         PUSH    HL                          ;      HL
8521         PUSH    IY                          ;      IY
8522         PUSH    IX                          ;      IX
8523
8524         LD      HL,(CURRENT_PCB)            ; ZERO OUT DCB'S
8525         LD      DE,P_SIZE                   ; POINT TO DCB'S
8526         ADD     HL,DE
8527         LD      E,L                         ; WANT DE = (CURRENT_PCB)+P_SIZE+1
8528         LD      D,H
8529         INC     DE
8530         LD      BC,(15*D_SIZE)-1
8531         LD      (HL),0
8532         LDIR
8533
8534         LD      IY,(CURRENT_PCB)            ; FIND START ADDR OF DCB'S
8535         LD      DE,P_SIZE
8536         ADD     IY,DE
8537
8538         LD      IX,(CURRENT_PCB)            ; IX = (CURRENT_PCB)
8539         LD      (IX+P_NUM_DCBS),1           ; NEED AT LEAST 1 ACTIVE DCB
8540                                             ; TO START CHECKING
8541
8542         LD      A,1                         ; START WITH DEV ADDR 1
8543
8544 LOOP3:
8545         PUSH    AF                          ; SAVE CURRENT DEVICE ADDR
8546
8547         LD      (IY+D_DEV_ADDR),A           ; SELECT A NEW ADDRESS
8548
8549         LD      (IY+D_COM_STAT),DCB_STATUS  ; ISSUE A REQUEST FOR STATUS
8550
8551 LOOP4:
8552         BIT     CMND_COMPLETE_BIT,(IY+D_COM_STAT) ; IO DONE ?
8553         JR      Z,LOOP4                     ; ***** NEED PROTECTION HERE
8554
8555         LD      A,(IY+D_COM_STAT)           ; CHECK IF VALID RESPONSE
8556         CP      CMND_FIN_STATUS
8557         JR      Z,RECVD_VALID_STAT          ; YES INDEED ...
8558
8559         POP     AF                          ; TRY NEXT DEVICE ADDR
8560
8561         INC     A
8562         CP      MAX_DEV_ADDR+1              ; PAST LAST VALID ADDR ?
8563         JR      NZ,LOOP3                    ; NOPE - NEW ADDR
8564
8565         DEC     (IX+P_NUM_DCBS)            ; DON'T NEED LAST DCB WE USED
8566
8567         JR      END_S_A                     ; ALL DONE
8568
8569 RECVD_VALID_STAT:
8570         INC     (IX+P_NUM_DCBS)
8571
8572         LD      DE,D_SIZE                   ; ADVANCE TO NEXT DCB
8573         ADD     IY,DE
8574
8575         POP     AF                          ; ADVANCE TO NEXT NET ADDR
8576
8577         INC     A
8578         CP      MAX_DEV_ADDR+1              ; NO MORE ?
8579         JR      NZ,LOOP3                    ; MORE TO CHECK
8580
8581         DEC     (IX+P_NUM_DCBS)             ; DON'T NEED LAST DCB WE USED
8582
8583 END_S_A:
8584         POP     IX                          ; RESTORE IX
8585         POP     IY                          ;         IY
8586         POP     HL                          ;         HL
8587         POP     DE                          ;         DE
8588         POP     BC                          ;         BC
8589
8590         RET
8591
8593
8594 ; ENTRY:        __RELOC_PCB
8595 ; NEEDS:        (CURRENT_PCB) SET TO CURRENTLY OPERATING PCB
8596 ;               HL = ADDRESS OF WHERE PCB SHOULD BE RELOCATED TO
8597 ; RETURNS:      PCB RELOCATED AS REQUESTED
8598 ;               (CURRENT_PCB) UPDATED TO REFLECT NEW ADDRESS
8599
8600         ;GLB    __RELOC_PCB
8601
8602 __RELOC_PCB
8603         PUSH    IY
8604
8605         LD      IY,(CURRENT_PCB)            ; IY = ADDRESS OF CURRENT PCB
8606
8607         LD      (IY+P_REL_ADDR_LO),L        ; PLACE ADDR OF NEW PCB IN PLACE
8608         LD      (IY+P_REL_ADDR_HI),H
8609
8610         LD      (IY+P_COM_STAT),PCB_SNA    ; TELL MASTER TO SET NEW PCB ADDRESS
8611
8612 LOOP5:
8613         LD      A,(IY+P_COM_STAT)           ; CHECK FOR OPERATION COMPLETE
8614         CP      PCB_SNA_ACK                 ; ***** NEED PROTECTION HERE
8615         JR      NZ,LOOP5                    ; NOPE
8616
8617         LD      (CURRENT_PCB),HL            ; REMEMBER WHERE PCB IS (PLEASE...)
8618
8619         POP     IY
8620
8621         RET
8622
8624
8625 ; ENTRY:        __GET_PCB_ADDR
8626
8627         ;GLB    __GET_PCB_ADDR
8628
8629 __GET_PCB_ADDR:
8630         LD      IY,(CURRENT_PCB)            ; ADDRESS OF PCB
8631
8632         RET
8633
8635
8636         ;GLB    __SOFT_RES_KBD,__SOFT_RES_PR,__SOFT_RES_TAPE
8637
8638         ;EXT    __FIND_DCB,CHK_IF_INACTIVE
8639
8640 __SOFT_RES_KBD:
8641         LD      A,KEYBOARD_ID               ; RESET KBD
8642         JR      __SOFT_RES_DEV
8643
8644 __SOFT_RES_PR:
8645         LD      A,PRINTER_ID                ; RESET PRINTER
8646         JR      __SOFT_RES_DEV
8647
8648 __SOFT_RES_TAPE:
8649         LD      A,TAPE_ID                   ; RESET TAPE
8650         JR      __SOFT_RES_DEV
8651
8652         ;GLB    __SOFT_RES_DEV
8653
8654 __SOFT_RES_DEV:
8655         PUSH    IY                          ; SAVE IY
8656
8657         CALL    __FIND_DCB                  ; FIND APPROPRIATE DEVICE
8658         JR      NZ,NO_DCB_FOUND             ; BAD DEVICE I.D.
8659
8660         CALL    CHK_IF_INACTIVE             ; IS DEVICE IDLE?
8661         JR      NZ,ACTIVE_DCB               ; NOPE ... OOPS ...
8662
8663         LD      (IY+D_COM_STAT),DCB_RESET   ; SOFT RESET DEVICE
8664
8665 DCB_CMND_LOOP:
8666         BIT     CMND_COMPLETE_BIT,(IY+D_COM_STAT) ; WAS COMMAND COMPLETE?
8667         JR      Z,DCB_CMND_LOOP             ; NOPE, CHECK AGAIN
8668
8669         LD      A,(IY+D_COM_STAT)           ; YUP
8670         CP      CMND_FIN_STATUS
8671
8672 ACTIVE_DCB:
8673 NO_DCB_FOUND:
8674         POP     IY
8675
8676         RET
8677
8679
8680 ; READ DCB RETURN CODE (FIRST BYTE OF DCB)
8681
8682         ;GLB    __RD_KBD_RET_CODE,__RD_PR_RET_CODE,__RD_TAPE_RET_CODE
8683
8684 __RD_KBD_RET_CODE:
8685         LD      A,KEYBOARD_ID               ; RESET KBD
8686         JR      __RD_RET_CODE
8687
8688 __RD_PR_RET_CODE:
8689         LD      A,PRINTER_ID                ; RESET PRINTER
8690         JR      __RD_RET_CODE
8691
8692 __RD_TAPE_RET_CODE:
8693         LD      A,TAPE_ID                   ; RESET TAPE
8694         JR      __RD_RET_CODE
8695
8696         ;GLB    __RD_RET_CODE
8697
8698 __RD_RET_CODE:
8699         PUSH    IY                          ; SAVE IY
8700
8701         CALL    __FIND_DCB                  ; FIND APPROPRIATE DEVICE
8702         JR      NZ,NO_DCB_FOUND2            ; BAD DEVICE I.D.
8703                                             ; Z CONDITION CODE PRESENT
8704
8705
8706         LD      A,(IY+D_COM_STAT)           ; COMMAND/STATUS BYTE FROM DCB
8707
8708 NO_DCB_FOUND2:
8709         POP     IY
8710
8711         RET

8714 ;WP             EQU     02000H                  ; WHERE TO GO TO START WP

8718         ;EXT    SWITCH_MEM
8719 __GOTO_WP:
8720         LD      A,(MEM_CNFGOO)              ;get configuration byte for 32K ROM and 32 RAM
8721         CALL    SWITCH_MEM
8722

8733 ;

8736 ;

8747 ;       COMPATIBILITY WITH THAT ROM
8748 ;

8753 ;EOS_SIZE       EQU 02000H+800H

8755 ;

8757 ;       OUT     (7FH),A                     ; (I HOPE)
8758 ;

8760 ;       LD      DE,EOS_DEST                 ; WHERE EOS GOES

8762 ;       LDIR                                ; MOVE EOS INTO PLACE
8763 ;

8771 ;;===============================================================
8772 ;;
8774 ; REV 0                  8/24/83  V/D
8775 ;***************************************************************
8776 ;
8777 ;       __RD_1_BLOCK WILL READ ONE BLOCK OF DATA FROM A BLOCK DEVICE
8778 ;       TO A LOCATION SPECIFIED BY REGISTER HL
8779 ;
8780 ;       INPUT:  A         DEVICE ID
8781 ;                         LOW NIBBLE - DEVICE ADDRESS
8782 ;                         HI NIBBLE  - SECONDARY DEVICE ID
8783 ;
8784 ;               HL        DESTINATION ADDRESS IN RAM
8785 ;
8786 ;               REGISTER PAIR.
8787 ;                         BC DE       - SECTOR NUMBER ON DEVICE
8788 ;
8789 ;       THE TRANSFER BYTE COUNT IS DEFINED BY "DCB_MAX_MESS_LEN" WHICH
8790 ;       IS FOUND IN THE DEVICE DCB.
8791 ;
8792 ;       OUTPUT:
8793 ;               CONDITION BITS:
8794 ;                         Z: NO ERRORS
8795 ;                         NZ: ERRORS OCCURED
8796 ;                             A - ERROR CODE
8797 ;
8798 ;       ALL REGISTERS ARE PRESERVED
8799 ;
8800 ;***************************************************************
8801
8802         ;GLB    __RD_1_BLOCK
8803         ;EXT    __FIND_DCB
8804         ;EXT    CHK_IF_INACTIVE
8805
8806 __RD_1_BLOCK:
8807         PUSH    IY
8808
8809         PUSH    AF                          ; SAVE DEVICE ID
8810
8811         CALL    __START_RD_1_BLOCK
8812
8813         JR      NZ,ERROR_WITH_DCB
8814
8815 RD_NOT_COMPLETED:
8816         POP     AF                          ; GET DEVICE ID
8817         PUSH    AF                          ; SAVE IT AGAIN
8818
8819         CALL    __END_RD_1_BLOCK            ; LOOK FOR STATUS
8820
8821         JR      NC,RD_NOT_COMPLETED         ; COMMAND NOT COMPLETED, CHECK STATUS AGAIN
8822
8823 ERROR_WITH_DCB:
8824         POP     IY                          ; REALLY IS AF BUT NEED TO SAVE IF AN ERROR
8825
8826         POP     IY
8827
8828         RET
8830 ;***************************************************************
8831 ;
8832 ;       __WR_1_BLOCK WILL WRITE ONE BLOCK OF DATA TO A BLOCK DRIVER
8833 ;       FORM A LOCATION SPECIFIED BY REGISTER HL
8834 ;
8835 ;       INPUT:
8836 ;               A         DEVICE ID
8837 ;                         LOW NIBBLE - DEVICE ADDRESS
8838 ;                         HI NIBBLE  - SECONDARY DEVICE ID
8839 ;
8840 ;               HL        SOURCE ADDRESS IN RAM
8841 ;
8842 ;               REGISTER PAIR:
8843 ;                         BC DE   - SECTOR NUMBER ON DEVICE
8844 ;
8845 ;       THE TRANSFER BYTE COUNT IS DEFINED BY "DCB_MAX_MESS_LEN"
8846 ;       WHICH IS FOUND IN THE DEVICE DCB
8847 ;
8848 ;       OUTPUT:
8849 ;               CONDITION BITS:
8850 ;                         Z:  NO ERRORS
8851 ;                         NZ: ERRORS OCCURED
8852 ;                             A - ERROR CODE
8853 ;
8854 ;       ALL REGISTERS  ARE PRESERVED
8855 ;
8856 ;***************************************************************
8857
8858         ;GLB    __WR_1_BLOCK
8859
8860 __WR_1_BLOCK:
8861         PUSH    IY
8862
8863         PUSH    AF                          ; SAVE DEVICE ID
8864
8865         CALL    __START_WR_1_BLOCK
8866
8867         JR      NZ,ERROR_WITH_DCB2          ; DCB NOT FOUND OR NOT IDLE
8868
8869 WR_NOT_COMPLETED:
8870         POP     AF                          ; GET DEVICE ID
8871
8872         PUSH    AF
8873
8874         CALL    __END_WR_1_BLOCK
8875
8876         JR      NC,WR_NOT_COMPLETED         ; COMMAND NOT COMPLETED,CHECK STATUS AGAIN
8877
8878 ERROR_WITH_DCB2:
8879         POP     IY                          ; IS REALLY AF BUT NEED TO PRESERVE A REG IN CASE OF ERRORS
8880
8881         POP     IY
8882
8883         RET
8885 ;***************************************************************
8886 ;
8887 ;       __START_RD_1_BLOCK WILL SEND A READ COMMAND TO READ A BLOCK
8888 ;       OF DATA FROM A BLOCK DEVICE TO A BUFFER SPECIFIED BY REGISTER HL
8889 ;
8890 ;       INPUT:  A         DEVICE ID
8891 ;                         LOW NIBBLE  - DEVICE ADDRESS
8892 ;                         HI NIBBLE   - SECONDARY DEVICE ID
8893 ;
8894 ;               HL        DESTINATION ADDRESS IN RAM
8895 ;
8896 ;               REGISTER PAIR:
8897 ;                         BC DE       - SECTOR NUMBER ON DEVICE
8898 ;
8899 ;       THE TRANSFER BYTE COUNT IS DEFINED BY "DCB_MAX_MESS_LEN" WHICH
8900 ;       IS FOUND IN THE DEVICE DCB.
8901 ;
8902 ;       OUTPUT:
8903 ;               CONDITION BITS:
8904 ;                         Z:  NO ERRORS
8905 ;                         NZ: ERRORS OCCURED
8906 ;                             A - ERROR CODE
8907 ;
8908 ;       ALL REGISTERS ARE PRESERVED
8909 ;
8910 ;***************************************************************
8911
8912 ;
8913 ;NO_DCB_FOUND1 CHANGED TO BLK_NO_CB_FOUND1 TO AVOID CONFLICT
8914 ;WITH IDENTICAL LABEL ELSEWHERE
8915         ;GLB    __START_RD_1_BLOCK
8916
8917 __START_RD_1_BLOCK:
8918         PUSH    BC
8919         PUSH    DE
8920         PUSH    HL
8921         PUSH    IY
8922
8923         CALL    __FIND_DCB
8924
8925         JR      NZ,B_NO_DCB_FOUND1          ; COULD NOT FIND DCB FOR THIS DEVICE
8926
8927         CALL    CHK_IF_INACTIVE             ; IS DEVICE IDLE?
8928         JR      NZ,ACTIVE_DCB1              ; NOPE, GET OUT
8929
8930         CALL    PREP_DCB                    ; SET UP THE DCB FOR READ
8931
8932         LD      (IY+D_COM_STAT),DCB_RD      ; SEND READ COMMAND
8933
8934 ACTIVE_DCB1:
8935 B_NO_DCB_FOUND1:
8936         POP     IY
8937         POP     HL
8938         POP     DE
8939         POP     BC
8940
8941         RET
8942
8944 ;***************************************************************
8945 ;
8946 *       __END_RD_1_BLOCK CHECKS THE STATUS AFTER A __START_RD_1_BLOCK
8947 *       IS EXECUTED
8948 *
8949 *       INPUT:  A       DEVICE ID
8950 *                       LOW NIBBLE - DEVICE ADDRESS
8951 *                       HI NIBBLE  - SECONDARY DEVICE ID
8952 *
8953 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
8954 *               C:  OPERATION COMPLETED
8955 *               NC: OPERATION NOT COMPLETED
8956 *               Z:  NO ERRORS
8957 *               NZ: ERRORS
8958 *                   A = ERROR CODE
8959 *
8960 *       ALL REGISTERS ARE SAVED
8961 *
8962 ;***************************************************************
8963
8964         ;GLB    __END_RD_1_BLOCK
8965         ;EXT    CHK_IF_IDLE
8966
8967 __END_RD_1_BLOCK:
8968         PUSH    IY
8969
8970         CALL    __FIND_DCB
8971
8972         SCF                                 ; SET CARRY FLAG TO INDICATE OPERATION COMPLETED
8973
8974         JR      NZ,NO_DCB_FOUND5           ; NO DCB FOUND
8975
8976         CALL    CHK_IF_IDLE                ; IS DEVICE IDLE?
8977
8978         JR      NZ,DCB_IS_IDLE5
8979
8980         OR      A                           ; CLEAR CARRY FLAG
8981         BIT     CMND_COMPLETE_BIT,(IY+D_COM_STAT) ; WAS COMMAND COMPLETE?
8982         JR      Z,COMMAND_NOT_COMPLETED     ; NOPE,
8983
8984
8985         LD      A,(IY+D_COM_STAT)           ; GET STATUS OF LAST OPERATION
8986
8987         CP      CMND_FIN_STATUS             ; ANY ERRORS?
8988         SCF                                 ; INDICATE COMMAND COMPLETED
8989                                             ; CONDITION FLAGS: IF NO ERRORS:  C,Z
8990                                             ;                  IF ERRORS:     C,NZ  A=ERROR CODE
8991
8992 DCB_IS_IDLE5:
8993 NO_DCB_FOUND5:
8994 COMMAND_COMPLETED:
8995 COMMAND_NOT_COMPLETED:
8996         POP     IY
8997
8998         RET
8999
9001 ;***************************************************************
9002 ;
9003 ;       __START_WR_1_BLOCK WILL SEND A WRITE COMMAND TO WRITE A BLOCK
9004 ;       OF DATA TO A BLOCK DEVICE FROM A LOCATION SPECIFIED BY REGISTER HL
9005 ;
9006 ;       INPUT:
9007 ;               A         DEVICE ID
9008 ;                         LOW NIBBLE  - DEVICE ADDRESS
9009 ;                         HI NIBBLE   - SECONDARY DEVICE ID
9010 ;
9011 ;               HL        SOURCE ADDRESS IN RAM
9012 ;
9013 ;               REGISTER PAIR:
9014 ;                         BC DE   - SECTOR NUMBER ON DEVICE
9015 ;
9016 ;
9017 ;       THE TRANSFER BYTE COUNT IS DEFINED BY "DCB_MAX_MESS_LEN"
9018 ;       WHICH IS FOUND IN THE DEVICE DCB
9019 ;
9020 ;       OUTPUT:
9021 ;               CONDITION BITS:
9022 ;                         Z:  NO ERRORS
9023 ;                         NZ: ERRORS OCCURED
9024 ;                             A - ERROR CODE
9025 ;
9026 ;       ALL REGISTERS ARE PRESERVED
9027 ;
9028 ;***************************************************************
9029
9030 ;NO_DCB_FOUND3 CHANGED TO B_NO_DCB_FOUND3 TO AVOID CONFLICT WITH
9031 ;IDENTICAL LABEL ELSEWHERE
9032
9033         ;GLB    __START_WR_1_BLOCK
9034
9035 __START_WR_1_BLOCK:
9036         PUSH    BC
9037         PUSH    DE
9038         PUSH    HL
9039         PUSH    IY
9040
9041         CALL    __FIND_DCB
9042
9043         JR      NZ,B_NO_DCB_FOUND3          ; NO DCB FOUND
9044
9045         CALL    CHK_IF_INACTIVE             ; IS DEVICE ACTIVE?
9046         JR      NZ,ACTIVE_DCB3              ; NOPE, GET OUT
9047
9048         CALL    PREP_DCB                    ; SET UP DCB FOR WRITE
9049
9050         LD      (IY+D_COM_STAT),DCB_WR      ; SEND WRITE COMMAND
9051
9052 B_NO_DCB_FOUND3:
9053 ACTIVE_DCB3:
9054         POP     IY
9055         POP     HL
9056         POP     DE
9057         POP     BC
9058
9059         RET
9061
9062 ;***************************************************************
9063 ;
9064 ;       __END_WR_1_BLOCK WILL CHECK THE STATUS OF THE LAST OPERATION
9065 ;       EXECUTED
9066 ;
9067 ;       INPUT:
9068 ;               A         DEVICE ID
9069 ;                         LOW NIBBLE  - DEVICE ADDRESS
9070 ;                         HI NIBBLE   - SECONDARY DEVICE ID
9071 ;
9072 ;       OUTPUT:
9073 ;               CONDITION FLAGS
9074 ;                         C        OPERATION COMPLETED
9075 ;                         NC       OPERATION NOT COMPLETED
9076 ;                         Z        NO ERRORS
9077 ;                         NZ       ERRORS
9078 ;                                  A - ERROR CODE
9079 ;
9080 ;       ALL REGISTERS ARE PRESERVED
9081 ;
9082 ;***************************************************************
9083
9084 ;NO_DCB_FOUND4              B_NO_DCB_FOUND4
9085 ;DCB_IS_IDLE4 CHANGED TO BLK_DCB_IS_IDLE4 TO AVOID CONFLICT WITH
9086 ;IDENTICAL LABEL ELSEWHERE
9087
9088         ;GLB    __END_WR_1_BLOCK
9089
9090 __END_WR_1_BLOCK:
9091         PUSH    IY
9092
9093         CALL    __FIND_DCB
9094
9095         SCF
9096         JR      NZ,B_NO_DCB_FOUND4          ; CONDITION FLAGS: C,NZ  A=ERROR CODE
9097
9098         CALL    CHK_IF_IDLE                 ; IS DEVICE IDLE?
9099         JR      NZ,BLK_DCB_IS_IDLE4         ; YUP
9100
9101         OR      A                           ; RESET CARRY FLAG
9102
9103         BIT     CMND_COMPLETE_BIT,(IY+D_COM_STAT) ; WAS COMMAND COMPLETE?
9104         JR      Z,CMND_NOT_COMPLETED        ; NOPE,
9105
9106         LD      A,(IY+D_COM_STAT)           ; YUP
9107
9108
9109         CP      CMND_FIN_STATUS             ; ANY ERRORS?
9110         SCF                                 ; INDICATE COMMAND COMPLETED
9111                                             ; CONDITION FLAGS: IF NO ERRORS:  C,Z
9112                                             ;                  IF ERRORS:     C,NZ, A=ERROR CODE
9113
9114
9115 BLK_DCB_IS_IDLE4:
9116 B_NO_DCB_FOUND4:
9117 CMND_COMPLETED:
9118 CMND_NOT_COMPLETED:
9119         POP     IY
9120         RET
9122 ;***************************************************************
9123 *
9124 *       PREP_DCB LOADS THE DCB BUFFER WITH THE PROPER VALUES FOR
9125 *       A READ OR WRITE COMMAND
9126 *
9127 *       INPUT:
9128 *               IY      START OF SPECIFIC DCB
9129 *               HL      ADDRESS OF RAM DESTINATION(READ)/SOURCE(WRITE)
9130 *
9131 *               BC DE   DEVICE SECTOR NUMBER
9132 *
9133 *       OUTPUT: NONE
9134 *
9135 ;***************************************************************
9136
9137 PREP_DCB:
9138         PUSH    AF
9139
9140         SRL     A                           ; EXTRACT SECONDARY DEVICE ID
9141         SRL     A
9142         SRL     A
9143         SRL     A
9144
9145         LD      (IY+D_SEC_DEV_ID),A         ; .... AND PLACE IN DCB
9146         LD      A,(IY+D_MAX_MSG_LN_LO)      ; GET BLOCK LENGTH AND PLACE ....
9147         LD      (IY+D_BUF_LEN_LO),A         ; .... IT IN BUFFER LENGTH ....
9148         LD      A,(IY+D_MAX_MSG_LN_HI)      ; .... FOR READ
9149         LD      (IY+D_BUF_LEN_HI),A
9150
9151         LD      (IY+D_SECT_NUM),E           ; LOAD UP A 4 BYTE SECTOR....
9152         LD      (IY+D_SECT_NUM+1),D         ; .... NUMBER FROM REGISTERS....
9153         LD      (IY+D_SECT_NUM+2),C         ; .... PAIR BC DE
9154         LD      (IY+D_SECT_NUM+3),B
9155
9156         LD      (IY+D_BUF_ADR_LO),L         ; ADDRESS OF SOURCE/DESTINATION
9157         LD      (IY+D_BUF_ADR_HI),H         ; .... IN RAM
9158
9159         POP     AF
9160         RET
9161 ;
9162 ;===============================================================
9163 ;===============================================================
9164 ;===============================================================
9165 ;
9166
9168
9169 ;REV 0                  (V/D  8-24-83)
9170
9171         ;EXT    DEVICE_ID
9172
9173 ;***************************************************************
9174 * THIS ROUTINE WILL INITIATE A READ COMMAND ON A CHARACTER DEVICE.  THE
9175 * ROUTINE WILL WAIT FOR THE COMMAND TO BE COMPLETED.  IF THE COMMAND
9176 * COMPLETED WITH NO ERRORS, THEN THE FLAG WILL BE ZERO.  IF THERE
9177 * WERE ERRORS THEN THE FLAG WILL BE NON-ZERO.
9178 *       INPUT:  A ==>   DEVICE ID
9179 *               DE =>   DESTINATION ADDRESS
9180 *               BC =>   NUMBER OF BYTES TO READ
9181 *       OUTPUT:
9182 *               CONDITION FLAGS
9183 *                         Z :     NO ERROR
9184 *                         A ==>   KEY READ
9185 *                         NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
9186 *                         A ===>  ERROR CODE
9187 ;***************************************************************
9188
9189         GLOBAL  __RD_CH_DEV
9190         ;EXT    __FIND_DCB
9191
9192 __RD_CH_DEV:
9193         LD      (DEVICE_ID),A               ; SAVE DEVICE ID
9194
9195         CALL    __START_RD_CH_DEV
9196         JR      NZ,GOOD_BYE
9197
9198 RD_DEV_LOOP:
9199         LD      A,(DEVICE_ID)               ; RESTOR THE DEVICE ID
9200         CALL    __END_RD_CH_DEV
9201         JR      NC,RD_DEV_LOOP
9202
9203 GOOD_BYE:
9204         RET
9205
9207 ;***************************************************************
9208 * WRITE CHAR DEV ALLOWS THE CALLER TO SEND A PRINT COMMAND BY SPECIFYING
9209 * THE BUFFER OF THE STRING AND THE NUMBER OF BYTES TO PRINT.
9210 *       INPUT:  A ==>   DEVICE ID
9211 *               BC =>   NUMBER OF BYTES TO WRITE
9212 *               HL =>   SOURCE OF DATA
9213 *       OUTPUT:
9214 *               CONDITION FLAGS
9215 *                         Z :     NO ERRORS
9216 *                         NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
9217 *                         A ===>  ERROR CODE
9218 ;***************************************************************
9219
9220         GLOBAL  __WR_CH_DEV
9221
9222 __WR_CH_DEV:
9223         LD      (DEVICE_ID),A
9224
9225         CALL    __START_WR_CH_DEV
9226         JR      NZ,SO_LONG
9227
9228 DCB_WRITE_LOOP:
9229         LD      A,(DEVICE_ID)               ; RESTORE THE ID FOR USE IN THE CALL
9230         CALL    __END_WR_CH_DEV
9231         JR      NC,DCB_WRITE_LOOP
9232
9233 SO_LONG:
9234         RET
9235
9237
9238 ;***************************************************************
9239 * THIS ROUTINE IS RESPONSIBLE FOR SETTING UP A DEVICE DCB TO DO A READ.
9240 * THE LAST THING DONE IN THIS ROUTINE IS TO ISSUE THE COMMAND TO READ
9241 * A KEY FROM THE KEYBOARD.
9242 *       INPUT:  A  ===> CONTAINS THE DEVICE ID
9243 *               DE ===> DESTINATION ADDRESS
9244 *               BC ===> NUMBER OF BYTES TO READ
9245 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
9246 *               Z:      THE READ DATA FROM KEYBOARD COMMAND HAS BEEN SENT
9247 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
9248 *                       A ===>  ERROR CODE
9249 ;***************************************************************
9250
9251         ;GLB    __START_RD_CH_DEV
9252         ;EXT    CHK_IF_INACTIVE
9253
9254 __START_RD_CH_DEV:
9255         PUSH    IY
9256         CALL    __FIND_DCB                  ; FIND THE DCB
9257
9258         JR      NZ,NO_DCB_DEFINED
9259
9260         CALL    CHK_IF_INACTIVE             ; SEE IF THE KEY BOARD IS AVAILABLE
9261
9262         JR      NZ,DCB_IS_ACTIVE            ; DCB IS CURRENTLY ACTIVE
9263
9264         LD      (IY+D_BUF_ADR_LO),E         ; LOAD THE DESTINATION ADDRESS INTO DCB
9265         LD      (IY+D_BUF_ADR_HI),D
9266
9267         LD      (IY+D_BUF_LEN_LO),C         ; LOAD THE BYTE COUNT
9268         LD      (IY+D_BUF_LEN_HI),B
9269
9270         LD      (IY+D_COM_STAT),DCB_RD      ; INITIATE THE COMMAND TO READ
9271                                             ; ZERO FLAG WAS SET ABOVE
9272
9273 DCB_IS_ACTIVE:                              ; NZ IS ALREADY THE CONDITION OF THE FLAG
9274 NO_DCB_DEFINED:                             ; A CONTAINS THE ERROR CODE
9275         POP     IY
9276
9277         RET
9278
9280 ;***************************************************************
9281 * THIS ROUTINE WILL CHECK THE DEVICES DCB COM/STATUS BYTE AND RETURN
9282 * THE RESULT OF THE CHECK.
9283 *       INPUT:  A ===> DEVICE ID
9284 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
9285 *               C       COMMAND HAS FINISHED
9286 *               NC      COMMAND HAS NOT BEEN PROCESSED
9287 *               Z :     NO ERROR OCCURED
9288 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
9289 *                       A ===>  ERROR CODE
9290
9291 ;***************************************************************
9292
9293         ;GLB    __END_RD_CH_DEV
9294         ;EXT    CHK_IF_IDLE
9295
9296 __END_RD_CH_DEV:
9297         PUSH    IY
9298         CALL    __FIND_DCB
9299         SCF
9300
9301         JR      NZ,NO_DCB                   ; THERE WAS NO KEYBOARD DCB
9302
9303         CALL    CHK_IF_IDLE                 ; IS DEVICE IDL?
9304         JR      NZ,DCB_IS_IDL               ; YUP
9305
9306         OR      A                           ; CLEAR THE CARRY FLAG
9307         BIT     CMND_COMPLETE_BIT,(IY+D_COM_STAT) ; WAS THE COMMAND PROCESSED?
9308         JR      Z,ROUTINE_FINISHED          ; NO, RETURN WITH THE CARRY FLAG CLEARED
9309                                             ; YES, THE COMMAND WAS PROCESSED
9310         LD      A,(IY+D_COM_STAT)           ; DID ANY ERRORS OCCUR?
9311         CP      CMND_FIN_STATUS
9312         SCF
9313                                             ; NO ERROR, SET THE CARRY FLAG, ZERO FLAG CONDITION
9314 DCB_IS_IDL:
9315 NO_DCB:
9316 ROUTINE_FINISHED:
9317         POP     IY
9318
9319         RET
9320
9322 ;***************************************************************
9323 * __START_WR_CH_DEV ALLOWS THE CALLER TO SEND A PRINT COMMAND BY SPECIFYING
9324 * THE BUFFER OF THE STRING AND THE NUMBER OF BYTES TO PRINT.
9325 *       INPUT:  A ==>   DEVICE ID
9326 *               BC =>   NUMBER OF BYTES TO WRITE
9327 *               HL =>   SOURCE OF DATA
9328 *       OUTPUT:
9329 *               CONDITION FLAGS
9330 *                         Z :     NO ERRORS
9331 *                         NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
9332 *                         A ===>  ERROR CODE
9333 ;***************************************************************
9334
9335 ;NO_DCB_FND3 CHANGED TO C_NO_DCB_FND3 TO AVOID CONFLICT WITH
9336 ;IDENTICAL LABEL ELSEWHERE
9337
9338         ;GLOBAL __START_WR_CH_DEV
9339
9340 __START_WR_CH_DEV:
9341         PUSH    IY
9342         CALL    __FIND_DCB                  ; FIND THE CORRECT DEVICE DCB
9343
9344         JR      NZ,C_NO_DCB_FND3
9345
9346         CALL    CHK_IF_INACTIVE             ; IS DCB ACTIVE?
9347         JR      NZ,DCB_IS_ACTIVE3           ; YUP
9348
9349         LD      (IY+D_BUF_LEN_LO),C         ; LENGTH OF THE BUFFER
9350         LD      (IY+D_BUF_LEN_HI),B
9351
9352         LD      (IY+D_BUF_ADR_LO),L         ; ADDRESS OF THE STRING
9353         LD      (IY+D_BUF_ADR_HI),H
9354
9355         LD      (IY+D_COM_STAT),DCB_WR      ; INITIATE THE WRITE DATA COMMAND
9356
9357 C_NO_DCB_FND3:
9358 DCB_IS_ACTIVE3:
9359         POP     IY
9360         RET
9362 ;***************************************************************
9363 * __END_WR_CH_DEV WILL CHECK THE DEVICES DCB COM/STATUS BYTE AND RETURN
9364 * THE RESULT OF THE CHECK.
9365 *       INPUT:  A ===> DEVICE ID
9366 *       OUTPUT: CONDITION FLAGS
9367 *               C :     COMMAND HAS FINISHED
9368 *               NC:     COMMAND HAS NOT BEEN PROCESSED
9369 *               Z :     NO ERROR OCCURED
9370 *               NZ:     ERROR OCCURED
9371 *                       A ===>  ERROR CODE
9372
9373 ;***************************************************************
9374
9375 ;NO_DCB_FND4              C_NO_DCB_FND4
9376 ;DCB_IS_IDL4 CHANGED TO CHAR_DCB_IS_IDL4 TO AVOID CONFLICT WITH
9377 ;IDENTICAL LABEL ELSEWHERE
9378
9379         ;GLB    __END_WR_CH_DEV
9380
9381 __END_WR_CH_DEV:
9382         PUSH    IY
9383
9384         CALL    __FIND_DCB
9385         SCF
9386
9387         JR      NZ,C_NO_DCB_FND4            ; THERE WAS NO PRINTER DCB
9388
9389         CALL    CHK_IF_IDLE                 ; IS DEVICE IDL?
9390         JR      NZ,CHAR_DCB_IS_IDL4
9391
9392         OR      A                           ; CLEAR THE CARRY FLAG
9393         BIT     CMND_COMPLETE_BIT,(IY+D_COM_STAT) ; WAS THE COMMAND PROCESSED?
9394         JR      Z,NOT_COMPLETED             ; NO, RETURN WITH THE CARRY FLAG CLEARED
9395                                             ; YES, THE COMMAND WAS PROCESSED
9396         LD      A,(IY+D_COM_STAT)           ; DID ANY ERRORS OCCUR?
9397         CP      CMND_FIN_STATUS
9398         SCF
9399                                             ; NO ERROR, SET THE CARRY FLAG, ZERO FLAG CONDITION
9400 CHAR_DCB_IS_IDL4:
9401 C_NO_DCB_FND4:
9402 NOT_COMPLETED:
9403         POP     IY
9404         RET
